### [1] [$Z$-sight: Unifying Vision-Language-Action and Latent World Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24940)
*Junjie Ye,Yiqi Zhao,Muchen Xu,Celina Shiyu Wang,Hao Dong,Ruohai Ge,Rong Xue,Benjamin J Kolligs,Aditya Dutt,Sasanka Nagavalli,Nassim A. Samad,Gaurav S. Sukhatme,Jyotirmoy V. Deshmukh,Yue Wang,Yuliang Guo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Z-sight是一个统一机器人基础模型，将VLA与生成式潜在世界模型结合，直接在VLM视觉token空间预测未来，无需像素重建或外部编码器，在SIMPLE和Unitree G1任务上显著超越基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频世界动作模型联合生成未来与动作有价值，但视频生成器代价高；潜在空间策略虽避免像素生成，却常对齐中间特征或依赖冻结目标编码器，使未来预测与VLM原生特征空间解耦。

Method: 采用混合Transformer结构，将前瞻专家和连续动作专家与VLM耦合，在VLM感知当前的同一视觉token空间中联合去噪未来空间token与动作，推理时无需像素重建或外部编码器，并在人类视频和机器人轨迹上训练。

Result: 在SIMPLE上达到81.9%成功率（比最强基线高9.6点），在六个Unitree G1任务上平均73.3%（高20.0点），每块耗时110毫秒，在桌面基准上保持竞争力。

Conclusion: Z-sight验证了在VLM原生视觉token空间进行联合未来与动作建模的可行性，以较低推理成本取得显著性能提升，为统一机器人基础模型提供了有效路径。

Abstract: We introduce $Z$-sight, a unified robot foundation model that couples vision-language-action (VLA) and generative latent world modeling. Video-based world action models (WAMs) show the value of joint future-and-action generation for control, yet inherit the heavy cost of video generators. Recent latent-space policies avoid pixel generation, yet typically align intermediate features or rely on frozen target encoders, decoupling future prediction from the VLM's native feature space. $Z$-sight instead predicts the future directly in the visual token space in which its VLM perceives the present. Coupling a foresight expert and a continuous action expert with the VLM in a mixture-of-transformers, $Z$-sight jointly denoises future spatial tokens and actions at inference without pixel reconstruction or external encoders. Trained on human video and robot trajectories, $Z$-sight achieves 81.9% success on SIMPLE (+9.6 points over the strongest baseline) and 73.3% across six Unitree G1 tasks (+20.0 points) at 110 ms per chunk, while remaining competitive on tabletop benchmarks.

</details>


### [2] [Learning to Interact in Multi-Agent Self-Play for Controllable Autonomous Driving Scenario Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24876)
*Beyazit Yalcinkaya,Ethan Pronovost,Maximilian Naumann,Marcell Vazquez-Chanlatte*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种扩展的多智能体自博弈框架，引入智能体相对动态目标，以可控地生成复杂交互驾驶场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有目标条件自博弈框架主要依赖静态地图位置目标，无法可靠生成如激进尾随、对抗性切入等复杂智能体交互，而这些交互对自动驾驶严格测试至关重要。

Method: 扩展多智能体自博弈框架，引入智能体相对动态目标，并以该扩展目标表示对策略进行条件化，实现有针对性的可控场景合成。

Result: 该方法成功生成静态目标基线无法模拟的交互式驾驶行为，为自动驾驶测试中的长尾场景生成提供了灵活工具。

Conclusion: 智能体相对动态目标条件化能够实现可控、有针对性的复杂交互场景合成，弥补静态目标自博弈在安全关键边缘场景生成上的不足。

Abstract: Large-scale, multi-agent self-play trains robust driving policies effectively in simulation, but controlling these models to generate specific, safety-critical edge cases remains a key challenge. Existing goal-conditioned self-play frameworks rely primarily on static map-location goals and fail to reliably produce complex agent interactions such as aggressive tailgating or adversarial cut-offs. These agent interactions are necessary for rigorous autonomous vehicle (AV) testing. To address this, we present an extended multi-agent self-play framework that introduces agent-relative dynamic goals. By conditioning policies on this extended goal representation, our framework enables targeted, controllable scenario synthesis. We demonstrate that our approach successfully generates interactive driving behaviors that static-goal baselines cannot simulate, providing a flexible tool for long-tail scenario generation in AV testing.

</details>


### [3] [Phoenix: Learning Scene Structure Representations for Generalizable World-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24938)
*Yupeng Zheng,Chaoyue Li,Linbo Wang,Xiang Li,Yuhang Zheng,Xiaotong Su,Songen Gu,Yichen Zhang,Zebin Xing,Shuai Tian,Jingxiang Guo,Jing Li,Zhiyong Zhao,Zhongpu Xia,Qichao Zhang,Haoran Li,Shuicheng Yan,Dongbin Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Phoenix通过双教师蒸馏构建几何与时空语义表征，结合因果聚合的CS-VAE生成潜变量，联合预测场景演化与机器人动作，在多种操作基准上取得SOTA并展现强鲁棒性与少样本适应能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界-动作模型中的重建导向视觉表征未显式强调操作所需的空间关系与物体状态变化，限制了操作任务的表现。

Method: 提出Phoenix：Phoenix Encoder通过几何与时空语义的双教师蒸馏增强DINOv3的通用视觉语义；CS-VAE因果聚合压缩特征为生成潜变量并保留场景信息与空间布局；联合预测该潜变量、互补外观潜变量与机器人动作；并整理约39,000小时第一人称与机器人操作数据进行表征学习与WAM预训练。

Result: 经基准特定后训练，Phoenix在单臂、双臂与灵巧操作的仿真及真机基准上达到SOTA；LIBERO-Plus成功率94.8%，RoboTwin 2.0 C2R为72.4%，并展现强扰动鲁棒性、跨本体迁移及Bench2Dex与RoboDojo-RealWorld真机性能；预训练规模分析显示数据扩展收益优于仅视频VAE基线；少样本下游演示下仍保持高成功率。

Conclusion: 显式学习场景结构表征可有效提升世界-动作建模的数据利用效率、扩展性与操作泛化能力，Phoenix为操作任务提供了可复现的强基线。

Abstract: World-action models jointly predict future observations and actions, yet reconstruction-oriented visual representations do not explicitly emphasize the spatial relationships and object-state changes needed for manipulation. We introduce Phoenix, which learns representations of scene structure for world-action modeling. Specifically, Phoenix Encoder enriches DINOv3's general visual semantics through dual-teacher distillation of geometric and spatiotemporal semantic knowledge. Next, CS-VAE causally aggregates and compresses Phoenix Encoder features into generative latents while preserving scene information and spatial layout. Finally, Phoenix jointly predicts these latents, complementary appearance latents, and robot actions, linking scene evolution with action generation. To study pretraining data efficiency, we curate approximately 39,000 hours of egocentric and robot manipulation data for representation learning and use the robot demonstration subset for WAM pretraining. Following benchmark-specific post-training, Phoenix achieves state-of-the-art performance across simulation and real-robot benchmarks spanning single-arm, bimanual, and dexterous manipulation. It demonstrates strong robustness to perturbations, achieving success rates of 94.8% on LIBERO-Plus and 72.4% on RoboTwin 2.0 C2R, alongside strong cross-embodiment transfer and real-world manipulation performance on Bench2Dex and RoboDojo-RealWorld, respectively. Pretraining-scale analysis shows larger performance gains from data scaling than a video-VAE-only baseline, indicating stronger scaling benefits and more effective data utilization. Moreover, Phoenix maintains high success rates when adapted with limited downstream demonstrations, demonstrating strong few-shot adaptation. Our code, pretraining data, and models are available on the project page to help the community reproduce our results.

</details>


### [4] [Grana: Compositional Reward Modeling for Long-Horizon Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24926)
*Zi-ang Cao,Kaiyuan Zheng,Yinzhen Xu,Haonan Duan,Jiasheng Gu,Fengyuan Hu,Nan Zhu,Kun Lei,Kevin Lin,Ruijie Zheng,Shenyuan Gao,Fangqi Zhu,Zhe Zhang,Radha Poovendran,Linxi Fan,Guanzhi Wang,Yuke Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Grana是一个视觉-语言机器人奖励模型，通过开放词汇子任务描述识别未完成子任务并估计连续进度，无需人工数值进度标注。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人奖励建模面临跨任务泛化和长时程操作进度跟踪两大挑战：通用奖励模型从短任务预训练迁移到长时程任务尚不充分，长时程进度模型依赖任务特定子任务分类器或固定动作词汇，限制进度知识的复用。

Method: 训练语言条件模型估计短时程任务及长时程任务各子任务的进度；长时程任务中将所选子任务预测进度与已完成子任务的信用相结合以估计整体进度；提出标注协议，将稀疏子任务边界和动作质量标注转换为稠密进度目标，避免让标注者给出数值进度分数；在200个多样且多为短时程的操作任务上预训练。

Result: 实验表明：(i) 具备可泛化的奖励预测能力，包括适配五个长时程任务；(ii) 预训练与子任务进度估计组合带来收益；(iii) 下游策略性能得到提升。

Conclusion: 基于开放词汇子任务描述和稠密进度监督的预训练，可实现零样本奖励预测并提升长时程任务适配后的进度预测，最终改善下游策略表现。

Abstract: Robot reward modeling faces two challenges: generalizing across tasks and accurately tracking progress in long-horizon manipulation. General-purpose reward models demonstrate broad task transfer, but transfer from short-task pretraining to long-horizon tasks remains underexplored. Long-horizon progress models often rely on task-specific subtask classifiers or fixed action vocabularies, limiting the reuse of learned progress knowledge when adapting to new tasks. To address these challenges, we introduce Grana, a vision-language robot reward model that learns to identify unfinished subtasks and estimate their continuous progress using open-vocabulary subtask descriptions. We train a language-conditioned model to estimate progress in short-horizon tasks and individual subtasks of long-horizon tasks. For long-horizon tasks, we combine the selected subtask's predicted progress with credit for preceding completed subtasks to estimate whole-task progress. General progress prediction requires training the model across diverse tasks with progress supervision that accounts for imperfect execution. Elapsed-time proxies can misrepresent progress when relative subtask durations vary, or failures reverse local progress. We therefore introduce an annotation protocol that converts sparse subtask boundaries and action-quality annotations into dense progress targets, without requiring annotators to assign numerical progress scores. Pretraining with this supervision on 200 diverse, mostly short-horizon manipulation tasks enables zero-shot reward prediction on unseen tasks and improves progress prediction after adaptation to multi-minute, long-horizon tasks. Experiments demonstrate (i) generalizable reward prediction, including adaptation to five long-horizon tasks, (ii) benefits from pretraining and composing subtask progress estimates, and (iii) improved downstream policy performance.

</details>


### [5] [Render the Robot, Generate the World: One Video World Model Across Embodiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24925)
*Yash Jangir,Karan Mirakhor,Renos Zabounidis,Muhammad Zubair Irshad,Sergey Zakharov,Yonatan Bisk,Homanga Bharadhwaj*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Doppel，一种与具身无关的视频世界模型，以相机对齐的未来身体渲染和动作轨迹为条件，统一学习物体运动与环境交互变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频世界模型大多仅从数值动作学习身体运动和环境响应，但几何与运动学已明确指明意图运动，导致模型需同时学习本已给定的信息，效率低且泛化差。

Method: Doppel以相机对齐的未来身体配置渲染和数值动作轨迹为条件；通过恢复无外参机器人数据集的相机位姿并对齐渲染轨迹与观测；在8个机器人/人类具身、约600小时视频上联合训练共享视频骨干与条件适配器，无需具身标签。

Result: 在三个机械臂数据集上，Doppel相比仅动作基线在留出轨迹上PSNR提升1.00 dB，分布外评估提升1.20 dB，并预测更一致的物体运动；八具身模型Doppel-8在六个评估集中的四个上优于三机器人模型Doppel-3。

Conclusion: 显式提供意图运动使模型聚焦于物体运动与交互变化，渲染运动提供跨具身与动作空间的通用视觉接口，多具身联合训练可进一步提升机器人预测性能。

Abstract: Predicting the consequences of an agent's actions requires modeling both the body's motion and the environment's response. Most video world models attempt to learn both from numerical actions, even though geometry and kinematics already specify the intended motion. We introduce Doppel, an embodiment-agnostic video world model conditioned on camera-aligned renderings of future body configurations and numerical action trajectories. By explicitly providing the intended motion, Doppel can focus on predicting object motion and interaction-driven changes in the environment. Rendered motion also provides a common visual interface across different embodiments and action spaces. To train on robot datasets without camera extrinsics, we recover camera poses and align rendered trajectories with recorded observations. We jointly train a single shared video backbone and conditioning adapter on eight datasets spanning eight robot and human embodiments, from single- and bimanual robot arms to a dexterous humanoid and human hands and bodies, using about 600 hours of video without embodiment labels. Across three robot-arm datasets, Doppel improves PSNR over action-only baselines by 1.00 dB on held-out trajectories and 1.20 dB under out-of-distribution evaluation, while predicting more consistent object motion. Training on all eight embodiments (Doppel-8) improves robot predictions over the three-robot model (Doppel-3) on four of six evaluation sets. Project page: this https URL .

</details>


### [6] [Teleoperated Haircutting with Delay Compensation and Passivity-Based Control for Unilateral Internet Teleoperation under Real Communication Uncertainties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24883)
*Viridiana Luna-Zempoalteca,Zhendai Huang,Rosalba Galvan-Guerra,Antonio Rosales,Xiaoli Liu,Shuai Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于机器人理发单边互联网遥操作的框架，基于实测通信不确定性评估了ZOH、FOH、LPF三种延迟补偿技术与PD控制器及基于无源性的同步控制器的组合效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人理发需要精确运动执行与可靠通信以安全复现操作员指令，而互联网遥操作中的传输延迟变化和数据包抖动会破坏主从系统同步并影响跟踪性能，且现有研究多基于合成延迟模型而缺乏真实通信数据验证。

Method: 构建单边互联网遥操作框架，采用真实云平台采集的通信数据（含数据包延迟和有效延迟特征），将ZOH、FOH、LPF三种延迟补偿技术分别与经典PD控制器和基于无源性的同步控制器组合进行实验评估。

Result: 结果表明，将延迟补偿技术与基于无源性的同步控制相结合，可在真实通信不确定性下提升单边互联网遥操作的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该框架为机器人理发应用提供了一种可靠的控制方案，验证了基于实测通信数据评估延迟补偿与同步控制策略的有效性。

Abstract: Robotic haircutting requires accurate motion execution and reliable communication to safely reproduce the operator's commands. Internet-based teleoperation is a key enabling technology to remotely perform precise manipulation tasks such as haircutting. However, communication uncertainties, including variable transmission delays and packet jitter, degrade synchronization between the master and slave systems and affect tracking performance. This paper presents a unilateral Internet teleoperation framework for robotic haircutting that takes into account experimentally measured communication uncertainties. Three delay-compensation techniques, namely Zero-Order Hold (ZOH), First-Order Hold (FOH), and Low-Pass Filtering (LPF), are evaluated together with a classical proportional-derivative (PD) controller and a passivity-based synchronization controller. Unlike conventional studies based on synthetic delay models, the proposed framework is validated using communication data acquired from a real cloud-based teleoperation platform, where both packet delay and effective delay are characterized. The results demonstrate that combining delay-compensation techniques with passivity-based synchronization improves the robustness of unilateral Internet teleoperation under realistic communication uncertainties, providing a reliable control framework for robotic haircutting applications.

</details>


### [7] [Communications-free Collective Intelligence Via Allocentric Belief](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24858)
*Cameron Mehlman,Arjun Mulchandani,Hovik Grigoryan,Anurag Koyyada,Gregory Falco*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出利用共同外部见证者（witness）编码团队状态，通过异中心信念网络实现无通信的分散式协调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自主系统依赖显式通信或复杂状态估计，在关键时刻易失效且导致分散智能体信念分歧与协调崩溃；团队协作中人类可通过共同观察与解读线索实现无信息交换协调。

Method: 训练一个异中心（allocentric）信念网络，让每个智能体基于共同见证者的反应独立重建完整团队状态，从而在无智能体间交换条件下保持群体信念一致。

Result: 在五个竞争环境中，异中心信念对齐在集体表现上优于自我中心方法，并可扩展到标准方法失效的六十智能体团队。

Conclusion: 在缺乏通信时，共同注视可替代共享信道，为集体智能确立新原则。

Abstract: A team of skilled card players share no information amongst each other, and yet still are able to coordinate their play by watching the same players and reading the same tells. Autonomous systems have failed to exploit this logic, relying instead on explicit communication or complex state estimation schemes that can fail when it matters most. Without it, decentralized agents diverge in their world beliefs, and coordination collapses. Here we show that anchoring all agents to a single shared external actor, a common witness whose reactions encode team state, produces spontaneous, communication-free coordination. An allocentric belief network trained on witness behavior allows each agent to independently reconstruct full team state, keeping group beliefs consistent with no inter-agent exchange. Across five competitive environments allocentric belief alignment outperforms egocentric approaches in collective performance, and even scales to sixty-agent teams where standard methods fail. These results establish a new principle for collective intelligence: in the absence of communication, a shared gaze replaces a shared channel.

</details>


### [8] [DiWALI: Diffusion Policy Learning in World Models via Latent Action Inference from Human and Robot Play](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24836)
*Akshay L Chandra,Iman Nematollahi,Lingyue Zhao,Daria Kulikova,Angela P. Schoellig,Fabien Despinoy,Abhinav Valada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DiWALI框架通过在世界模型中学习扩散策略，利用无动作标签的人类演示数据，减少对机器人动作标注数据的依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人操作学习受限于带有动作标签的机器人数据稀缺，而人类演示虽然容易获取但缺乏机器人动作信息。

Method: 使用逆动力学模型从连续帧中推断潜在动作，实现无动作标签的世界模型训练；通过单独在机器人play数据上训练的解码器将潜在动作映射到机器人命令；基于无动作人类演示的潜在动作训练技能策略，并在想象中通过强化学习改进。

Result: 在CALVIN基准上，仅用人类演示训练的DiWALI策略显著优于用机器人演示训练的基线；将人类play纳入世界模型训练后微调的策略优于同等量机器人play；物理机器人实验表明主要从人类演示学到的技能在想象中进一步改进。

Conclusion: 人类经验可同时支持操作技能获取和后续策略改进，降低对动作标注机器人数据的依赖。

Abstract: Learning robot manipulation is constrained by the availability of action-labeled robot data, for both imitation and world models. Humans can demonstrate skills in seconds, but their demonstrations lack the required robot actions. We introduce DiWALI, a framework that learns and improves diffusion policies within a world model trained on human and robot play. DiWALI infers latent actions from consecutive frames using an inverse dynamics model, enabling world model training without action labels, and grounds them in robot commands through a decoder trained separately on robot play. Skill policies are trained over latent actions from action-free human demonstrations and improved with reinforcement learning in imagination. On the CALVIN benchmark, DiWALI policies trained solely from human demonstrations substantially outperform baselines trained with robot demonstrations. Incorporating human play into the world model training also yields stronger policies after fine-tuning than using the same amount of robot play alone. Experiments on a physical robot show that skills learned primarily from human demonstrations are further improved in imagination with a world model trained on human and robot play. Together, these results demonstrate that human experience can support both manipulation skill acquisition and subsequent policy improvement, reducing reliance on action-labeled robot data. Videos, dataset and code are available at this https URL .

</details>


### [9] [RoboMorpher: Scaling Robot Morphology Representations for Cross-Embodiment Action Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24816)
*Wenwei Lin,Yan Qin,Weiliang Deng,Zixuan Li,Kaixuan Wang,Zanxin Chen,Yue Chen,Tianshuo Yang,Junting Chen,Zhixuan Liang,Yao Mu,Ximin Lyu,Renjing Xu,Ping Luo,Tianxing Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboMorpher是一种形态条件动作分词器，联合重建关节角与连杆位姿，将控制坐标与运动学效应关联；配合MorphGen生成可扩展运动学监督，预训练后集成到VLA策略，在RoboTwin 2.0和EmbodiBench上显著优于连续关节空间控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相同关节值在不同机器人上产生不同空间构型，仅从关节值学习的共享动作表示受限，跨形态操作需要将控制坐标与其运动学效应关联的表示。

Method: 提出RoboMorpher形态条件动作分词器，联合重建关节角与连杆位姿；MorphGen基于调研几何先验生成串联臂描述以提供可扩展运动学监督；在4000万形态-状态对上预训练，冻结后集成到视觉-语言-动作策略进行离散预测与连续细化；并构建EmbodiBench（每任务30个生成URDF、共10任务）。

Result: 下游URDF不参与分词器预训练时，策略在RoboTwin 2.0达56.40%成功率、EmbodiBench达23.27%，较连续关节空间控制分别高9.73和15.34个百分点；移除连杆位姿监督使EmbodiBench成功率下降17.94个百分点。

Conclusion: 形态感知的几何监督对关节动作表示学习具有支持作用，联合重建位姿可有效提升跨形态操作策略性能。

Abstract: Learning shared action representations is central to cross-embodiment manipulation. However, identical joint values can produce different spatial configurations across robots, limiting representations learned from joint values alone. We introduce RoboMorpher, a morphology-conditioned action tokenizer that jointly reconstructs joint angles and link poses to associate control coordinates with their kinematic effects. MorphGen supplies scalable kinematic supervision by generating serial-arm descriptions from survey-derived geometric priors. We pretrain RoboMorpher on 40 million morphology-state pairs and integrate the frozen tokenizer into a vision-language-action policy for discrete prediction and continuous refinement. We also introduce EmbodiBench, a ten-task benchmark with 30 generated URDFs per task. With downstream URDFs excluded from tokenizer pretraining, the policy achieves 56.40% success on RoboTwin 2.0 and 23.27% on EmbodiBench, exceeding continuous joint-space control by 9.73 and 15.34 percentage points. Removing link-pose supervision reduces EmbodiBench success by 17.94 percentage points, supporting morphology-aware geometric supervision for joint-action representation learning.

</details>


### [10] [Planner-Controller Interaction for Ackermann Navigation: A Matched Simulation and Hardware Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24800)
*Moaiz Saeed,Marah Saleh,Mounis Shawgi,Mohamed Al Musleh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 在1/10比例Ackermann转向QCar与匹配Gazebo仿真中对比三种几何控制器与两种全局规划器的组合，发现控制器排序在仿真与硬件间一致（Spearman 0.77-0.94），但规划器相关效应一致性弱，仿真无法准确预测硬件上规划器依赖的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全局规划器与路径跟踪控制器通常被独立评估，但二者交互决定车辆实际路径跟踪效果；仿真能否准确预测特定规划器-控制器组合在Ackermann转向硬件上的表现尚不确定。

Method: 在1/10比例Quanser QCar和匹配的Gazebo仿真中，将三种几何控制器（Regulated Pure Pursuit、Stanley、Vector Pursuit）与两种全局规划器（Hybrid A*、NavFn）配对；两环境共享自定义规划与控制算法、匹配的路径几何与目标、真值位姿参考及统一评估流程；每种配置每环境运行5次，共60次基准测试。

Result: 控制器在跟踪精度与指令平滑性上的排序在仿真与硬件间一致（Spearman相关0.77-0.94）；但规划器相关效应一致性明显较弱：仿真中规划器选择对指令速度RMS角加速度的影响不超过0.030 rad/s^2，而硬件上Hybrid A*使其增加0.141-0.843 rad/s^2（取决于控制器）；差异与规划器相关的实际速度有关，仿真的理想化纵向响应未能复现；Jetson TX2上控制器平均计算时间低于0.5 ms。

Conclusion: 控制器排序一致并不能保证仿真准确预测特定控制器组合在硬件上规划器依赖的性能，物理验证十分重要。

Abstract: Global planners and path-tracking controllers are often evaluated independently, although their interaction determines how a vehicle follows a planned path. Whether simulation accurately predicts the performance of specific planner-controller combinations on Ackermann-steered hardware remains uncertain. This study evaluated three geometric controllers (Regulated Pure Pursuit, Stanley, and Vector Pursuit) paired with two global planners (Hybrid A* and NavFn) on a 1/10-scale Quanser QCar and in a matched Gazebo simulation. Both environments used shared custom planning and control algorithms, matched route geometry and goals, ground-truth pose references, and a common evaluation pipeline. Five runs per configuration and environment yielded 60 benchmark runs. Controller rankings for tracking accuracy and command smoothness transferred consistently between environments, with Spearman correlations of 0.77-0.94. However, planner-dependent effects showed substantially weaker agreement. In simulation, planner selection changed the root-mean-square (RMS) angular acceleration of the commanded velocities by no more than 0.030 rad/s^2, whereas Hybrid A* increased it by 0.141-0.843 rad/s^2 on hardware, depending on the controller. These differences coincided with planner-dependent achieved speeds that the simulation's idealised longitudinal response did not reproduce. Mean controller computation times remained below 0.5 ms on the Jetson TX2. The findings demonstrate that agreement in controller rankings does not guarantee accurate prediction of planner-dependent performance for specific controller pairings on hardware, highlighting the importance of physical validation.

</details>


### [11] [HINT-WAM: Hindsight-Informed Training for World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24789)
*Phuong Tuan Dat,Qi Li,Xinchao Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HINT-WAM 提出首个在线自蒸馏框架，利用演示的未来视频作为后见信息，在策略自身的采样轨迹上监督流匹配动作专家，无需环境奖励或模拟器交互。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有后训练方法难以可靠执行任务：离线监督微调只在含噪演示动作上训练动作专家，且任务适配可能损害预训练能力；在线强化学习依赖稀疏奖励或稠密奖励塑形，前者探索与信用分配困难，后者仅提供标量评价而非直接动作目标。

Method: 对每个演示观测，学生通过自身去噪轨迹生成动作；在轨迹状态上，同一模型额外以未来视频（后见）为条件充当教师，给出条件动作分布作为稠密训练目标，学生仅见部署输入。为迁移至流匹配动作专家，采用能量分数蒸馏，以分布级目标匹配教师动作分布而非回归其速度场。

Result: 摘要未给出具体定量结果，作者声称HINT-WAM是首个利用后见信息在策略自身采样轨迹上监督动作专家的在线自蒸馏框架，且无需环境奖励或模拟器交互。

Conclusion: HINT-WAM通过后见视频引导的在线自蒸馏与能量分数匹配，为世界动作模型提供稠密动作级监督，在避免奖励设计和模拟器依赖的同时保持预训练能力。

Abstract: World action models (WAMs) have shown promising generalization and data efficiency in robot manipulation by coupling action generation with predictions of future visual dynamics. Translating these capabilities into reliable task execution, however, remains a challenge for existing post-training methods. Offline supervised fine-tuning provides dense supervision on expert trajectories, but its flow-matching action expert is trained only on noised demonstration actions and never on the intermediate states produced by its own sampler; task-specific adaptation can also compromise pretrained capabilities. On-policy reinforcement learning trains on the policy's own behavior, yet sparse task rewards make exploration and credit assignment difficult, while dense reward shaping still provides scalar evaluations rather than direct action targets. We introduce \textbf{HINT-WAM}, the first on-policy self-distillation framework that supervises the action expert on its own sampling trajectories using hindsight, the future video of the demonstration, as privileged information available to the WAM itself. For each demonstration observation, the student generates its action through its own denoising trajectory, and at the states along this trajectory the same model acts as a teacher conditioned additionally on this hindsight, providing a conditional action distribution as a dense training target, while the student sees only deployment-time inputs and needs no environmental rewards or simulator interaction. To transfer this supervision to a flow-matching action expert, HINT-WAM distills the teacher into the student with an energy score, a distribution-level objective that matches the teacher's action distribution from the student's own samples rather than regressing its velocity field. The project web is available in this https URL .

</details>


### [12] [Hierarchical Admittance-Impedance Control for Mobile Manipulator Load Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24771)
*Minghao Xu,Thushara Sandakalum,Christina Dao Wen Lee,Marcelo H. Ang Jr*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种分层导纳-阻抗控制器，结合内层笛卡尔阻抗控制、外层6D导纳监督器及工作空间感知的移动底座辅助，用于引导式负载搬运。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对机场行李搬运等辅助任务，机器人需支撑负载并允许人类通过微小物理力引导其运动。

Method: 基于导纳与阻抗的交互角色解释，Franka Research 3机械臂在机器人物体界面表现为笛卡尔阻抗以提供柔顺负载支撑；外层导纳监督器将测得的人类力旋量输入转换为平滑的笛卡尔参考运动；工作空间感知辅助层在需要较大运动时在机械臂与速度控制底座间分配导纳生成的平移运动，并通过前侧负载对齐使物体靠近机器人前部区域。

Result: 系统在ROS中实现，并通过手提箱搬运实验验证，机器人能支撑负载并允许人类以较小 effort 引导搬运。

Conclusion: 该分层导纳-阻抗控制器结合移动底座辅助，可有效实现人机协作的引导式负载搬运。

Abstract: This paper presents a hierarchical admittance-impedance controller that combines an inner Cartesian impedance controller, an outer 6D admittance supervisor, and workspace-aware mobile-base assistance for guided load transfer. The method is motivated by assistive tasks such as airport baggage handling, where a robot should support a payload while a human worker guides its motion through small physical forces. Following an interaction-role interpretation of admittance and impedance, the torque-controlled Franka Research 3 manipulator behaves as a Cartesian impedance at the robot-object interface, providing compliant load support around a commanded equilibrium pose. At the human-facing interaction level, the outer admittance supervisor converts measured human wrench input into smooth Cartesian reference motion. The workspace-aware assistance layer shares the admittance-generated translational motion between the manipulator and a velocity-controlled base when larger motion is required, while front-side load alignment keeps the carried object near the robot's frontal region for safer and more intuitive guidance. The system was implemented in ROS and validated through a suitcase transfer experiment, showing that the robot can support the load while allowing the human to guide the transfer with relatively little effort.

</details>


### [13] [Conceptual full-vehicle modeling of a modular all-wheel-drive robotic ADS test platform as a foundation for a digital twin architecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24646)
*Felix Florian Schwald,Martin Meiners,Michael Viehof,Erik Hultman,Edwin Kamau*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 在IPG CarMaker中开发PixKit模块化电动测试平台的初始全车仿真模型，作为未来数字孪生的底层基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PixKit平台采用滑板底盘、四轮毂电机和后轮大转角转向等非常规特性，且缺乏完整实测数据，需建立可扩展的物理仿真模型。

Method: 基于厂商文档、推导值和工程假设进行参数化，侧重子系统系统级集成；通过不同转向输入和配置的基础驾驶场景评估模型。

Result: 仿真行为稳定且物理合理；全驱纵向行驶车轮响应对称；后轮反相转向增大横摆响应并减小转弯直径；圆周行驶滚动和滑移有界。

Conclusion: 该CarMaker模型为PixKit提供了可追溯且功能可用的仿真基础，支持后续模型细化、实车数据标定及经验证的数字孪生架构开发。

Abstract: This paper presents the development of an initial full-vehicle simulation model of the PixKit modular electric test platform in IPG CarMaker. The objective is to create a physics-based and extendable model structure that can serve as a low-level foundation for future digital twin applications. The reference vehicle is a skateboard chassis combining unconventional vehicle characteristics such as four independent wheel-hub motors and rear axle steering capable of high steering angles. Since complete measurement data of the vehicle is not yet available, the model parameterization is based on a combination of manufacturer documentation, derived values and engineering assumptions. The modeling approach focuses on the system-level integration of the main vehicle subsystems rather than on a fully validated representation. The resulting model was assessed through basic driving scenarios with different steering inputs and configurations. The simulations showed stable model behavior and physically plausible interaction between the subsystems. The all-wheel-drive configuration produced symmetric wheel responses in longitudinal driving, while counter-phase rear axle steering increased the yaw response and reduced the simulated turning circle diameter compared with front axle steering only. The circular driving scenario further indicated bounded roll and slip behavior. Overall, the implemented CarMaker model provides a traceable and functional simulation basis for the PixKit vehicle. It can support future model refinement, calibration with real vehicle data and development with the goal of creating a validated digital twin architecture.

</details>


### [14] [Hydrodynamics of robotic micro-swimmers in confinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24631)
*Giovanni Corsi,Giovanni Noselli,Antonio DeSimone*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 受Purcell启发，通过数值模拟与厘米级原型实验研究受限环境中自主游泳机器人的性能预测与优化，并外推至毫米及亚毫米尺度以评估微机器人在狭窄血管中航行的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验自主微机器人在人体狭窄腔道内航行的设想在流体力学上是否可行，并为不同尺度下的合理设计提供指导。

Method: 显式数值求解游泳者诱导的流场，并用由载货头部和旋转推进尾部构成的厘米级原型进行实验验证；利用流体力学标度律预测缩小至毫米及亚毫米尺度复制原型的性能。

Result: 流体力学似乎并未对该设想构成根本性障碍，但给出了严格且有用的设计准则，这些准则随长度尺度变化。

Conclusion: 自主微机器人在狭窄腔道内航行在流体力学上可行，但需依据不同尺度的流体力学标度规律进行合理设计。

Abstract: Inspired by seminal work by E.M. Purcell, we discuss the problem of predicting and optimizing the performance of autonomous robots swimming in confinement. Our approach is based on the explicit numerical resolution of the fluid flow induced by the swimmer, and it is validated experimentally on a cm-size prototype composed of a cargo head and a rotating propulsive tail. Using hydrodynamic scaling, we predict the performance of replicas of our prototype scaled down to mm- and sub-mm-size to test the feasibility of autonomous micro-robots navigating in narrow vessels. The main result of our theoretical, computational, and experimental analysis is that, while hydrodynamics seems to place no fundamental obstruction to the vision of autonomous micro-robots navigating in narrow lumens inside the human body, it does provide useful and strict guidelines for rational design at different length scales.

</details>


### [15] [SHAPE: Discrete Supervision and Parallel Continuous Streams for Learning Dexterous Manipulation from Egocentric Human Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24585)
*Yaping Li,Zhihong Liu,Jiacheng Li,Yi Yang,Wenchao Yue,Mingzhe Ye,Xiangcheng Liu,Yiyang Chen,Haotian He,Qi Zhang,Haonan Wang,Dahua Lin,Zhijie Deng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SHAPE 是一个视觉-语言-动作模型，用离散动作 token 作为辅助监督从人类视频中学习，并通过共享流匹配专家并行输出末端执行器（EEF）与关节两路可执行动作流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类自我中心录制数据比机器人示教便宜，但人类与机器人在形态和运动学上不同，人类数据只提供重定向后的 EEF 动作，缺少监督机器人策略所需的关节位置和执行器指令；因此需要解决人类数据如何监督策略、以及部署时灵巧机器人如何读出动作两个问题。

Method: 提出 SHAPE（Supervision from Human Action tokens with Parallel Executable streams）：骨干网络以 EEF 动作块的离散动作 token 预测为辅助任务，共享的流匹配专家输出 EEF 和关节两条可执行动作流；在 22 自由度双手双臂机器人上做匹配对照研究。

Result: 离散监督有助于从人类数据学习，驱动骨干梯度并形成跨域的动作信息表征；标准控制管线下，直接关节读出在机器人示教细粒度控制上表现更好（六任务平均 0.80，对比 π0.5 的 0.63），而基于 IK 的 EEF 读出在人类到机器人迁移上更优；用人类数据和离散 token 预训练后，SHAPE 无需任务特定机器人数据即可从人类示教学习新任务。

Conclusion: 离散动作 token 监督结合并行 EEF/关节输出流，使 VLA 模型能有效利用廉价人类数据预训练，并根据部署场景选择合适的动作读出方式，实现无需任务特定机器人数据的技能学习。

Abstract: Egocentric human recordings are far cheaper than dexterous robot demonstrations, promising stronger pretraining and direct skill learning without task-specific robot data. Yet humans and robots differ in morphology and kinematics, so human recordings lack the joint positions and actuator commands that supervise robot policies, providing only retargeted end-effector (EEF) actions. This raises two questions: how human data should supervise the policy, and how a dexterous robot should read out actions at deployment. We address both in SHAPE (Supervision from Human Action tokens with Parallel Executable streams), a vision-language-action (VLA) model whose backbone predicts discrete action tokens of EEF chunks as an auxiliary task, while a shared flow-matching expert outputs two executable streams, EEF and joint. On a bimanual robot with 22-DoF hands, a matched study shows that discrete supervision helps learn from human data, driving backbone gradients and shaping action-informative representations across domains. Under standard control pipelines at deployment, direct joint readout excels at fine-grained control with robot demonstrations, scoring 0.80 across six tasks against 0.63 for {\pi}0.5, whereas IK-based EEF readout excels at human-to-robot transfer. Pretrained with human data and discrete tokens, SHAPE learns new tasks from human demonstrations without task-specific robot data.

</details>


### [16] [TinyARM-X: A Series of Open-Source, Configurable, and Transparent Torque-Controlled Desktop-Scale Robotic Manipulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24554)
*Nicolas Kosanovic,Jean Chagas Vaz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出TinyARM-X框架，使低成本（<350美元）串联机械臂具备可重构、扭矩可控和易制造的特性，并开源整套软硬件栈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有低成本机械臂多采用刚性位置控制舵机，难以进行仿真、操作和安全控制，缺少可扭矩控制且易配置的低成本方案。

Method: 采用统一硬件/软件架构，支持<7自由度TinyARM在10分钟内手动重构为>3自由度机器人；通过执行器建模和动态补偿实现扭矩控制，并支持混合现实阻抗控制遥操作与仿真器力反馈渲染。

Result: TinyARM-7负载33.5 g、最大速度0.77 m/s、工作范围210 mm；动态补偿下反驱启动力1.4 N、运行力0.7 N；笛卡尔阻抗控制平移刚度>15 N/m；完成机械基准测试和真实力感知遥操作实验。

Conclusion: TinyARM-X以低成本实现可重构、扭矩可控的桌面级机械臂操作，并通过开源推动社区可及性。

Abstract: Visuomotor policies have demonstrated that imprecise low-cost robots can autonomously complete complex manipulation tasks. However, most inexpensive manipulators rely on compact and rigidly position-controlled servos for actuation, which complicate simulation, operation, and safety. This work introduces TinyARM-X, a framework for easily manufacturable, configurable, and torque-controllable low-cost (<350 USD) serial manipulators. By using a single hardware/software architecture, a <7 Degree of Freedom (DoF) TinyARM can be manually reconfigured to an operational >3 DoF robot within 10 min. Actuator modeling and dynamic compensation enable torque-controlled applications like mixed-reality impedance control teleoperation and simulator force feedback rendering. Experiments include mechanical benchmarking and real-world force-aware telemanipulation. The TinyARM-7 has a payload of 33.5 g, a max speed of 0.77 m/s, and a reach of 210 mm. Moreover, with dynamic compensation, the robot has a backdrive-breakaway force of 1.4 N and a backdrive-running force of 0.7 N. Additionally, Cartesian-Impedance Control (CIC) attains a translational stiffness of >15 N/m. Lastly, the TinyARM-X stack is open-sourced to disseminate accessible desktop-scale torque-controlled robotic manipulation to the community.

</details>


### [17] [OmniFish: A Bioinspired, Modular, Reconfigurable Soft Robotic Fish Enabling Multi-Modal Swimming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24511)
*Mathias Jensen,Saravana Prashanth Murali Babu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OmniFish是一种仿生模块化软体机器鱼，通过腱驱动顺应性分段脊柱与渐进式机械堵塞实现刚度重构，可在单一平台上复现五种BCF游泳模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有软体机器鱼通常只能实现单一或少数游泳模式，缺乏在单一平台上覆盖完整BCF运动谱系的通用性，因此需要一种结构简单且刚度可重构的架构来生成多种仿生游泳模式。

Method: 设计结合代表性鱼种的形态学平均与腱驱动顺应性分段脊柱，通过渐进式机械堵塞重配有效刚度；选择性约束身体分段使变形从全身波动转为后体弯曲与局部尾摆；采用形态测量分析指导设计，并通过尾鳍推力表征、数值体阻模拟、运动学分析和游泳性能实验进行评估。

Result: 运动学分析证实机器人实现了从分布式身体波动到局部尾摆的预期转变；游泳实验显示明显的模式依赖性能，Ostraciiform构型在1.5次尾拍/秒时达到最高直线速度8.14 cm/s和最低运输成本2.42。

Conclusion: 概念验证结果表明，机械结构简单且刚度可重构的架构能够生成不同的BCF游泳模式，为开发多模态软体机器鱼提供了通用策略。

Abstract: In this work, we introduce OmniFish, a bioinspired modular soft robotic fish designed to emulate the full spectrum of body and/or caudal fin (BCF) locomotion modes within a single robotic platform. The design combines morphology averaging across representative fish species with a tendon-driven compliant segmented spine whose effective stiffness can be reconfigured through progressive mechanical jamming. By selectively constraining body segments, OmniFish shifts deformation from whole-body undulation to posterior body bending and localized tail oscillation, enabling bioinspired Anguilliform, Subcarangiform, Carangiform, Thunniform, and Ostraciiform swimming using a single tendon-driven actuator. The design was guided by morphometric analysis and evaluated through caudal-fin thrust characterization, numerical body-drag simulations, kinematic analysis, and swimming performance experiments. Kinematic analysis confirmed the intended robot transition from distributed body undulation to localized tail oscillation. Swimming experiments demonstrated distinct mode-dependent performance, with the Ostraciiform configuration achieving the highest straight-line velocity of 8.14 cm/s and the lowest cost of transport of 2.42 at 1.5 tail beats/s. These proof-of-concept results show that a mechanically simple stiffness-reconfigurable architecture can generate distinct BCF swimming modes and provide a versatile strategy for developing multimodal soft robotic fish.

</details>


### [18] [Single-Controller Teleoperation and Multimodal Dataset Generation for a Mobile Manipulator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24499)
*Fardien Azizi,Sitor Sla,Christiaan Peppelman,Dion van Santen,Andrei-Carlo Papuc,Khaled Mustafa,Diego Martinez-Baselga,Javier Alonso-Mora*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一套集成框架，用于在移动机械臂平台上进行遥操作多模态数据采集，并通过自动化ROS流水线实现数据同步、过滤与导出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人学习系统需要同步的高质量演示数据（视觉、本体感知和动作数据），但移动机械臂的数据采集常因遥操作接口分散和手动后处理流程而效率低下。

Method: 在配备Kinova Gen3 Lite机械臂和Intel RealSense RGB-D相机的Clearpath Dingo-O移动底座上，实现单控制器同时操作底座、机械臂和录制过程，并构建基于ROS的自动化流水线，涵盖数据捕获、图像质量过滤、时间同步、可视化及导出为Zarr格式数据集。

Result: 在拾放移动操作任务中，统一控制器无需外部命令即可完成完整任务执行与录制；数据流水线能为短演示生成同步数据集，并识别损坏的视觉帧。

Conclusion: 该框架为机器人学习研究生成结构化演示数据提供了实用基础。

Abstract: Robot learning systems require synchronized, high-quality demonstrations that combine visual, proprioceptive, and action data. However, collecting such datasets with mobile manipulators is often slowed down by fragmented teleoperation interfaces and manual post-processing workflows. This paper presents an integrated framework for teleoperated multimodal data collection on a Clearpath Dingo-O mobile base equipped with a Kinova Gen3 Lite manipulator and an Intel RealSense RGB-D camera. The framework combines single-controller operation of the base, arm, and recording process with an automated ROS-based pipeline for data capture, image-quality filtering, temporal synchronization, visualization, and export to machine-learning-ready Zarr datasets. We validate the system on a pick-and-place mobile-manipulation task in which the robot transports a bottle between two surfaces. The results show that the unified controller supports complete task execution and recording without external commands, while the data pipeline produces synchronized datasets for short demonstrations and identifies corrupted visual frames. The proposed framework provides a practical basis for producing structured demonstrations for robot-learning research.

</details>


### [19] [When to Refine: Selective Search for Frozen Visual World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24470)
*Jingni Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出选择性CEM精化门控SCR，在不增加世界模型推演的前提下，根据迭代间计划变化决定提前终止或延长搜索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉世界模型规划需同时决定执行哪些动作以及搜索多长时间；固定迭代深度难以在成功率与计算量之间取得自适应平衡。

Method: 在冻结的LeWorldModel上运行CEM，五轮迭代后由学习得到的门控依据精英计划平均终止代价下降幅度、均值首动作块移动距离和首动作块离散程度，决定返回当前计划或再搜索十轮；门控无需额外世界模型推演。

Result: 在16个PushT场景、两个规划种子下，SCR在32次试验中成功25次，固定15轮成功28次，25次成功均被Fixed-15覆盖，配对成功率保留89.29%；完整回合中候选计划评估少23.81%，八环境批次平均控制器时间减少17.22%；成功率比同等期望负载下两种固定深度控制器的解析混合高5.36个百分点，但该差异随规划种子变号；在重规划阶段再次门控比仅在初始求解门控多9次提前退出，候选评估减少15.79%，成功数由27降至25。

Conclusion: SCR能以学习门控实现自适应搜索深度并显著降低计算量，但相对固定深度搜索存在一定成功率损失，且增益对规划种子敏感。

Abstract: Planning with a visual world model requires deciding both which actions to execute and how long to search for them. We introduce Selective CEM Refinement (SCR), a learned rule for deciding whether to continue cross-entropy method (CEM) search with a frozen LeWorldModel. After five it- erations, the gate measures how far the mean first action chunk has moved, how much the mean terminal cost of elite plans has fallen, and how widely their first chunks vary. It returns the current plan for execution or continues the same search for ten more iterations. The gate requires no addi- tional world-model rollout. On 16 PushT scenes evaluated with two planning seeds, SCR succeeded in 25 of 32 trials, compared with 28 of 32 for fixed 15-iteration search (Fixed-15). All 25 SCR suc- cesses occurred in trials that Fixed-15 also solved, yield- ing 89.29% paired success retention. Across complete episodes, including replanning, SCR evaluated 23.81% fewer candidate plans and reduced mean controller time per eight-environment batch by 17.22%. Its observed suc- cess rate was 5.36 percentage points above an analytic mix- ture of the two fixed-depth controllers at the same expected candidate workload, although the difference changed sign across planning seeds. Compared with gating only at the initial solve under the same random-draw padding proto- col, gating again at replanning produced nine additional early exits. This reduced total candidate evaluations by 15.79%, while successes fell from 27 to 25.

</details>


### [20] [Homeward: No Place Like the Training View](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24455)
*Mingyang Lyu,Yinqian Sun,Gongce Cao,Guobin Shen,Yuhao Zhang,Lu Jia,Chenfei Yan,Qian Zhang,Pin Lyu,Chen Gao,Bo Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Homeward是一个免训练、即插即用的观测适配器，将未标定部署相机的观测规范化回训练视角，从而恢复冻结VLA策略在相机位姿变化下的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA策略隐含假设第三人称相机固定在数据采集位姿；部署时相机被碰动、重新安装或更换会导致任务成功率急剧下降，而任务和机器人本身并未改变。

Method: 利用机器人运动学已知的机械臂3D状态，从实时RGB帧和单张训练视角参考图估计未知的相机参数与静态场景深度，并直接由运动学模型渲染已知的机器人连杆；避免现成前馈3D基础模型对细长机械臂深度预测的撕裂与散乱问题。

Result: 在约30万闭环回合、四种冻结VLA和LIBERO-Plus全部1,599种相机变体上，OpenVLA-OFT、π0.5、π0和OpenVLA成功率分别从62.5%、76.5%、68.0%、3.6%提升至77.6%、95.1%、91.2%、26.4%；并可扩展到6-DoF随机摆放、焦距变化及逐步位姿估计下的连续相机漂移，在四个策略中的三个上匹配或超过经标定、场景训练的视图合成基线。

Conclusion: Homeward通过“估计未知、渲染已知”的非对称设计，无需场景微调或部署相机标定即可显著提升冻结VLA策略对相机变化的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) policies implicitly assume that third-person cameras remain fixed at their data-collection poses; once a camera is bumped, remounted, or swapped at deployment, task success degrades precipitously even though the underlying task and robot remain unchanged. We present Homeward, a training-free, plug-and-play observation adapter that canonicalizes observations from an uncalibrated deployment camera back into the training view for frozen VLA policies. Our design stems from a closed-loop diagnostic paradox in simulation: while re-rendering perturbed views into the training camera with oracle geometry recovers much of the lost performance, substituting an off-the-shelf feed-forward 3D foundation model can leave the policy worse off than applying no correction at all. This degradation concentrates on the thin, articulated robot arm, which feed-forward depth prediction tears and scatters - yet the arm's exact 3D state is already known from robot kinematics. Homeward exploits this asymmetry: it estimates what is unknown - camera parameters and static scene depth - from the live RGB frame and a single training-view reference, and renders what is known - the moving robot links - directly from the kinematic model. Evaluated across $\sim$300,000 closed-loop episodes spanning four frozen VLAs, Homeward boosts success across all 1,599 camera variants of LIBERO-Plus from 62.5%, 76.5%, 68.0%, and 3.6% to 77.6%, 95.1%, 91.2%, and 26.4% for OpenVLA-OFT, $\pi_{0.5}$, $\pi_0$, and OpenVLA, respectively. Without any scene fine-tuning or deployment camera calibration, Homeward extends to 6-DoF random placements, focal-length changes, and, with per-step pose estimation, continuous intra-episode camera drift, while matching or surpassing a calibrated, scene-trained view-synthesis baseline on three of four policies on LIBERO-Plus.

</details>


### [21] [Constructive Infinitely Differentiable Guidance Vector Fields for Robotic Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24424)
*Vinicius Mariano Goncalves,Peilin Wu,Adriano Rezende,Weijia Yao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种无限可微的构造性制导向量场，通过平滑参数推广已有方法，并在离散化和计算效率上具有优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有制导向量场方法可能不可微或导数较大，限制了其在需要制导信号导数的级联控制器中的应用。

Method: 提出一种依赖平滑参数的无限可微构造性制导向量场，该向量场是已有方法[1]的推广，当平滑参数趋于零时恢复原向量场；并给出保持离散化平滑性的高效实现。

Result: 理论分析建立了主要性质；数值和实际实验表明，该方法在保持较小路径跟踪误差的同时，可使用更少的曲线采样并减少计算时间。

Conclusion: 所提出的无限可微制导向量场克服了现有方法在可微性和导数大小方面的限制，具有理论保证和实际应用优势。

Abstract: Guidance vector fields (GVFs) are a popular framework for tasks such as robotic path-following and multi-robot motion coordination, but existing approaches may be non-differentiable or have large derivatives, limiting their use in controllers that require derivatives of the guidance signal, which is common when the GVF is used as an input in a cascaded controller. This paper proposes an infinitely differentiable constructive GVF. The resulting vector field depends on a smoothing parameter and is a generalization of [1], recovering the original GVF in the limit as this parameter approaches zero. It also admits a simple and efficient implementation that preserves smoothness under discretization and can reduce computation time by using substantially fewer curve samples while maintaining small path-following errors. We establish the main theoretical properties and demonstrate its practical advantages through numerical and real-world experiments.

</details>


### [22] [Teaching VLMs What to Say, Not How to Reason: Rethinking Counterfactual Reasoning in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24376)
*Hayeon Oh,Donghwan Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文检验了基于视觉语言模型（VLM）的自动驾驶中反事实（CF）推理的真实作用，发现其并未引发基于视觉证据的真正因果推理，而是作为一种语言捷径重塑动作分布，使行为更保守并增加不确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端自动驾驶系统存在捷径学习和因果混淆问题，VLM引入语言推理（尤其是反事实推理）被认为能提升驾驶性能并实现因果理解，但尚不清楚语言推理是缓解了捷径学习，还是仅将捷径从视觉表示转移到语言模式，从而制造因果理解的假象；同时现有方法常混淆干预推理与反事实推理。

Method: 分析VLM自动驾驶中反事实推理实际诱导的效果，包括检查动作分布变化、推理与决策不确定性，进行强化微调测试其持续性，开展视觉注意力分析与因果干预实验（在事故场景中遮蔽因果相关与非因果因素并比较决策变化率）。

Result: 反事实推理使动作分布偏向更保守行为，并增加推理与决策的不确定性，该效应在强化微调下依然存在；视觉注意力分析显示反事实推理未改变视觉 grounding；因果干预实验表明遮蔽因果相关与非因果因素导致的决策变化率相当。

Conclusion: 反事实推理并未引发基于视觉证据的真正因果推理，而是作为语言捷径重塑动作分布，教模型“说什么”而非“如何推理”，揭示了自动驾驶中语言推理与因果理解之间的差距。

Abstract: End-to-end autonomous driving systems have been shown to suffer from shortcut learning and causal confusion, limiting their ability to generalize robustly under distribution shift. To mitigate these limitations, recent works based on Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have explored incorporating linguistic reasoning capabilities into driving decision-making, reporting improvements in driving performance. In particular, counterfactual (CF) reasoning has been proposed to enable self-reflective, high-level causal understanding. However, it remains unclear whether the language-based reasoning mitigates shortcut learning or merely shifts it from visual representations to linguistic patterns, creating an illusion of causal understanding. Moreover, existing approaches often conflate intervention-based reasoning with CF reasoning, leaving the effects of genuine CF reasoning unexplored. In this work, we examine what CF reasoning actually induces in VLM-based autonomous driving and observe that it shifts the action distribution toward more conservative behaviors while increasing uncertainty in reasoning and decision-making. This effect persists under reinforcement fine-tuning, suggesting that it is not merely a transient artifact. Furthermore, visual attention analysis shows that CF reasoning does not alter visual grounding, while causal intervention experiments reveal that masking causally relevant and non-causal factors in accident scenarios results in comparable decision change rates. Overall, these results suggest that CF reasoning does not induce genuine causal reasoning grounded in visual evidence. Instead, it acts as a linguistic shortcut that reshapes the action distribution, teaching models what to say but not how to reason, highlighting a gap between language-based reasoning and causal understanding in autonomous driving.

</details>


### [23] [SearchDiffusion: Information-Guided Diffusion Planning for Object Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24375)
*Sausar Karaf,Dmitrii Plotnikov,Alexey Kotcov,Dzmitry Tsetserukou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SearchDiffusion：一种基于扩散模型的规划器，利用学习的稠密效用图生成位姿轨迹，实现未知环境下的目标搜索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未知环境中机器人搜索目标需在避障和传感器视野受限条件下采集有效观测，传统方法难以兼顾几何可达性、可见性与语义进展。

Method: SearchDiffusion用学习的稠密效用图（表征几何可达性、有效可见性与语义进展）条件化轨迹生成并提供采样引导目标；采样地图假设提供额外几何上下文，结合几何与朝向引导、有效性检查及解析选择支持执行。

Result: 在24种地图布局的90个仿真任务中目标检测率达95.6%，但计算开销更大；消融显示效用与引导互补，运行时地图假设的额外检测收益有限；在OneMap相关性图下进行10次四足机器人实物试验，8次通过操作员评估成功，且未用真实机器人数据微调。

Conclusion: 结果支持将学习到的空间效用与引导式轨迹生成相结合，用于部分可观测条件下的目标搜索。

Abstract: Object search in unknown environments requires robots to gather useful observations while avoiding obstacles and accounting for limited sensor visibility. We present SearchDiffusion, a diffusion-based planner that generates position and heading trajectories using a learned dense utility map. The map represents geometric accessibility, useful visibility, and semantic progress. It conditions trajectory generation and provides an objective for sampling guidance. Sampled map hypotheses provide additional geometric context, while geometric and heading guidance, validity checks, and analytical selection support execution. Across 90 simulated tasks on 24 map layouts, SearchDiffusion achieved 95.6\% target detection at greater computational cost. Ablations show complementary benefits from utility and guidance, with limited additional detection benefit from runtime map hypotheses. In ten physical quadruped trials using OneMap relevance maps, eight were successful under operator assessment, without fine-tuning on real-robot data. These results support combining learned spatial utility with guided trajectory generation for object search under partial observability.

</details>


### [24] [Proprioceptive Simultaneous Base and Terrain Elevation Estimation for Legged Robot Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24310)
*Michael Maravgakis,Stylianos Piperakis,Panos Trahanias*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SBEE框架，将地形高程作为本体感知地标，通过LI-EKF与栅格高程图双向耦合，实现视觉退化环境下腿足机器人基座与高程的实时估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉退化环境中腿足机器人只能依赖本体感知，全局位置和偏航不可观测，垂直漂移会破坏摆动腿轨迹所需的高度参考并导致失稳，因此需要利用地形本身作为持久性地标来锚定垂直轴。

Method: SBEE框架包含用于浮动基座估计的左不变扩展卡尔曼滤波（LI-EKF）和基于栅格的高程图，二者双向闭环耦合；稳定足接触将不确定性感知的高度样本插入地图，已建图单元作为不确定性加权垂直伪测量反馈，类似回环检测；LI-EKF使用运动学速度测量而非为每条腿增广接触模型，状态维度与腿数无关。

Result: 在MuJoCo四足和双足平台上以及ANYmal-D GrandTour数据集上（296米移动）评估，绝对轨迹误差相比三种先进本体感知估计器中最佳者降低22.1%。

Conclusion: 将地形高程视为持久性本体感知地标并双向耦合基座与高程估计，可有效抑制垂直漂移，在视觉退化环境下实现高精度、与腿数无关的实时状态估计。

Abstract: Legged robots in visually degraded environments must rely exclusively on proprioceptive sensing, under which global position and yaw are unobservable. Vertical drift is particularly detrimental, as it corrupts the height reference required by swing-leg trajectories and can destabilize the robot. We treat the terrain itself as a persistent proprioceptive landmark: every footstep provides a height measurement that anchors the vertical axis. We propose Simultaneous Base and Elevation Estimation (SBEE), a real-time open-source framework in which a Left-Invariant Extended Kalman Filter (LI-EKF) for floating-base estimation and a grid-based elevation map are coupled in a bidirectional loop, mirroring SLAM with terrain elevation in place of landmarks. Stable foot contacts insert uncertainty-aware height samples into the map, and previously mapped cells are fed back as uncertainty-weighted vertical pseudo-measurements that act analogously to loop closures. Unlike contact-aided invariant filters, the LI-EKF consumes a kinematic velocity measurement instead of augmenting the state with a contact model per leg, so its state dimension is independent of the number of legs. We evaluate SBEE in MuJoCo on quadrupedal and bipedal platforms and on the ANYmal-D GrandTour dataset over 296 m of locomotion, reducing the absolute trajectory error by 22.1% relative to the best of three state-of-the-art proprioceptive estimators.

</details>


### [25] [Remote Teleoperation of Endovascular Robots: A Network Emulation Framework for Systematic Performance Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24280)
*Xingyu Chen,Nikola Fischer,Harry Robertshaw,Weiyuan Deng,Christos Bergeles,Thomas Booth*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 开发了血管内机器人远程操作网络仿真与基准测试框架，通过16名参与者480次导航实验评估网络退化对操作性能和临床可用性的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 远程血管内遥操作需评估网络性能与物理导航及操作者体验的关系，但网络退化对系统可用性的影响尚未从临床用户角度充分表征。

Method: 结合血管内机器人与数据流传输构建网络仿真和基准测试框架，招募8名介入神经放射学和普通介入放射学临床医生及8名工程学生，在15种网络条件下完成480次导航尝试，评估通信性能、视频质量、导航结果、信心、工作负荷和临床可接受度。

Result: 最差组合条件下成功率为78.1%（参考条件93.8%），平均尝试时间从63.5秒增至94.3秒；5%配置丢包时成功率87.5%，但全部8名临床医生评定该条件不可接受；感知视频质量与临床医生信心和可接受度的组内Spearman相关系数均值分别为0.72和0.78；框架在第二机器人平台上通过真实网络支持了成功远程导航。

Conclusion: 该框架为识别通信瓶颈并评估其对血管内介入远程手术导航和临床医生接受度的影响提供了可复用基础。

Abstract: Remote endovascular teleoperation requires network performance to be evaluated in relation to physical navigation and operator experience, while the effects of network degradation on the usability of remote endovascular systems remain insufficiently characterized from the perspective of clinical users. We developed a network emulation and benchmarking framework that combines an endovascular robot with data streaming. We conducted a user study involving eight clinicians from interventional neuroradiology and general interventional radiology and eight engineering students, who performed 480 navigation attempts across 15 network conditions. Communication performance and received video quality were assessed alongside navigation outcomes, confidence, workload and clinician rated acceptability. Under the most impaired combined condition, success was 78.1\%, compared with 93.8\% under the reference condition, and mean attempt time increased from 63.5 to 94.3\,s. At 5\% configured packet loss, 87.5\% of attempts succeeded, although all eight clinicians rated the condition unacceptable. Perceived video quality was associated with clinician confidence and acceptability, with mean within participant Spearman correlations of 0.72 and 0.78, respectively. Framework integration on a second robotic platform additionally supported successful remote navigation over a real network. Our framework provides a reusable basis that can help identify communication bottlenecks and assess their effects on navigation and clinician acceptance for endovascular intervention telesurgery tasks.

</details>


### [26] [QDD-MM: A Simple and Compact Open-Source Muscle Module with Quasi-Direct-Drive Motor for Tendon-driven Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24279)
*Kento Kawaharazuka,Yoshimoto Ribayashi,Takahiro Hattori,Shunnosuke Yoshimura,Kei Okada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出QDD-MM肌腱驱动肌肉模块，利用准直驱电机实现更简单紧凑、高背驱性和快速驱动的设计，并开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有肌腱驱动机器人的小型肌肉模块结构复杂、难维护，且因高减速比导致背驱性低、驱动速度受限，需要紧凑模块化且灵活适配多种肌肉布局的方案。

Method: 基于准直驱电机设计QDD-MM肌肉模块，省去专用力传感机构以简化结构，支持多方向柔性走线、多种模块间连接，并可将模块本体作为结构件；设计开源。

Result: 用QDD-MM构建了单关节二/三肌肉的肌腱驱动机器人及部分肌肉骨骼人形机器人，并验证了其有效性。

Conclusion: QDD-MM有望降低肌腱驱动机器人门槛并推动该领域进展。

Abstract: Tendon-driven robots, which actuate like biological muscles by using motors to wind wires, offer advantages such as variable stiffness and redundant actuation. Despite these advantages, musculoskeletal structures inherently involve a large number of diverse muscles, making it desirable to compactly modularize each muscle unit and flexibly accommodate diverse muscle arrangements. While various small muscle modules have been proposed to date, existing designs tend to be complex, difficult to maintain, and suffer from low backdrivability and limited actuation speed due to their high gear ratios. In this work, we propose QDD-MM, a muscle module that leverages recent advances in quasi-direct-drive motors to achieve a simpler and more compact structure with higher backdrivability and faster actuation than previous modules. By utilizing quasi-direct-drive motors, the module eliminates the need for dedicated force-sensing mechanisms, significantly simplifying the overall design, while also supporting flexible wire routing in multiple directions, various module-to-module connections, and even the use of the module body itself as a structural component. QDD-MM integrates these capabilities into a reusable muscle module, and its design is openly available at this https URL . We developed tendon-driven robots with one joint and either two or three muscles, as well as a partial musculoskeletal humanoid, using the proposed QDD-MM and verified their effectiveness. We expect that this muscle module will democratize tendon-driven robotics and further accelerate progress in the field.

</details>


### [27] [Old Experience, New Lessons: Relabeling Autonomous Robot Rollouts in Interactive Imitation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24273)
*Zhaoting Li,Cosimo Della Santina,Jens Kober*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SeRA通过集合值监督重标注自主机器人经验，结合人类纠正提升交互式模仿学习性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有交互式模仿学习方法要么未利用无人工动作标签的自主经验，要么模仿历史机器人动作而强化次优行为。

Method: 提出SeRA框架：后续策略检查点重标注自主经验，检查点一致性过滤重标注；由于一致性不保证正确性，围绕接受的重标注构建期望动作集，按SDP训练动作分块扩散策略以提升集合内动作概率；采用步掩码去噪损失在部分监督块中结合接受重标注与人类纠正。

Result: 在五个模拟操作任务的在线与离线设置中，SeRA优于SDP及模仿历史动作或精确重标注的基线；真实番茄采摘任务中，在相同人类反馈与训练预算下成功率达80%，而SDP为40%。

Conclusion: 重标注的自主机器人经验可补充人类纠正，从而提升策略性能。

Abstract: Interactive imitation learning enables robots to learn from human interventions during deployment. These interactions naturally produce sparse human corrections and abundant autonomous robot experience without human action labels. Yet existing methods either leave this experience unused for action supervision or imitate historical robot actions, which can reinforce suboptimal behavior. To address this limitation, we propose Set-valued Relabeling of Autonomous experience (SeRA), a framework that learns from autonomous robot experience through set-valued supervision of policy relabels. Later policy checkpoints relabel this experience, and agreement across checkpoints filters the relabels. Since agreement does not guarantee correctness, SeRA constructs desired action sets around accepted relabels. Following Set-Supervised Diffusion Policy (SDP), it trains an action-chunking diffusion policy to increase the probability of actions within these sets. A step-masked denoising loss combines accepted relabels with available human corrections in partially supervised chunks. Experiments across five simulated manipulation tasks in online and offline settings show that SeRA improves policy performance over SDP and outperforms baselines that imitate historical robot actions or exact relabels. On a real-robot tomato-picking task, SeRA achieves 80% success, compared with 40% for SDP, using the same human feedback and training budget. These results demonstrate that relabeled autonomous robot experience can complement human corrections to improve policy performance.

</details>

*Sheena Shabana,Laxmi Nag Laxminarayan Nagesh,Marta Vallejo,Karen Donaldson,Maria Koskinopoulou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种统一的机器人框架，使用单个真空辅助末端执行器进行超声引导下的液体和组织提取，在体模实验中实现了92%的胸腔积液抽吸和88%的组织获取成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人超声引导针干预主要关注靶点定位和精确针放置，而后续诊断材料的获取相对未被充分探索；胸腔积液抽吸和组织活检通常被视为单独程序，尽管它们共享超声引导、安全进入和样本运输的共同需求。

Method: 该系统结合了力控超声扫描、语义目标识别、自动针部署和程序特定的材料获取。同轴针组件和共享泵辅助路径实现直接胸腔积液抽吸，而受控套管驱动提供获取实体组织所需的额外切割动作。使用Franka Emika Panda机器人在50次体模试验中进行端到端评估。

Result: 在25次胸腔积液抽吸试验中成功提取23次（92%），在25次组织获取试验中成功22次（88%）。在成功进入组织靶点后，所有22次试验均完成了获取和运输。

Conclusion: 该统一机器人框架能够有效执行超声引导下的液体和组织提取，为整合诊断材料获取的机器人干预提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Robotic ultrasound-guided needle interventions have primarily focused on target localization and accurate needle placement, while the subsequent acquisition of diagnostic material remains comparatively underexplored. Moreover, pleural fluid aspiration and tissue biopsy are typically addressed as separate procedures, despite sharing common requirements for ultrasound guidance, safe access, and sample transport. This work presents a unified robotic framework for ultrasound-guided fluid and tissue extraction using a single vacuum-assisted end effector. The proposed system combines force controlled ultrasound scanning, semantic target identification, automated needle deployment, and procedure specific material acquisition. A coaxial needle assembly and shared pump assisted pathway enable direct pleural fluid aspiration, while controlled cannula actuation provides the additional cutting action required for solid-tissue acquisition. End-to-end evaluation using a Franka Emika Panda robot over 50 phantom trials achieved successful extraction in 23/25 pleural fluid aspiration trials (92\%) and 22/25 tissue acquisition trials (88\%). Following successful tissue-target access, acquisition and transport were completed in all 22 trials.

</details>


### [29] [CommDreamer: Radio-Geometric World Models for Mapless Cellular-Connected UAV Trajectory Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24244)
*Yuqi Ping,Tianhao Liang,Tingting Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出CommDreamer，一种无地图无线电-几何世界模型，融合机载3D LiDAR、多小区蜂窝报告与相对运动学，通过类别循环状态空间模型学习坐标不变潜在信念，实现无人机在城市峡谷中无先验地图的联合避障与SINR预测及在线策略训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低空城市峡谷中密集3D建筑遮挡视线、遮蔽基站并造成同频干扰，可靠蜂窝连接与无碰撞飞行至关重要；现有规划器要么依赖机载不可得的3D城市网格与信道知识地图，要么采用样本效率低、跨城市泛化差的无模型强化学习。

Method: CommDreamer融合原始机载3D LiDAR扫描、多小区蜂窝报告与平移不变相对运动学，使用类别循环状态空间模型将多模态测量历史压缩为坐标不变潜在信念，预测局部障碍间隙与方向性SINR动态；仅通过多步潜在想象训练轻量在线策略，无需先验地图或天线方向图。

Result: 在开源光线追踪仿真器AeroCity-RT的未见城市形态零样本评估中，CommDreamer在150回合内收敛（无模型强化学习无法完成飞行），实现100.0%任务成功率与零碰撞，中位路径SINR提升至8.02 dB，有效SINR中断较无线电盲世界模型与反应式LiDAR基线降低超50%。

Conclusion: CommDreamer证明无地图、坐标不变的多模态世界模型可在无先验城市信息下实现高可靠、无碰撞且通信感知的无人机在线规划，并具备跨城市形态的零样本泛化能力。

Abstract: Reliable cellular connectivity and collision-free flight are essential for unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) operations in low-altitude urban canyons, where dense 3D buildings obstruct flight clearance, shadow serving base stations, and create strong co-channel interference at street intersections. Existing planners either require prior 3D city meshes and channel knowledge maps unavailable onboard, or rely on model-free reinforcement learning with low sample efficiency and poor cross-city generalization. We propose CommDreamer, a mapless radio-geometric world model that fuses raw onboard 3D LiDAR scans, multicell cellular reports, and shift-invariant relative kinematics to jointly infer local obstacle clearance and directional signal-to-interference-plus-noise ratio (SINR) dynamics. Using a categorical recurrent state-space model, CommDreamer compresses multimodal measurement histories into a coordinate-invariant latent belief, enabling the UAV to foresee deep shadow zones and train a lightweight online policy purely via multi-step latent imagination without prior maps or antenna patterns. Zero-shot evaluations across unseen urban morphologies in our open-source ray-tracing simulator, AeroCity-RT, show that CommDreamer converges within 150 episodes where model-free reinforcement learning fails to complete flights, achieving 100.0% mission success with zero collisions while lifting median route SINR to 8.02 dB and cutting effective SINR outage by over 50% relative to radio-blind world-model and reactive LiDAR baselines.

</details>


### [30] [ProbeShare: Probing a Few, Sharing Across Many for Fast World-Model Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24229)
*Zejian Kang,Xiantao Lin,Zekai Chen,Boyang Shen,Mingyu Ma,Shengwen Zheng,Chengtai Guo,Xiangru Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出ProbeShare，利用候选动作共享动作到特征映射的观察，通过少量代表性探针测量局部响应并重建特征网格，大幅减少CEM规划中的冗余计算，加速机器人视觉规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准CEM规划器对每个候选动作独立通过十亿参数预测器进行前向计算，即使邻近动作产生的场景变化相似，导致计算量随种群规模和搜索轮次增长，拖慢闭环重规划。

Method: 观察到单步规划内候选共享观测历史与共同的动作到特征映射；用少量代表性探针测量局部响应，并通过场景条件、几何感知加权重建每个候选的特征网格；当有潜力的候选超出已有探针覆盖时，评估新探针以重新评分邻近候选。

Result: 在四个RoboCasa任务上，ProbeShare相比密集RoboJEPA将回合规划加速43.8至128.9倍，相比有竞争力的基线至少加速14.2倍，并在等时比较中取得更高的平均目标进度。

Conclusion: 利用候选间共享的动作到特征映射并以探针重建特征，可在保持规划质量的同时显著加速基于冻结联合嵌入世界模型的机器人视觉规划。

Abstract: Visual planning with a frozen joint-embedding world model supports robotic reaching and object manipulation by evaluating candidate action sequences against an image goal before execution. Standard cross-entropy method (CEM) planners roll out every candidate independently through a billion-parameter predictor, even when nearby actions induce similar scene changes. This redundant computation grows with population size and search rounds, slowing closed-loop replanning. We observe that candidates within a planning step share an observation history and thus a common action-to-feature map. Based on this insight, we propose ProbeShare, which measures local responses with a few representative probes and reconstructs each candidate's feature grid through scene-conditioned, geometry-aware weighting. When promising candidates move beyond existing probe coverage, ProbeShare evaluates a new probe to rescore nearby candidates. Across four RoboCasa tasks, ProbeShare accelerates episode planning by 43.8--128.9 times over dense RoboJEPA and at least $14.2\times$ over competitive baselines, with greater mean goal progress in a matched-time comparison.

</details>


### [31] [Neural Network-Based In-Contact Task Segmentation of Demonstrated Robot Motions via Hand Guiding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24226)
*Johannes Hartwig,Marcello Kögler,Dominik Henrich*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于神经网络的分割方法，利用末端笛卡尔 twists 和 wrenches 数据将运动分为三种基本类型，以提升非专家通过示范编程进行接触式任务的可用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非专家通过手把手示范编程机器人时，接触式任务需对部分运动进行力控制，自动分割运动类型对提高可用性至关重要。

Method: 采集7轴机器人腕部力/力矩传感器数据（笛卡尔 twists 与 wrenches），手工生成真值标签，训练神经网络将运动分为三类基本类型。

Result: 研究仍在开发中，尚未给出定量分割性能结果。

Conclusion: 目标是使非专家能更直观地编程接触式任务，当前方向为神经网络运动类型分割。

Abstract: Intuitive robot programming approaches, such as Programming by Demonstration, enable non-experts to teach robots tasks through hand-guided demonstrations. However, for in-contact tasks requiring force control for parts of the motion, automated segmentation of motion types is crucial for improving usability. Considering the related work, this work proposes a neural network-based segmentation method categorizing motion into three fundamental types using input data of cartesian twists and wrenches at the end effector. We collect demonstration data using a 7-axis robot with a wrist-mounted force torque sensor and generate ground truth labeling by hand. While still in development, our research direction aims to enable non-experts to program in-contact tasks more intuitively.

</details>


### [32] [TapTacTouch: Shape Completion from Sparse Tactile Contacts with Geometric Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24207)
*Yoobin Shin,Jaeyoung Lee,Jieun Kang,Hyun Song,Woojin Kim,Andrew Jaeyong Choi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种无需视觉、无需训练、与类别无关的框架，利用稀疏触觉接触重建3D物体网格。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统3D重建依赖视觉，但视觉受遮挡或环境限制；希望仅用稀疏触觉接触实现连续3D重建。

Method: 6-DoF机械臂配20-DoF多指手，指尖3x3压力传感器阵列采集触觉；经运动学转为3D接触约束，融入多尺度高斯过程隐式曲面（GPIS），并加入接触置信加权、弱伪表面约束和观测驱动几何先验以处理稀疏不完整观测。

Result: 在六个真实物体上达到Chamfer距离1.610、EMD 7.738、F-Score 95.00，平均每物体重建耗时49.99秒。

Conclusion: 稀疏触觉感知可在无视觉、无语义信息条件下提供足够几何信息，实现连续3D物体重建。

Abstract: 3D object reconstruction is essential for robotic perception and manipulation, yet conventional approaches predominantly rely on visual observations that can be limited by occlusion or unavailable in visually constrained environments. In this work, we propose a vision-free, training-free, and category-agnostic framework for reconstructing 3D object meshes from sparse tactile contacts without relying on pretrained shape models. A 6-DoF robotic arm equipped with a 20-DoF multi-finger robotic hand collects tactile observations using 3x3 pressure sensor arrays mounted on the finger pads. The acquired tactile measurements are transformed into 3D contact constraints through robot kinematics and integrated into a multi-scale Gaussian Process Implicit Surface (GPIS). To address sparse and incomplete surface observations, the proposed method incorporates contact confidence weighting, weak pseudo-surface constraints, and an observation-driven geometric prior to preserve local surface geometry and provide structural guidance in unobserved regions. Real-world experiments on six objects achieve a Chamfer Distance (CD) of 1.610, an Earth Mover's Distance (EMD) of 7.738, and an F-Score of 95.00, with reconstruction completed in an average of 49.99 s per object. These results demonstrate that sparse tactile sensing can provide sufficient geometric information for continuous 3D object reconstruction without visual or semantic information.

</details>


### [33] [High-Precision Docking of Differential-Drive Mobile Robots Through LiDAR and Vision Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24191)
*Zaide Zhang,Zheyuan Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 结合LiDAR与视觉的差速驱动机器人精确对接方法，在短距离内校正横向偏差，111次任务全部完成，96.4%满足±10 mm与±1°验收标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 差速驱动机器人在短距离接近中需通过耦合旋转与平移校正横向偏移，单一传感器难以兼顾全局导航与终端精确定位。

Method: 融合LiDAR与视觉观测：拟合柜体前缘线获取法向距离与航向；用AprilTag识别工作站，其中心射线与LiDAR平面相交确定切向位置；以示教观测定义对接参考并抵消固定偏差；采用横向预对准与连续视觉伺服，并预测性停车补偿低速驱动滞后。

Result: 两轮会话中5个工作站111次任务全部完成（101次首次成功）；相对示教观测的纵向、横向、航向残差RMS分别为1.51 mm、5.93 mm、0.238°；±10 mm/轴与±1°联合验收通过率96.4%（107/111），航向限±0.5°时为95.5%（106/111）。

Conclusion: LiDAR与视觉互补的多传感器方案可在短距离内实现毫米级、亚度级的重复对接精度，示教参考能有效抵消固定偏差，满足严格对接验收要求。

Abstract: Precise docking requires a differential-drive robot to correct lateral offset through coupled rotation and translation within a short approach distance. We combine global navigation with terminal control using complementary LiDAR and visual observations. A line fitted to the cabinet front gives normal distance and heading. An AprilTag identifies the workstation; intersecting its center ray with the LiDAR plane determines tangential position. Taught observations define the docking reference and can cancel repeatable fixed biases under unchanged mounting and geometry. Lateral pre-alignment and continuous visual servoing coordinate the approach, with predictive stopping to account for low-speed actuation lag. Across two sessions using the same navigation and docking code at five workstations on Robot 2, all 111 tasks completed, including 101 on the first attempt. Relative to the taught observations, onboard longitudinal, lateral and heading residual root-mean-square values were 1.51 mm, 5.93 mm and 0.238 degrees, respectively. Joint acceptance within plus or minus 10 mm per position axis and plus or minus 1 degree heading was 96.4% (107/111), or 95.5% (106/111) with a plus or minus 0.5 degree heading limit.

</details>


### [34] [Dual-Actuation Emulation of Scaled Relative Orbital Dynamics on a Free-Flyer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24173)
*Elias Krantz,Pedro Roque,Gunnar Tibert,Huina Mao,Christer Fuglesang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种双驱动架构，在开源自由飞行器上物理模拟Clohessy-Wiltshire轨道相对动力学，并通过硬件实验与高保真轨道仿真对比验证其一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地面气浮自由飞行器试验台只能复现平面运动，无法复现航天器在轨相对动力学，限制了控制器的真实在轨验证。

Method: 采用两个并行运行的模型预测控制器：一个基于轨道动力学模型驱动电磁推力器，代表被测航天器；另一个驱动双向螺旋桨物理施加轨道加速度，并持续更新参考以补偿推力器引起的速度变化；同时用动态相似映射将轨道场景缩放到试验台有限空间与驱动速率，使航天器控制器可直接按轨道尺度运行。

Result: 在硬件上验证该架构，并与高保真轨道仿真对比，其中轨道加速度自然产生且由同一航天器控制器驱动，两者结果一致。

Conclusion: 该试验台能够复现控制器在飞行中将遇到的相对轨道运动，为在轨相对动力学控制提供物理仿真验证手段。

Abstract: Ground-based free-flyer testbeds reproduce the planar motion of a free-floating spacecraft using air bearings, but not the relative dynamics of spacecraft in orbit. We present a dual-actuation architecture that physically emulates Clohessy-Wiltshire dynamics on an open-source free-flyer. Two model predictive controllers run in parallel: one drives the solenoid thrusters with an orbital dynamics model, representing the spacecraft under test; the other drives bidirectional propellers that physically apply the orbital accelerations, continuously updating its reference to account for thruster-induced velocity changes. A dynamic similitude maps the orbital scenario onto the limited floor area and actuation rates of the testbed, letting the spacecraft controller run directly at orbital scale. We validate the architecture on hardware and against a high-fidelity orbital simulation, where the orbital accelerations instead arise naturally, both driven by the identical spacecraft controller. Their agreement indicates that the testbed reproduces the relative orbital motion the controller would encounter in flight.

</details>


### [35] [BEV-Diffusion: Camera-Only Traversability Navigation with BEV Memory and Efficient Cross-Robot Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24155)
*Iana Zhura,Timofei Kozlov,Dmitrii Plotnikov,Ivan Valuev,Miguel Altamirano Cabrera,Dzmitry Tsetserukou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出仅用相机的BEV-Diffusion模型，在单一网络中联合学习鸟瞰图可通行性地图、其不确定性及路径，实现高效机器人导航与跨平台快速适配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人导航方法依赖昂贵的LiDAR、长期建图及模块化流程，且难以快速适配新机器人，因此需要一种仅依赖相机、可端到端学习且适配成本低的方法。

Method: 使用冻结的DINOv2主干编码连续RGB帧，通过逆透视映射与贝叶斯融合提升至BEV空间；采用排序损失实现分级可通行性（区分沥青与草地）；用Transformer去噪器经少量DDIM步生成极坐标路点，并以不确定性感知引导优化路径；部署时使用度量BEV记忆保留视野外障碍，并以自运动补偿的上一路径热启动采样。

Result: 在未见庭院中，轮腿机器人自主行驶120米，96%路线无需干预（基线75-88%）；日落后低光条件下亦无需干预完成路线；仅重训BEV头（约3%参数）可在2.3分钟内适配小型足式机器人；室内记忆保留62-66%视野外邻近障碍（基线至多3%），任务进度67-100%（基线20-40%）。

Conclusion: 联合学习路径、地图及其不确定性使规划器选择可自信通行的路线，仅相机方案在性能、鲁棒性和跨机器人适配效率上显著优于基线。

Abstract: Robot navigation requires both an understanding of the scene and an efficient adaptation to new robots, yet current methods rely on expensive LiDAR sensors, long mapping and modular pipelines. We present BEV-Diffusion, a camera-only model that learns a bird's-eye view (BEV) traversability map, its uncertainty and the path in a single network. A frozen DINOv2 backbone encodes consecutive RGB frames, which inverse perspective mapping and Bayesian fusion lift into the BEV, and a ranking loss keeps outdoor traversability graded rather than binary, separating asphalt from grass (median 0.94 vs. 0.75). On this map, a transformer denoiser generates polar waypoints in a few DDIM steps, and uncertainty-aware guidance refines the path and ends it where the map is no longer reliable. At deployment, a metric BEV memory keeps obstacles after they leave the camera view and warm starts each sample from the previous, ego-motion compensated path. Because the planner jointly learns the path, the map, and its uncertainty, it selects routes through terrain it can confidently traverse. In an unseen courtyard, a wheeled-legged robot autonomously traveled 120 m, completing 96% of the route without intervention, compared with 75-88% for the baselines. Even under low-light conditions after sunset, the proposed model completed the route without requiring any intervention. Retraining only the BEV head, about 3% of the parameters, adapts the model to a small legged robot in 2.3 min. Indoors, the memory gets this robot out of maze-like traps: it retains 62-66% of the nearby out-of-view obstacles, against at most 3% for the baselines, and the robot achieves 67-100% task progress, compared with 20-40% for the evaluated baselines.

</details>


### [36] [Assessing Vision-Language Models for Early Prediction of Unsafe Situations in Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24146)
*Muhammad Farooq,Aitor Arrieta,Paolo Arcaini,Shaukat Ali,Thomas Peyrucain*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文对Qwen、InternVL和LLaVA-OneVision三种视觉语言模型在三个机器人案例中预测机器人故障的能力进行了实证评估，发现Qwen分类与可靠性最佳，InternVL早期预警能力更强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型具备理解与推理上下文的能力，理论上适合在机器人故障发生前进行预测，但支持这一点的实证证据仍然有限，因此需要系统评估。

Method: 在HuRoN、Pal Robotics和CrowdBot三个机器人案例中，分析视频与里程计数据，评估Qwen、InternVL和LLaVA-OneVision将情境分类为安全、潜在不安全和不安全的表现，并用多种指标量化不确定性，研究不确定性与性能的关系。

Result: Qwen在整体分类和预测可靠性上表现最强，InternVL在多种配置下展现出更强的早期预警能力；机器人状态信息和时间历史的影响取决于模型与评估目标。

Conclusion: 通用视觉语言模型可支持早期机器人安全监控，但最佳配置取决于优先目标是准确评估安全状态、可靠的置信度与不确定性，还是在危险事件前及时预警。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) process visual information and textual instructions and, by using their ability to understand and reason about context, are well-suited for predicting robot failures before they occur. However, evidence supporting this remains limited. To address this, we present an empirical evaluation of VLMs for predicting robot failures. In particular, we experiment with Qwen, InternVL, and LLaVA-OneVision across three robotic case studies (HuRoN, Pal Robotics, and CrowdBot), analyzing both video and odometry data for early failure warning (i.e., before an unsafe situation occurs). Specifically, we evaluate their performance in classifying situations as safe, potentially unsafe, and unsafe, together with uncertainty quantified using various metrics, and study the relationship between uncertainty and performance. Our results showed that Qwen provides the strongest overall classification and prediction-reliability performance, while InternVL shows stronger early-warning capability in several configurations. The results further show that the effect of robot-state information and temporal history depends on the model and evaluation objective. Overall, our findings indicate that general-purpose VLMs can support early robot-safety monitoring, but the most suitable configuration depends on whether the priority is accurate assessment of safety-state, reliable confidence and uncertainty, or timely warning before unsafe events.

</details>


### [37] [BiPathCPG: A Novel Load-Sharing Design Architecture for Compound Planetary Gearboxes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24142)
*Deepak Kapa,Aman Singh,Saketh Vegesna,Suryank Joshi,Mayank Goswami,Shishir Kolathaya*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出BiPathCPG双路径复合行星齿轮箱架构，在传统CPG的闲置空间内引入惰性二级太阳轮和均载二级行星轮，同时提升刚度、减小宽度和降低质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QDD执行器常用行星或摆线齿轮箱，但单级行星扭矩密度有限、多级效率低、摆线存在扭矩脉动；传统复合行星齿轮箱（CPG）虽折中，却导致执行器宽度、质量增加及3D打印件扭转刚度下降。

Method: 设计BiPathCPG架构，在传统CPG未利用空间内加入惰性二级太阳轮与均载二级行星轮；建立优化框架，针对给定电机和目标传动比计算参数；通过3D打印概念验证执行器及定制测试台架与传统CPG对比验证。

Result: BiPathCPG扭转刚度达1394 Nm/rad，较传统CPG提升1.70倍；背隙降低32%，质量减轻25 g，轴向宽度减少约6.4 mm，峰值堵转扭矩达36.49 Nm。

Conclusion: BiPathCPG可在不牺牲扭矩能力的前提下同时改善刚度、背隙、质量和轴向尺寸，是一种适用于QDD执行器的有效复合行星齿轮箱新架构。

Abstract: Quasi-Direct Drive (QDD) actuators are widely used in legged robots due to their low reflected inertia, high backdrivability, and high torque density, commonly employing planetary or cycloidal gearboxes. Single-stage planetary gearboxes offer high efficiency but limited torque density; multi-stage designs improve torque density at the cost of efficiency; and cycloidal gearboxes provide high stiffness but suffer from torque ripple. Compound Planetary Gearboxes (CPG) offer a compromise, but conventional designs result in increased actuator width, mass, and reduced torsional stiffness in 3D-printed actuators.
