This paper proposes the BiPath Compound Planetary Gearbox (BiPathCPG), a novel architecture that introduces an idle second-stage sun gear and load-sharing second-stage planets within the otherwise unused space of a conventional CPG to simultaneously improve stiffness, reduce actuator width, and reduce mass. An optimization framework computes BiPathCPG parameters for a given motor and target gear ratio. The architecture is validated through a 3D-printed proof-of-concept actuator design, benchmarked against a conventional CPG using custom-built test rigs. The BiPathCPG achieves a torsional stiffness of 1394 Nm/rad, a 1.70x improvement over the conventional CPG, with a 32% reduction in backlash, 25 g lower mass, and approximately 6.4 mm reduction in axial width, while achieving a peak stall torque of 36.49 Nm.

</details>


### [38] [Asymmetric Decomposition for Multi-Agent Path Finding with Unassigned Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24108)
*Xi Jin,Chen Qian,Renjun Zhang,Guowei Wu,Xiaobo Li,Jun Jiang,Weiwei Shang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出FOCBS算法解决含未分配智能体的多智能体路径规划问题，利用最小费用流oracle为无目标任务智能体规划路径，并通过流冲突分支保证完备性与最优性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未被分配任务的机器人可能阻塞活跃机器人，而将其调度至固定停车点又会产生不必要的移动开销，因此需要在规划中同时处理已分配与未分配智能体的路径。

Method: 基于冲突搜索（CBS）在已分配智能体路径空间搜索，用最小费用流oracle为无目标任务智能体规划路径；当所选路径导致未分配智能体无无碰撞路径时，采用流冲突分支将最小割阻塞转化为已分配智能体约束；并采用批量零成本热启动和避让起点平局打破策略。

Result: 在有限图上证明算法终止且全局最小化已分配智能体服务时间和（以最后到达时间计）；基准实验显示FOCBS随未分配智能体数量扩展良好；消融实验表明流冲突分支比丢弃不可完成路径能求解更多实例且保持最优性；仿真与实体机器人实验验证可执行性，实验室测试相对停车协调基线降低车队移动40.8%。

Conclusion: FOCBS有效解决了MAPF-UA问题，在保证已分配智能体最优性的同时条件最小化未分配智能体移动，并在实际场景中显著减少车队移动。

Abstract: Robots without assigned tasks may obstruct active robots, while routing them to designated parking locations can cause unnecessary travel. This article proposes flow-oracle conflict-based search (FOCBS) for Multi-Agent Path Finding with unassigned agents (MAPF-UA). FOCBS searches over assigned-agent paths using conflict-based search (CBS) and routes targetless unassigned agents through a min-cost flow oracle. When the selected assigned-agent paths leave unassigned agents no collision-free paths, flow-conflict branching converts min-cut blockers into assigned-agent constraints, which guide the search without excluding optimal solutions. A batched zero-cost warm start accelerates the oracle, and start-avoiding tie-breaking reduces unassigned-agent movement. For feasible instances on finite graphs, we prove termination and global minimization of assigned-agent sum of service time, measured by last-arrival times. Unassigned-agent movement is minimized conditional on the selected assigned-agent paths, without a global lexicographic optimality guarantee. Benchmark experiments show that FOCBS scales well with the number of unassigned agents, and ablation studies show that, compared with discarding non-completable assigned-agent paths, flow-conflict branching solves substantially more instances while retaining optimality. Simulation and physical-robot experiments demonstrate plan executability, with laboratory tests reducing fleet travel by 40.8% relative to the parking-based coordination baseline. The source code and related resources are available at this https URL

</details>


### [39] [Lost Between Mind, Model Collaboration, and Motion: Understanding and Measuring Cross-Model Action Misalignment in Commercial Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24100)
*Yuhang Huang,Junchao Li,Boyang Ma,Minghui Xu,Kaidi Xu,Pengfei Hu,Xiuzhen Cheng,Yue Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出TARS黑盒框架，用于检测具身机器人中LLM与动作模型之间因语义边界导致的非预期物理行为，并在四款商用机器人上验证了广泛的跨模型动作失配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身机器人依赖LLM理解用户意图并协调专用动作模型执行物理交互，多模型流水线在“理解—翻译—执行”之间产生关键语义边界，可能使用户输入即使不含显式动作命令也触发非预期物理动作，构成安全风险。

Method: 开发TARS黑盒框架，自动探索命令—推理—动作流水线，发现并验证非预期物理行为；在四款商用机器人、44种实验设置下共执行133,400次。

Result: TARS揭示广泛的跨模型动作失配，验证动作覆盖率达94%。

Conclusion: 多模型具身流水线存在系统性语义一致性与动作对齐安全风险，TARS可有效自动化发现并验证此类非预期物理行为。

Abstract: Embodied robots increasingly rely on large language models (LLMs) to interpret user intent and coordinate with robot-specific action models for physical interaction. This multi-model pipeline creates a critical semantic boundary across mind, model collaboration, and motion: semantic consistency may break between what the large model understands, how the small action model translates that understanding into executable commands, and what physical actions the robot ultimately performs. As a security consequence, commercial robots may execute unintended physical actions even when user inputs contain no explicit action commands. To measure this problem, we develop TARS, a black-box framework that explores the command-reasoning-action pipeline and automatically discovers and validates unintended physical behaviors. We evaluate TARS on four commercial robots across 44 experimental settings, conducting 133,400 executions. TARS uncovers widespread cross-model action misalignment and achieves up to 94% verified action coverage.

</details>


### [40] [JEPA Dots: Dual-Latent World Model for Language-Guided Robot Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24062)
*Xianhao Pan,Chi Li,Hanchao Li,Yunxin Liu,Jiankai Sun,Luhui Hu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出JEPA Dots双潜变量框架，将语言引导任务表示与视觉动态预测解耦，在LIBERO任务上以更小参数量取得更高成功率并验证动态预测对指令不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言引导机器人控制中，指令应影响动作选择，但不应改变相同观测与固定动作下的预测结果，现有预测世界模型难以满足这种条件约束。

Method: 构建紧凑双潜变量框架，分别学习动力学表示与任务表示；语言条件任务特征驱动流匹配策略，掩码注意力将世界预测限制在指令无关的动态历史与给定动作上。

Result: 在40个LIBERO任务闭环评估中总体成功率84.9%，约39.1M参数；参考实验中较任务ID条件基线76.0%提升至84.9%，LIBERO-Goal从67.1%升至88.5%；指令切换实验显示任务表示与策略输出随语言变化，而动态表示与世界预测在固定观测与动作下保持不变。

Conclusion: JEPA Dots有效实现任务条件与动态预测的解耦，在较小模型规模下取得较高控制成功率，并满足指令不影响固定动作预测的条件要求。

Abstract: Predictive world models for language-guided robot control face a fundamental conditioning challenge: instructions should influence action selection without altering the predicted consequences of fixed actions under identical observations. To address this challenge, we introduce JEPA Dots (Dynamics-Oriented Task Separation), a compact dual-latent framework that jointly learns visual dynamics and language-guided control through separate dynamics and task representations. Language-conditioned task features guide a flow-matching policy, while masked attention restricts world prediction to instruction-independent dynamics history and supplied actions. In closed-loop evaluation on 40 trained LIBERO tasks, JEPA Dots achieves 84.9% overall success with approximately 39.1M parameters, compared with the published 78.7% of NanoVLA-S with 161.0M parameters under a different evaluation protocol. In the reference experiment, JEPA Dots also improves overall success from 76.0% for our task-ID-conditioned adaptation baseline to 84.9%, with LIBERO-Goal success increasing from 67.1% to 88.5%. Instruction-switching experiments further confirm that task representations and policy outputs respond to language changes, while dynamics representations and world predictions remain invariant under fixed observations and supplied actions.

</details>


### [41] [Modelling the human-exoskeleton interaction interface - Benchmarking MuJoCo's deformable model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24008)
*Lisette Masselink,Xiaolong Zhu Thomas George Thuruthel,Mohamed Irfan Refai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MuJoCo的flexcomp软组织模型（StVK、SNH）与COMSOL的Yeoh有限元模型在三种工况下进行基准对比，结果显示MuJoCo能捕捉力趋势但偏硬、低估滞后，计算速度优势依赖于工况。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人-外骨骼交互界面涉及外骨骼附着与人体软组织的相互作用，影响助力传递；MuJoCo的flexcomp曾被用于软组织建模，但其精度尚未与有限元模型进行基准验证。

Method: 在三种工况（球形压痕、压缩、大腿压缩）下，将MuJoCo的StVK和SNH模型与COMSOL的Yeoh模型对比，比较力与应变能随压痕深度的变化、滞后及计算时间。

Result: MuJoCo模型与有限元结果的力趋势相关系数为0.97-0.99，但响应偏硬（可能源于线性有限元的体积锁定），并系统性低估滞后（Rayleigh阻尼无法替代粘弹性模型）；计算速度方面，球形压痕下快2-2.5倍，但自由度与接触更多的其他工况下COMSOL更快。

Conclusion: MuJoCo flexcomp虽结合了软组织建模与多体动力学，但在用于人-外骨骼交互界面分析前，需解决其偏硬和滞后低估的局限。

Abstract: The human-exoskeleton interaction interface, consisting of the exoskeleton attachment and the underlying human soft tissue, can affect the transfer of assistance. MuJoCo's \textit{flexcomp} has been previously used to model the soft tissue, but its accuracy has not been benchmarked against a finite element model. We benchmarked the \textit{flexcomp} Saint Venant-Kirchhoff (StVK) and Stable Neo-Hookean (SNH) models against COMSOL's Yeoh model in three cases: \textit{case 1}) spherical indentation, \textit{case 2}) compression, and \textit{case 3}) compression of a thigh. We compared force and strain energy as a function of indentation depth, hysteresis and computation time. MuJoCo's models capture force trends with a correlation of 0.97-0.99, but showed a stiffer response, which could be attributed to the volumetric locking of the linear finite elements. The MuJoCo models systematically underestimated hysteresis as MuJoCo's Rayleigh damping could not substitute a viscoelastic model. Moreover, MuJoCo's computational advantage was speed dependent: it was 2-2.5 times faster for spherical indentation, but COMSOL was faster for the other cases, which have more degrees of freedom and contacts. Although MuJoCo's \textit{flexcomp} offers a combination of soft-tissue modelling and multibody dynamics, the limitations regarding the stiffening and hysteresis should be addressed to be used for the analysis of the human-exoskeleton interaction interface.

</details>


### [42] [LaST1.0: A Unified Latent World-Action Model with Control-Centric SpatioTemporal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23974)
*Hao Chen,Xuheng Zhang,Zhuangzhe Wu,Jiaming Liu,Shaobo Zhang,Jialin Gao,Zhonghao Yan,Di Wu,Sixiang Chen,Peng Jia,Chi-Wing Fu,Shanghang Zhang,Pheng-Ann Heng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LaST1.0提出以控制为中心的潜在世界-动作模型，通过在潜在空间预测未来腕部视角的紧凑几何感知特征，替代重建式视频预测，在多个操控任务和基准上取得最优成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界-动作模型采用重建导向方式建模未来视频，将大量容量分配给与控制的无关的外观细节，导致建模效率低且对精细操控所需机器人-物体交互关注不足。

Method: 将操控世界建模重构为潜在空间中的控制中心未来预测：不重建完整视频，而是预测来自未来腕部视角的紧凑几何感知潜在目标；采用无视觉编码器设计配轻量patch embedder，将细粒度视觉线索直接输入主干，并引入LaST-Attention实现跨模态结构化时空交互。

Result: 在两个机器人本体、五项挑战性操控任务及RLBench、LIBERO、LIBERO-Plus三个仿真基准上评估，LaST1.0取得最先进成功率，并能泛化到新场景。

Conclusion: 控制中心的潜在世界建模能有效提升鲁棒操控性能，验证了该范式相较于重建式未来预测的优越性。

Abstract: World-Action Models couple action learning with future prediction, enabling robotic policies to capture environmental dynamics for robust control. However, prevailing methods take a reconstruction-oriented approach to model future videos, allocating substantial capacity to appearance details that are incidental to control. In this work, we reformulate world modeling for manipulation as control-centric future prediction in latent space, and present LaST1.0, a unified latent World-Action Model. Rather than modeling an observation-complete video future, LaST1.0 predicts compact, geometry-aware latent targets derived from future wrist views, focusing the supervision on the evolving robot-object interactions required for fine-grained manipulation. To better support this control-centric paradigm, LaST1.0 revisits the visual input interface and attention mechanism of its unified architecture. It adopts a vision-encoder-free design with a lightweight patch embedder, exposing fine-grained visual cues directly to the backbone, and introduces LaST-Attention for structured spatiotemporal interactions across modalities. Evaluations across two robot embodiments, five challenging manipulation tasks, and three simulation benchmarks---RLBench, LIBERO, and LIBERO-Plus---demonstrate that LaST1.0 achieves state-of-the-art success rates and generalizes to novel scenarios. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of control-centric latent world modeling for robust manipulation.

</details>


### [43] [CoRA-VTLA: Contact-Guided Attention and Queryable Response Memory for Tactile Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23963)
*Chengye Song,Rui Song,Shengqi Wang,Jiawei Zhang,Hongzhou Wang,Xueyang Guo,Yang Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CoRA-VTLA通过轻量触觉模块、可查询动作响应记忆和接触引导注视监督，将触觉反馈融入预训练VLA模型，在MuJoCo、UniVTAC和真实机器人任务上显著提升精密操作成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精密操作中微小对齐或力误差会导致卡阻或变形，需触觉反馈使预训练VLA适应此类任务；但单次触觉观测对近期动作响应存在歧义，直接触觉融合会分散动作专家的视觉注意力并减少接触区域关注。

Method: 在预训练VLA上增加轻量触觉模块；设计可查询的动作响应记忆以检索近期接触响应，供动作专家与即时触觉反馈结合；采用接触引导的注视监督，使触觉语义令牌的视觉注意力集中于指尖接触对应图像区域。

Result: 在三个MuJoCo任务上平均成功率达73.33%（直接触觉融合为50%）；在公开UniVTAC基准的光学触觉任务上成功率达67%；在三个真实机器人任务上平均成功率达66.7%。

Conclusion: 触觉后训练能帮助预训练VLA适应变化的接触条件，验证了所提方法的有效性。

Abstract: In precision manipulation, small errors in alignment or force can cause jamming or deformation, making contact feedback essential for adapting pretrained vision-language-action (VLA) models to these tasks. A single tactile observation leaves the response to recent actions ambiguous. Direct tactile fusion during post-training can also disperse the action expert's visual attention and reduce the share it allocates to contact regions. To address these challenges, CoRA-VTLA augments pretrained VLAs with lightweight tactile modules. A queryable action-response memory retrieves recent contact responses so that the action expert can combine them with immediate tactile feedback. Contact-guided gaze supervision concentrates the visual attention of the tactile semantic token on image regions corresponding to fingertip contact. CoRA-VTLA achieves 73.33% mean success across three MuJoCo tasks, compared with 50% for direct tactile fusion with the same sensor inputs. It also achieves 67% success on an optical-tactile task from the public UniVTAC benchmark and 66.7% mean success across three real-robot tasks. These results show that tactile post-training helps pretrained VLAs adjust to changing contact.

</details>


### [44] [3DTouch: A Tactile-aware VLA Model with 3D Geometric Relations for Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23961)
*Xin Zhang,Shiqi Zhang,Siyuan Cao,Juncheng Cai,Yedong Shen,Yuxuan Gao,Dianlong Wen,Kaixue Long,Lu Zhang,Yao Li,Jiajun Deng,Yanyong Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 3DTouch 通过将 RGB-D 场景点与五指触觉测量统一表达在机器人基座坐标系中，构建共享的 3D 视触觉表征来条件化 VLA 模型，提升接触丰富灵巧操作的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触丰富灵巧操作中，手指与交互物体会遮挡局部几何，视觉观测无法完整捕捉接触位置；视觉相似场景在物体相对位置上存在细微差异，导致不同接触状态，因此需要显式引入空间化触觉与几何关系信息。

Method: 提出 3DTouch 触觉-几何条件化方法：在机器人基座坐标系中表达 RGB-D 场景点和稠密五指触觉测量，保留每个 taxel 的空间位置与力向量；由手部关键点和姿态对齐的重建表面推导手-物、物-物关系，显式描述交互实体的相对排布；以这些表征条件化 VLA 动作专家。

Result: 在 UR7e 机械臂配 XHand 灵巧手的五个真实接触丰富灵巧操作任务上，平均成功率较对应 VLA 基线提升 42 个百分点，较最强对比方法提升 20 个百分点；消融实验显示场景几何与空间触觉反馈具有互补收益，共享空间 grounding 与显式几何关系带来进一步增益。

Conclusion: 空间定位的触觉力与相对表面几何为接触调整和物体对齐提供了显式线索，3DTouch 能有效弥补视觉遮挡与细微场景差异带来的不足，显著提升接触丰富灵巧操作性能。

Abstract: In contact-rich dexterous manipulation, occlusion by the fingers and interacting objects obscures local geometry, preventing visual observations from fully capturing contact locations. Visually similar scenes may also differ subtly in the relative positions of interacting objects, leading to different contact states. We propose 3DTouch, a tactile-geometric conditioning approach for vision-language-action (VLA) models. 3DTouch constructs a shared 3D visuo-tactile representation by expressing RGB-D scene points and dense five-finger tactile measurements in the robot-base frame, preserving each taxel's spatial location and force vector. Hand-object and object-object relations derived from hand landmarks and pose-aligned reconstructed surfaces explicitly describe the relative arrangement of interacting entities. Together, these representations condition the VLA action expert on spatially localized contact forces and relative surface geometry, providing explicit cues for contact adjustment and object alignment. We evaluate 3DTouch on five real-world contact-rich dexterous manipulation tasks using a UR7e robot arm equipped with an XHand dexterous hand. 3DTouch improves the average success rate by 42 percentage points over the corresponding VLA baseline and by 20 percentage points over the strongest evaluated alternative. Ablation studies reveal the complementary benefits of scene geometry and spatial tactile feedback, with further gains from shared spatial grounding and explicit geometric relations.

</details>


### [45] [Effects of Locomotion and Viewpoint Control on Immersive Robot Telepresence for Navigation and Inspection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23939)
*Ammaar Zaman,Neven ElSayed,Oliver Prentner,Mohamed Ellethy,Mostafa M. Lotfy,Maram Selsabila Mahmoudi,Eduardo Veas*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 在VR远程操作四足机器人同时导航与检查的任务中，头部引导相机控制优于摇杆控制，而移动方式仍影响客观性能与体验的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人远程临场界面常用手持输入同时控制移动与视角，导致在转向、观察和检查之间注意力分散，需比较不同移动与视角控制方式的影响。

Method: 采用被试内2×2实验，17名参与者佩戴VR头显引导四足机器人穿越物理迷宫，定位并拍摄墙上标记符号，比较摇杆与全向跑步机移动、摇杆与头部引导相机控制，并进行混合效应分析。

Result: 头部引导相机控制带来更快完成、更低撞墙成本、更低工作负荷，以及更好的临场感、可用性、技术接受度和心流；客观性能最佳为摇杆移动加头部引导相机，体验最佳折中为全向跑步机加头部引导相机；头部引导还减少视角切换、增加云台视图依赖并形成更稳定的观察策略，符号捕获接近上限且晕动症低。

Conclusion: 视角控制是该导航加检查任务中更强的区分因素，而移动方式仍塑造客观性能与体验之间的权衡。

Abstract: In many robot telepresence interfaces, movement and viewing are managed through handheld inputs. This can divide attention between steering, looking, and inspection. We compared joystick versus omnidirectional treadmill locomotion and joystick-based versus head-guided camera control in a within-subject 2x2 study in which 17 participants used a virtual reality headset to guide a quadruped robot through a physical maze while locating marked wall symbols and capturing them as visual evidence. Mixed-effects analyses showed that head-guided camera control was associated with faster completion, lower wall-contact cost, lower workload, and more favorable presence, usability, technology acceptance, and flow than joystick-based camera control. The strongest overall objective performance was observed in the joystick locomotion plus head-guided camera condition, whereas the omnidirectional treadmill plus head-guided camera condition emerged as the strongest experiential compromise. Exploratory camera analyses showed that head-guided camera control reduced switching, increased reliance on the pan-tilt view, and supported a more stable viewing strategy across the maze. These strategy differences were consistent with the performance advantage, especially for wall-contact duration. Targets captured remained near ceiling and simulator sickness remained low across all conditions. Overall, the results indicate that viewpoint control was the stronger differentiator in this combined navigation and inspection task, while locomotion still shaped the objective and experiential tradeoff.

</details>


### [46] [Emo-H2A: Driving a Facial Robot Directly from LLM Hidden States at Token Rate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23932)
*Yige Liu,Jingcheng Huang,Hao Sun,Min Li,Zhouping Yin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Emo-H2A范式，直接从LLM隐藏状态驱动面部机器人，无需情感标签，实现连续、上下文敏感且低延迟的表情生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型赋予人形机器人流畅语音，但面部表情仍难以跟随对话，存在表情与语音不同步的差距。

Method: Emo-H2A范式在自回归解码过程中从LLM隐藏状态提取效价-唤醒-支配（VAD）坐标并投影，以token粒度驱动机器人面部；实例化为免训练方法EmoManifold，驱动29自由度面部机器人。

Result: 在六个语言模型上验证读出；87.5%的回复内效价转换中表情方向匹配，而整段回复单一标签的方向跟踪接近50%随机水平；首次动作仅需0.96秒，而非等待回复结束和回合级标签的10-26秒。

Conclusion: 直接从LLM隐藏状态驱动面部表情可实现连续、上下文敏感且低延迟的人机交互表情同步。

Abstract: Diverse, semantically synchronized, and low-latency facial expressions are essential for human-robot interaction. While large language models (LLMs) have endowed anthropomorphic robots with fluent speech, facial expression still struggles to follow the conversation. Motivated by this gap, we present Emo-H2A (hidden-to-actuation), a paradigm that drives a facial robot directly from LLM hidden states during autoregressive decoding. Without a separate emotion label, expression stays continuous and context-sensitive, and the face moves with the unfolding reply. We instantiate this paradigm as EmoManifold, a training-free method that extracts a valence-arousal-dominance (VAD) coordinate from each hidden state, projects it, and drives the face at token granularity. The readout is checked on six language models, and the same projection drives our 29-degree-of-freedom (DoF) facial robot. When driven by this projection, the face shifts its expression in the matching direction on 87.5% of within-reply valence transitions, whereas holding a single label for the whole reply leaves directional tracking near the 50% chance level, and first moves in 0.96s rather than waiting 10-26s for the reply to finish and a turn-level label to return.

</details>


### [47] [GTI: Grounding Task Intent Throughout Long-Horizon VLA Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23926)
*Tianqi Gong,Haochen Niu,Yusheng Zhao,Lantao Zhang,Haoyu Liu,Rendong Ying,Peilin Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出GTI框架，通过持续定位当前子任务及其执行阶段，并生成阶段相关的视觉目标来增强VLA模型的长程操作能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型生成局部动作时需保持全局任务意图与当前可执行上下文一致，但长程操作中意图易漂移，缺乏对当前子任务阶段的显式定位。

Method: GTI利用当前观测和执行历史定位活动子任务及其执行阶段；对同一子任务生成交互/接触状态与局部完成状态两个阶段相关视觉目标，与局部语言指令共同条件化VLA；执行中通过异常监控与回滚、重新观测、阶段重评估实现局部恢复。

Result: 在RoboCasa365上GR00T成功率的ID/OOD从14.6%/12.1%提升至31.8%/20.4%；VLA-Arena上GTI+π0.5总体成功率47.6% vs ForeAct 45.2%，L1/L2任务提升5.2/6.0个百分点；三个真实机器人任务平均全任务成功率从21.7%提升至59.2%。

Conclusion: 持续语义意图定位与阶段条件视觉接地可改善多阶段执行，失败触发的局部恢复为部署提供额外支持。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models generate local actions from visual observations, language, and robot state, but long-horizon manipulation requires the global task intent to remain grounded in the currently executable context. We propose GTI (Grounding Task Intent), which uses the current observation and execution history to localize the active subtask and its execution phase. For the same local subtask, GTI generates two phase-dependent visual targets: an interaction/contact state and a local completion state. The local language instruction and the corresponding phase-specific visual target jointly condition the VLA, specifying both what to do now and what state should be reached at the current phase. During execution, anomaly monitoring and local recovery re-establish an intent-consistent execution context through rollback, re-observation, and phase re-evaluation after persistent deviation. We evaluate GTI on RoboCasa365, VLA-Arena Horizon-Long, and real-world PiPER tasks. Relative to matched Global-FT baselines, GR00T success on seen composite RoboCasa365 tasks increases from 14.6%/12.1% to 31.8%/20.4% under ID/OOD evaluation. On VLA-Arena, GTI+$\pi_{0.5}$ reaches 47.6% overall success versus 45.2% for an adapted ForeAct baseline, with gains of 5.2/6.0 percentage points on the harder L1/L2 tasks. In the three real-robot task experiments, mean full-task success increases from 21.7% to 59.2%. The results indicate that persistent semantic intent localization and phase-conditioned visual grounding improve multi-stage execution, while failure-triggered local recovery provides additional deployment-time support.

</details>


### [48] [ReLoco: Unified Humanoid Locomotion and Fall Recovery via Behavior-Conditioned Motion Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23916)
*Lequn Fu,Yufeng Hu,Yijun Zhong,Jie Zhang,Xiao Li,Lingxiang Ji,Zhiyuan Xu,Jian Tang,Shiqi Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ReLoco是一个使用行为条件对抗运动先验的框架，用一个本体感觉策略同时学习人形机器人的行走、从任意跌倒姿势起身以及恢复指令运动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人需要从跌倒中恢复并继续执行指令运动以维持移动能力，而单一策略学习这些行为需要同时监督直立运动和地面接触恢复过程。

Method: 提出ReLoco框架：利用机器人姿态选择专家池并条件化一个具有两个行为特定输出的共享判别器，实现行为条件对抗运动先验；通过任务奖励和共享正则化支持指令跟踪与恢复，临时辅助促进探索；采用基于仿真的精炼阶段提供机器人执行的运动参考，并进行消融实验考察条件监督、输出分离和参考质量。

Result: 在相同训练迭代下，完整策略实现96.80%的独立起身成功率和96.96%的推倒恢复成功率（含指令恢复）；躯干平面速度均方根误差为0.1266 m/s，比条件单输出变体低14.7%，关节角差异（动态时间规整度量）相近；在64.5 kg的天宫3.0人形机器人上部署，使用单一本体感觉actor实现行走、跑步、起身及跌倒后运动恢复，无需控制器切换或机器人上的策略微调。

Conclusion: ReLoco通过行为条件对抗运动先验和仿真参考精炼，成功在单一本体感觉策略中统一了行走、起身和跌倒后指令恢复，并在真实人形机器人上验证了有效性。

Abstract: Humanoid robots must recover from falls and resume commanded motion to sustain locomotion. Learning these behaviors in one policy requires supervision that accommodates both upright movement and ground-contact recovery. We present ReLoco, a framework that uses behavior-conditioned adversarial motion priors to learn locomotion, get-up from arbitrary fallen postures, and command resumption within one proprioceptive policy. During training, robot posture selects the expert pool and conditions a shared discriminator with two behavior-specific outputs. Task rewards and shared regularization support command tracking and recovery, while temporary assistance facilitates exploration. A simulation-based refinement stage supplies robot-executed motion references for this supervision. Ablations examine conditional supervision, output separation, and reference quality under matched settings. At a common training iteration, the full policy achieves $96.80\%$ independent get-up success and $96.96\%$ push-recovery success, including command resumption. Its torso planar velocity root-mean-square error is $0.1266$~m/s, $14.7\%$ lower than the conditional single-output variant, with similar joint-angle discrepancy measured by dynamic time warping. Deployment on the 64.5-kg Tiangong~3.0 humanoid demonstrates walking, running, get-up, and locomotion resumption after falls using one proprioceptive actor, without controller switching or on-robot policy fine-tuning.

</details>


### [49] [Scalable Iterative Multi-Agent Path Finding under Turn Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23911)
*Zheyu Tao,Chunlong Yu,Yicheng Hu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PIBT*_T 是一种针对带转向动作的多智能体路径规划（MAPF_T）的高效迭代求解器，在保留 PIBT 轻量单步规划机制的同时处理转向依赖、局部死锁、活锁与低效重复推挤，实现高成功率与强可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模多移动机器人系统需要在真实运动约束下快速可靠地规划路径；MAPF_T 显式建模朝向变化与转向延迟，但会加重搜索方法的计算负担，而现有基于优先级继承的可扩展方法难以直接扩展到 MAPF_T，因为转向动作破坏了传统优先级继承所依赖的节点立即腾空假设。

Method: 提出 PIBT*_T，在保留 Priority Inheritance with Backtracking（PIBT）轻量单时间步规划机制的基础上，引入专门机制来处理转向引发的依赖关系、局部死锁、活锁以及低效的重复推挤，形成面向 MAPF_T 的高效迭代求解器。

Result: 在标准 MAPF 基准上取得高成功率与强可扩展性，可在数秒内求解多达 1000 个智能体的实例；基于动作依赖图（ADG）的执行仿真与柔性制造系统案例表明，转向感知规划更能反映机器人实际执行并降低实际完工时间；TurtleBot4 机器人实验进一步验证了方法的实际可行性。

Conclusion: PIBT*_T 能够在真实运动约束下实现可扩展且高效的 MAPF_T 求解，相比经典 MAPF 规划更贴合机器人执行过程并缩短实际完工时间，具备工程与实机可行性。

Abstract: Large-scale multi-mobile-robot systems require fast and reliable path planning under realistic motion constraints. Multi-Agent Path Finding with turn actions (MAPF$_T$) explicitly models orientation changes and turn-induced delays, but these constraints increase the computational burden of search-based methods. Priority-inheritance-based planning offers a highly scalable alternative, yet directly extending it to MAPF$_T$ is nontrivial because turn actions break the immediate node-vacation assumption underlying conventional priority inheritance. To address this challenge, this paper proposes PIBT$^*_T$, an efficient iterative solver for MAPF$_T$. PIBT$^*_T$ advances scalable multi-agent coordination by preserving the lightweight single-timestep planning mechanism of Priority Inheritance with Backtracking (PIBT), while introducing dedicated mechanisms to handle turn-induced dependencies, local deadlocks, livelock, and inefficient repeated pushing. Experiments on standard MAPF benchmarks show that PIBT$^*_T$ achieves high success rates and strong scalability, solving instances with up to 1000 agents within seconds. For realistic engineering environments, Action Dependency Graph (ADG)-based execution simulation and a flexible manufacturing system case study show that turn-aware planning better reflects robot execution and reduces actual makespan compared with classical MAPF planning. Experiments with TurtleBot4 robots further validate the practical feasibility of the proposed method.

</details>


### [50] [Navigating Robots from Unknown and Uncalibrated Exocentric Cameras via Map Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23894)
*Raul Steinmetz,Raphael Druon,Yusuke Yoshiyasu,Akihisa Ohya,Ayanori Yorozu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出ROMUX，利用未标定且未知的外置摄像头生成机器人为中心的障碍地图，无需相机内外参即可实现导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 外置摄像头视野广、遮挡少，但现有方法需预先标定相机位姿和内参，耗时且需专业人员，相机移动或换房间后须重新标定。

Method: 在大规模仿真数据上训练跨视角Transformer，从外置图像预测机器人为中心的度量障碍地图，不依赖相机信息；再用PPO训练小型策略，基于预测地图朝目标坐标导航。

Result: 在每回合随机化相机的仿真基准中，成功率与机器人搭载相机的PPO策略相当；支持单相机或多相机部分视图，并零样本迁移到真实机器人，适应不同位置、朝向和房间布局。

Conclusion: ROMUX无需标定和预部署微调，即可用未知外置相机实现鲁棒的机器人导航。

Abstract: Exocentric cameras, mounted on walls or ceilings in indoor environments, offer wider vantage points and are less easily occluded than robot-mounted cameras, making them an appealing alternative for perception in robot navigation algorithms. Previous methods that use such cameras, however, require their pose and intrinsics to be known before deployment, demanding calibration that is time-consuming, requires specialized personnel, and must be repeated whenever a camera moves or a new room is navigated. We propose Robot-centric Obstacle Maps from Unknown and uncalibrated eXocentric cameras (ROMUX), an agent that navigates a robot toward point goals while avoiding obstacles using exocentric cameras that are both uncalibrated, meaning the system does not require their pose or intrinsic information as input, and unknown, meaning it is not fit to a specific camera during training and does not require pre-deployment fine-tuning. We train a cross-view transformer on large-scale simulation data to predict metric robot-centric obstacle maps from exocentric images, without conditioning image tokens on camera information. A small policy trained with Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) then navigates on the predicted maps toward a goal coordinate relative to the robot. We benchmark our agent in simulation with cameras randomized per episode, and it matches the success rates of a PPO policy that navigates from a robot-mounted camera. Further, ROMUX works with one camera or multiple cameras with partial views and has been transferred zero-shot to a real robot using a camera at varying positions and orientations across various room layouts.

</details>


### [51] [Estimation-Aware Reachability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23830)
*Himanshu Paudel,Javier Borquez*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文将Hamilton-Jacobi可达性扩展到机器人信念、估计与不确定性，把意外测量视为有界对抗，并用当前不确定性放大不安全集，从而以指定概率保证认证状态安全。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Hamilton-Jacobi可达性假设状态精确已知，但真实机器人依赖带噪声且随运动演化的状态估计，因此需要把不确定性纳入可达性分析。

Method: 扩展Hamilton-Jacobi可达性到信念空间：将意外测量建模为有界对抗，按当前不确定性扩大不安全集，并与现有求解器兼容。

Result: 仿真中所得安全过滤器几乎消除了确定性等价HJ的碰撞，且比同等安全的确定性不确定性填充基线更不保守。

Conclusion: 该方法能在概率意义下为带噪声估计的机器人提供安全认证，并保持与现有求解器的兼容性。

Abstract: Hamilton-Jacobi Reachability computes the states from which a system can be kept safe under worst-case disturbances, but it assumes the state is known exactly, whereas real robots act on noisy estimates whose uncertainty evolves with their motion. We extend Hamilton-Jacobi Reachability to the robot's belief, estimate and uncertainty, treating unexpected measurements as a bounded adversary and enlarging the unsafe set by the current uncertainty, so that certified states are safe with a prescribed probability. The formulation is compatible with existing solvers. In simulation, the resulting safety filters nearly eliminate the collisions of certaintyequivalent HJ and are less conservative than equally safe uncertainty-padding baselines. The implementation code is available at this https URL

</details>


### [52] [RoboDx: Repair and Diagnosis of Faults in Robot Software](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23816)
*Soham Dan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboDx生成带有已知注入故障的机器人导航软件修复任务，通过隐藏验证器检验修复效果，发现大多数通过验证的修复并未真正移除所有注入故障，且模型难以识别静默故障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单元测试无法保证机器人导航软件修复后行为正确，且修复通过验证并不代表找出了所有错误，需要能揭示隐藏故障的修复评测任务。

Method: 构造RoboDx，为模拟机器人导航软件生成带已知注入故障的修复任务；采用隐藏验证器仅在隐藏场景下机器人行驶正确才标记修复通过；设置第二赛道隐藏代码要求从假设列表中选择原因；构建含单故障和双故障（含静默故障）的100个任务RoboDx-Hard-100，评测Claude Opus 5.5等模型，并给出真实bug修复PR的97个任务。

Result: Opus的修复几乎全部通过标准工具验证；在分析的94个任务（44个单故障、50个双故障）中，50个双故障任务里49次验证通过的修复仅17次移除了两处注入编辑，遗留的多为文档未给出正确值的配置常量；看不到代码时Opus在10/50任务中同时命名两个故障；提供各故障机器人测量后，四个模型最多在38/94任务中命名正确原因，若同时给出所有假设的模拟器输出则几乎全对，而模型自身预测的输出无增益；真实PR的97个任务上Claude Haiku 5.5得分最低，其余三个模型无明显排序。

Conclusion: 通过隐藏行为验证的修复常常遗漏静默故障，尤其是文档缺失的配置常量；模型仅凭测量难以定位根因，需依赖模拟器输出，说明现有修复评测与方法存在盲区。

Abstract: A patch to a robot's navigation software can pass every unit test and still drive the robot off its path. Checking how the repaired robot drives catches such patches. It does not show that the agent found everything that was wrong. We introduce RoboDx, a generator of repair tasks for simulated robot navigation software with known injected faults. A hidden verifier marks a repair as verified only if the robot then drives correctly in hidden scenarios, which the agent never sees. A second track hides the code and asks the agent to pick the cause from a list of hypotheses. A task with two faults can contain a quiet fault, a fault that makes no hidden scenario fail unless the other fault is also present. On our set of 100 tasks, RoboDx-Hard-100, almost every repair by Claude Opus 5.5 with standard coding tools is verified. We analyse 94 of the tasks, 44 with one fault and 50 with two. On the 50 two-fault tasks, only 17 of Opus's 49 verified repairs remove both injected edits. Most faults left behind are configuration constants whose correct value the task's documentation does not state. Opus names both faults on 10/50 of these tasks when it cannot see the code. We then give four current models the same measurements of each faulty robot, picked so that, in the simulator, only the true cause produces all of them. The models name the cause on at most 38 of 94 tasks. They are right on almost all when they also get the simulator's output for every hypothesis. Their own predictions of that output give no detectable gain. On 97 tasks from real bug-fix pull requests, Claude Haiku 5.5, the weakest model on the generated tasks, scores lowest, and the other three models are not ordered.

</details>


### [53] [TacPilot: Does Touch Need to Be Everywhere?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23804)
*Yunzhen Zhang,Yuxiao Zhu,Jie Yang,Yufeng Miao,Bing Luo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TacPilot 是一个触觉残差框架，通过分离触觉表征更新与动作干预，在冻结的视觉策略上实现触觉反馈的高效利用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 触觉反馈有助于视觉操作策略响应局部接触，但触觉观测变化并不总需要纠正动作，持续纠正也不总需要新的触觉特征，需要将触觉感知与动作纠正解耦以适应不同的时空需求。

Method: 围绕冻结的视觉策略，使用感知门控决定何时刷新触觉特征，仅更新受影响区域并复用未变特征；使用独立的干预门控决定是否有界笛卡尔和夹爪残差修改标称视觉动作。

Result: 在离线专家轨迹回放中，局部特征复用使编码区域相比周期性全编码减少56.4%，动作预测基本不变；探索性仿真中两种圆柱尺寸下30次试验成功26次（86.7%）；在配备Paxini触觉传感器的OpenArm v2上跨四个接触丰富操作任务验证。

Conclusion: TacPilot 通过分离触觉感知更新与动作干预，有效减少触觉编码计算并保持动作性能，在仿真和真实机器人上均验证了可行性。

Abstract: Tactile feedback helps visual manipulation policies respond to local contact, but changes in tactile observations do not always require corrective motion, and sustained corrections do not always require new tactile features. We introduce TacPilot, a tactile residual framework that separates representation updates from action intervention around a frozen visual policy. TacPilot uses a perception gate to determine when tactile features need refreshing and updates only affected regions while reusing unchanged features. A separate intervention gate determines whether bounded Cartesian and gripper residuals modify the nominal visual action. This separation allows tactile perception and action correction to follow different spatial and temporal demands during manipulation. In offline expert-trajectory replay, local feature reuse reduces encoded regions by 56.4% relative to periodic full encoding, with essentially unchanged action predictions. In an exploratory simulation evaluation spanning two cylinder sizes, TacPilot succeeds in 26 of 30 trials (86.7%). We further validate TacPilot on OpenArm v2 equipped with Paxini tactile sensors across four contact-rich manipulation tasks.

</details>


### [54] [CoT-WAM: Collaborative Training of Vision-Language Models and World-Action Models via Plan Latents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23803)
*Jinhao Zhang,Yushen Zuo,Ke Rui,Weitao Zhou,Haibao Yu,Honglong Tian,Xiaoheng Ma,Jie Mei,Jiawei Wang,Minglei Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CoT-WAM通过可学习嵌入将视觉语言模型（VLM）与视觉-动作世界模型（WAM）联合训练，注入语义先验，在控制、零样本泛化和长程规划六项基准上取得SOTA，并完成真实部署验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用机器人操作既需语义能力（开放场景理解、语义推理、长程规划），又需物理预见能力。现有WAM虽能联合建模视觉预见与动作分布，但语义能力不足；VLM虽提供语义先验，却难以有效融入WAM的预测与控制。

Method: 提出CoT-WAM，联合训练的WAM-VLM框架：用可学习嵌入在VLM与WAM间交换信息，训练时以文本与目标图像信号监督以编码意图；并设计两阶段训练策略，实现联合优化与更有效协作。

Result: 在覆盖控制性能、零样本泛化、长程规划的六个基准上评估：LIBERO、CALVIN、RoboCasa-Kitchen达SOTA；LIBERO-Plus与LIBERO-Pro上无需微调即展现更强零样本泛化；RoboCasa365上长程规划优于有无外部规划器的基线；真实实验验证有效性。

Conclusion: 将VLM语义先验通过可学习嵌入与两阶段联合训练注入WAM，可同时提升控制性能、零样本泛化与长程规划，并在真实部署中有效。

Abstract: General robot manipulation requires both semantic competence and physical foresight. World action models (WAMs) acquire an understanding of and predictive capability over the physical world by jointly modeling visual foresight and action distributions. However, existing WAMs still lack semantic competence: they have limited ability to perform open-world scene understanding, semantic reasoning, and long-horizon planning. Although vision-language models (VLMs) provide these capabilities, effectively integrating their semantic priors into WAM prediction and control remains challenging. To address this challenge, we propose CoT-WAM, a jointly trained WAM-VLM framework. CoT-WAM uses a learnable embedding to exchange information between the VLM and the WAM, which is supervised by textual and goal-image signals during training to encode rich intent information. A tailored two-stage training strategy enables joint optimization and more effective collaboration between the VLM and WAM. We evaluate CoT-WAM on six benchmarks covering control performance, zero-shot generalization, and long-horizon planning. CoT-WAM achieves SOTA performance on LIBERO, CALVIN, and RoboCasa-Kitchen, and demonstrates stronger zero-shot generalization on LIBERO-Plus and LIBERO-Pro without fine-tuning. On RoboCasa365, it further improves long-horizon planning over baselines with and without external planners. Real-world experiments further demonstrate the effectiveness of our method in practical deployments.

</details>


### [55] [Improve How You Improve: Recursive Policy Self-Improvement for Zero-Shot Open-Vocabulary Multi-Object Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23774)
*Yang Li,Ziju Han,Aming WU,Zihao Zhang,Sijia Zhang,Yahong Han,Qinghua Hu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出RPSI-Nav，一种递归策略自改进框架，同时修订导航程序和修订机制本身，用于零样本开放词汇多目标导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有零样本多目标导航方法依赖人工设计且固定不变的诊断与修订机制，导致系统自我改进能力受限，无法随导航策略演进而持续提升。

Method: RPSI-Nav通过诊断搜索行为、在现有观测无法区分竞争原因时请求诊断视图、生成针对性导航修订；将后续目标搜索的反馈与产生修订的规则关联，指导诊断、观测获取和修订规划的更新；更新后的改进器治理后续导航改进，形成递归自改进循环；机器人跨目标条件性继承已接受的程序更新与空间记忆。

Result: 基准实验与定性真机评估验证了RPSI-Nav的有效性。

Conclusion: 递归地自改进导航程序及其改进机制，可突破固定人工修订机制的限制，并借助搜索经验迁移提升未来导航与适应能力。

Abstract: To advance the development of embodied general navigation, zero-shot open-vocabulary multi-object navigation has attracted increasing attention, aiming to enable robots to efficiently search multiple objects in unseen environments without training. For this task, the key lies in improving navigation policies from earlier searches to locate subsequent goals more efficiently. Recent methods automate this process through trajectory reflection and rule revision, enabling policy self-improvement. However, the mechanisms for diagnosing problems and generating revisions are typically designed by humans and remain fixed across iterations. Consequently, the system's capacity for continued self-improvement remains constrained, even as its navigation policy evolves. To this end, we propose RPSI-Nav, a Recursive Policy Self-Improvement framework for zero-shot multi-object navigation that revises both the navigation program and its revision mechanism. RPSI-Nav diagnoses search behavior, requests diagnostic views when existing observations cannot distinguish competing causes, and generates targeted navigation revisions. Feedback from subsequent goal searches is linked to the rules that produced these revisions, guiding changes to diagnosis, observation acquisition, and revision planning. The updated improver governs subsequent navigation improvements, forming a recursive loop in which RPSI-Nav revises its own improvement process. Across goals, the robot conditionally inherits accepted program updates and spatial memory, enabling search experience to improve both future navigation and adaptation. Benchmark experiments and qualitative real-robot evaluations validate RPSI-Nav's effectiveness.

</details>


### [56] [ZERITH V2.5: From Predictive Power to Policy Learnability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23746)
*Chenxi Han,Ruby,Epoch,Orion,Luke,Dusk,Lyra,Alvin,Hubert,Alex,Jiahe Han,Tao Zhang,Hang Liu,Shoujie Li,Yizhe Li,Bokui Chen,Houde Liu,Guoping Pan,Yi Cheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ZERITH V2.5是一种视频-动作模型，其动作策略读取视频生成模型的中间特征；研究发现，预测性训练中噪声水平的监督方式显著影响后续动作学习，多噪声水平适应带来大幅增益而仅部署噪声水平适应无效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类在学习操作陌生物体时，往往先能预测其状态变化，后才能真正熟练控制；这种预测与控制之间的差距促使作者研究如何有效利用视频生成模型获得的先验来提升动作策略。

Method: 提出ZERITH V2.5视频-动作模型，动作策略读取视频生成模型的中间特征；在固定演示和固定总更新次数下，比较不同视频预测训练监督方式对后续动作学习的影响，并进行权重迁移实验和初始损失/梯度测量。

Result: 多噪声水平适应带来大幅增益，而仅在部署噪声水平适应则无增益；增益在适应权重保留于视频生成模型时持续存在，而仅用于初始化动作策略时消失；目标域预测训练使视频模型获得更好的起始状态，在匹配数据和训练预算下预测适应改善控制，并在LIBERO、LIBERO-Plus、Meta-World MT50和真实机器人任务上评估完整策略。

Conclusion: 视频预测训练的监督方式影响后续动作学习；预测适应在匹配数据和训练预算下能提升控制性能，其增益源于适应权重留在视频生成模型中而非仅用于初始化动作策略。

Abstract: When humans learn to manipulate an unfamiliar object, they may already be able to anticipate how its state will change while still being unable to control it skillfully. This gap between prediction and control motivates us to study how the priors acquired by video generation models can be used effectively by an action policy. We present ZERITH V2.5, a video-action model (VAM) in which the action policy reads intermediate features of a video generation model, and use it to study, under fixed demonstrations and a fixed total number of updates, what kind of predictive training improves subsequent action learning. We find that the way video predictive training is supervised affects subsequent action learning. In our experiments, adapting at many noise levels yields a large gain, while adapting only at the noise level used at deployment yields none. Weight-transfer experiments further show that the gain stays with the adapted weights when they remain in the video generation model and disappears when they are used only to initialize the action policy. Initial loss and gradient measurements at the start of joint training indicate that target-domain predictive training gives the video model a better starting state, making subsequent joint learning of prediction and action easier. Under matched data and training budgets, predictive adaptation improves control. We evaluate the complete policy on LIBERO, LIBERO-Plus, Meta-World MT50, and real-robot tasks.

</details>


### [57] [RecallFlow: Episodic Memory as the Source Distribution of Generative Robot Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23731)
*Xuji Zhang,Qilin Zhong,Zichen Zhang,Wei Zhai,Jian Di,Kun Li,Yang Cao,Wenjun Lv*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RecallFlow 是一种基于情景记忆构建源分布的流策略框架，通过回忆重定位与回忆锚点将生成起点从高斯噪声改为接近目标动作的记忆引导分布，从而提升记忆依赖型操作任务的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散/流匹配策略在记忆依赖型操作中，将历史观测作为额外条件输入，但生成仍从高斯噪声开始；随着记忆负载增长，策略需从冗长冗余的历史中挑选相关目标，导致虚假关联和不可靠决策，且初始噪声不携带已见信息，使生成缺乏朝向记忆目标的引导。

Method: RecallFlow 包含两个核心组件：回忆重定位（Recall-Relocalization）通过少量回忆槽将记忆与当前视图关联，使源分布反映记忆目标当前所在位置；回忆锚点（Recall Anchors）将每个槽转化为源分布的高斯分量，使生成从接近意图动作处开始而非从噪声开始；传输网络仅基于当前观测完成动作生成，推理成本接近无记忆策略。作者还构建了按记忆负载分组的记忆依赖任务套件，并在匹配协议下比较所有策略。

Result: 在公开 MIKASA-Robo 记忆基准上平均成功率达 80.4%，为当前最优；在自建任务套件上比最强基线高 20.3 个百分点，在四个真实机器人任务上高 18.8 个百分点。

Conclusion: 以情景记忆构建流策略的源分布、并用回忆锚点引导生成起点，能有效缓解记忆负载增长带来的虚假关联与无引导生成问题，在仿真与真实机器人记忆依赖任务上均显著优于现有基线。

Abstract: Diffusion and flow-matching policies have become a standard choice for visuomotor imitation learning, owing to their ability to model multimodal action distributions. In memory-dependent manipulation, the robot must act on information that is no longer visible. Existing methods feed the observation history to the policy as an additional condition, while action generation still starts from Gaussian noise. However, the policy must then pick out the relevant target from a long and redundant history, leading to spurious correlations and unreliable decisions as the memory load grows. Additionally, the initial noise carries no information about what the robot has seen, leaving generation without guidance toward the remembered target. To address these issues, we propose RecallFlow, a flow-policy framework that builds the source distribution from episodic memory. Recall-Relocalization relates the memory to the current view through a few recall slots, so that the source reflects where the remembered targets are now. Recall Anchors then turns each slot into a Gaussian component of the source, enabling generation to start close to the intended action instead of from noise. The transport network then completes the action from the current observation alone, keeping inference nearly as cheap as a memoryless policy. We further build a suite of memory-dependent tasks grouped by memory load and compare all policies under a matched protocol. Extensive experiments show that RecallFlow achieves a state-of-the-art average success rate of 80.4% on the public MIKASA-Robo memory benchmark, and outperforms the strongest baseline by 20.3 points on our suite and by 18.8 points on four real-robot tasks.

</details>


### [58] [Improving While Deployed: Learning Proactive End-to-End Driving from Imperfect Human Takeover Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23719)
*Yangang Ren,Di Zeng,Jun Li,Zirui Li,Chengjiang Zhang,Xiaoqing Ye,Ji Tao,Chen Lv*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种离线后训练框架，将不完美的接管数据转化为主动监督，用于更新已部署的端到端驾驶模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已部署的端到端驾驶模型在遇到困难场景时会产生包含模型输出和人类行为的人类接管数据，但人类干预往往不完美（干预过晚或不必要），直接模仿会强化延迟响应或抑制原本合理的操作。

Method: 构建多样化的轨迹候选：增强已部署模型的规划，并将记录的人类纠正操作在时间上提前；训练场景条件轨迹评分器，利用专家演示和接管日志中的非配对偏好信号对候选进行排序；在接管前场景中用得分更高的候选选择性替换训练目标，正常驾驶场景保留原目标；最后将目标蒸馏进驾驶模型且不改变推理架构。

Result: 在基于4D高斯泼溅重建的真实场景闭环评估中，该方法取得最高总体成功率，优于已部署模型、直接模仿学习和轨迹后优化。

Conclusion: 该工作为利用现有车队日志高效更新已部署驾驶模型提供了一条实用路径。

Abstract: Once deployed on vehicles, end-to-end driving models continuously encounter challenging scenarios, generating valuable human takeover data that capture both model outputs and human behaviors. However, human interventions are often imperfect, as drivers may intervene too late or unnecessarily. Consequently, direct imitation can reinforce late responses or discourage otherwise appropriate maneuvers. To address this challenge, we propose an offline post-training framework that transforms imperfect takeover data into proactive supervision. We first construct diverse trajectory candidates by augmenting the plans of the deployed model and shifting recorded human corrective maneuvers earlier in time. We then train a scene-conditioned trajectory scorer to rank these candidates using expert demonstrations and unpaired preference signals from takeover logs. Using this scorer, we selectively replace pre-takeover training targets with higher-scoring candidates while retaining the original targets in normal driving scenarios. Finally, we distill the resulting targets into the driving model without changing its inference architecture. In closed-loop evaluations using real-world scenarios reconstructed with 4D Gaussian splatting, our method achieves the highest overall success rate, outperforming the deployed model, direct imitation learning and trajectory post-optimization. These results highlight a practical path toward efficient updates of deployed driving models using existing fleet logs.

</details>


### [59] [ExecGap: Exploiting Command-Execution Gap for Patch Attacks on Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23714)
*Keke Tang,Guolin Qiu,Tianyu Hao,Daizong Liu,Yikai Zhang,Peican Zhu,Zhihong Tian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ExecGap是一种对抗性补丁攻击框架，利用VLA模型预测指令与下游执行之间的差距，通过偏置指令至低可执行性方向来降低机器人任务完成能力，仿真与真机实验均验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对VLA控制机器人的对抗攻击主要优化预测层面或rollout层面的偏差，但忽略了VLA预测的指令需经下游控制与机器人本体才能执行的事实，导致攻击未充分利用指令-执行差距带来的执行漏洞。

Method: 提出ExecGap对抗补丁攻击框架，基于运动学低效性和动态弱驱动估计低可执行性方向，将VLA预测的任务空间指令偏置至这些方向，诱导执行退化，使预测指令难以有效实现。

Result: 多VLA策略仿真实验表明，ExecGap比现有补丁攻击产生更大的指令-执行不匹配和更弱的实际执行效果；真机实验验证部署的补丁可在物理平台上诱导执行退化。

Conclusion: ExecGap揭示了VLA控制机器人中指令-执行差距带来的本体特定执行脆弱性，证明考虑下游执行环节的对抗攻击能更有效破坏任务完成，对VLA机器人实际部署的鲁棒性提出警示。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have become a powerful paradigm for robotic manipulation and show strong promise for real-world deployment, making adversarial robustness critical for reliable robot operation. Existing adversarial attacks on VLA-controlled robots mainly optimize prediction-level discrepancies or rollout-level deviations to disrupt manipulation. However, they rarely account for the fact that VLA-predicted commands are not executed directly, but must be realized through downstream control and robot embodiment. In this paper, we propose ExecGap, an adversarial patch attack framework that exploits this command-execution gap to expose embodiment-specific execution vulnerabilities. Specifically, ExecGap biases VLA-predicted task-space commands toward low-executability directions, which are estimated from kinematic inefficiency and dynamic weak actuation. This induces execution degradation, where the predicted command becomes difficult to realize effectively, thereby impairing task completion. Extensive simulation experiments across multiple VLA policies show that ExecGap induces larger command-execution mismatch and weaker realized execution than existing patch attacks. Real-robot experiments further validate that the deployed patch can induce execution degradation on physical platforms.

</details>


### [60] [Radar-Native Control Barrier Functions: Doppler Range Rate as a Measured Barrier Derivative](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23654)
*Johannes Mootz,Hesam Mojtahedi,Reza Akhavian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出直接利用FMCW雷达多普勒测得的径向相对速度作为以机器人为中心的距离控制屏障函数（CBF）的导数，得到前向速度的闭式安全滤波器，并证明其相比基于测距重建距离变化率的滤波器能显著减少安全缓冲侵入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态障碍物附近的安全控制需要距离和相对运动信息；传统基于测距的滤波器需从连续距离测量或障碍物状态估计中重建距离变化率，在障碍物运动变化时会引入估计延迟或对测距噪声敏感。

Method: 利用FMCW雷达多普勒直接测量径向相对速度，将其作为以机器人为中心距离CBF的导数，构造机器人前向速度的闭式滤波器；由传感器误差界（距离、多普勒、自身速度和方位）构建测量裕度；在平台后退能力内的508次配对遭遇中，与恒加速度和恒速度卡尔曼滤波器对比缓冲侵入次数，并在Unitree Go1上验证停止距离和动态响应。

Result: 508次配对遭遇中，直接多普勒产生2次缓冲侵入，恒加速度卡尔曼滤波为15次，恒速度卡尔曼滤波为25次；在Unitree Go1上控制器在0.777±0.015 m处停止（预测值0.758 m），且能在更近的静态回波存在时对更远的接近物体做出反应。

Conclusion: 直接多普勒消除了安全回路中重建距离变化率的需求，减少了障碍物运动变化与其对安全指令影响之间的延迟；在测量误差有界且检测持续可用的条件下，该保证覆盖纵向距离通道。

Abstract: Safe control around dynamic obstacles requires information about both distance and relative motion. Range-based filters commonly reconstruct the required range rate from successive range measurements or from obstacle-state estimators, which can introduce estimator delay or sensitivity to range noise when obstacle motion changes. A frequency-modulated continuous-wave (FMCW) radar measures radial relative velocity directly through Doppler. We use this measurement as the derivative of an ego-centered range control barrier function (CBF) and obtain a closed-form filter on the robot's forward velocity. A measurement margin constructed from sensor error bounds accounts for errors in range, Doppler, ego velocity, and bearing. In 508 paired encounters within the platform's retreat capability, direct Doppler produces two buffer incursions, compared with 15 for a constant-acceleration and 25 for a constant-velocity Kalman filter. On a Unitree Go1, the controller stops at $0.777 \pm 0.015$ m against the predicted $0.758$ m and reacts to a farther approaching object while a nearer static return remains. The guarantee covers the longitudinal range channel under bounded measurement errors and continuously available detections. Direct Doppler removes the need to reconstruct range rate in the safety loop, reducing the delay between a change in obstacle motion and its effect on the safety command.

</details>


### [61] [LoRN: Low-Rank Noise Steering for Generative Robot Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23628)
*Wei Liu,Abrar Anwar,Huihua Zhao,Soha Pouya,Yan Chang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出LoRN方法，通过低秩噪声引导在不更新模型权重的情况下提升预训练生成式机器人策略性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估发现世界动作模型的动作对视频噪声比动作噪声更敏感，但现有噪声引导方法难以扩展到高维噪声输入的大策略类。

Method: LoRN是一种强化学习方法：小型引导actor通过固定低秩随机投影选择方向，并用范数控制构造使噪声保持在Gaussian样本的典型尺度附近，从而分离actor控制维度与完整噪声维度，可同时引导视频和动作输入。

Result: 在三个世界动作模型和十个模型-任务对上，视频引导相比基础策略平均成功率提升26.2个百分点，而DSRL动作引导仅提升7.1个百分点；对较小动作策略达到相当最终成功率并普遍改善早期训练成功率；模拟训练的引导actor无需额外真实训练即可迁移到三个双臂物理操作任务和两个基础策略。

Conclusion: LoRN成功将噪声引导扩展到世界动作模型，视频噪声引导显著优于动作噪声引导，且具备仿真到现实的迁移能力。

Abstract: Noise steering improves pretrained generative robot policies by learning to select their initial noise without updating model weights. We find that actions in the evaluated world-action models are more sensitive to video noise than action noise, making video noise an expressive input for steering. However, the noise-steering methods we evaluate, in their published forms, struggle to scale to the high-dimensional noise inputs of large policy classes such as world-action models. We introduce Low-Rank Noise Steering (LoRN), a reinforcement learning method that scales noise steering to world-action models. A small steering actor selects a direction through a fixed low-rank random projection, while our norm-controlled construction keeps the resulting noise near the typical scale of a Gaussian sample. This separates the actor's control dimension from the full noise dimension, enabling steering of both video and action inputs. Across three world-action models and ten model-task pairs, video steering improves average success by 26.2 percentage points over the base policy, compared with 7.1 points for DSRL action steering. For smaller action policies, the same construction achieves comparable final success while generally improving early-training success. Steering actors trained in simulation also transfer to three physical bimanual manipulation tasks across two base policies, without additional real-world training.

</details>


### [62] [Recoverability, Not Invariance: Certified Safety for Robots at the Limits of Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23589)
*Thomas A. Berrueta,Joshua D. Ibrahim,Gilchrist X. Johnson,John Lathrop,Soon-Jo Chung*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出可恢复性（recoverability）概念，通过认证机器人重新进入可信恢复模式的能力，而非部署行为的前向不变性，从而为激进、学习和实验性行为提供形式化安全保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器人安全认证依赖前向不变性，需要高保真模型，而模型在操作包线边缘最不可靠，导致许多期望行为（如学习策略、极限牵引下的激进机动）无法被认证。

Method: 引入基于收缩理论的安全概念——可恢复性；证明从认证管道内任意位置切换到恢复模式可保证指数收敛（即使存在扰动和模型误差）；推导收缩插入梯度以决定切换时机，并嵌入切换时间规划中（可行性保证安全，最优性仅影响性能）；开发序列凸合成方法，单调增长认证管道逼近精确可行核。

Result: 在模拟四旋翼穿越不确定障碍物中优于基于不变性的安全滤波器；在全尺寸IndyNXT自动驾驶赛车的硬件部署中，在雨天极限操控下从激进赛道线反复恢复车辆。

Conclusion: 可恢复性提供了一种认证的退出策略，无需部署行为本身具有不变性，即可将形式化安全保证扩展到激进、学习和实验性行为。

Abstract: Safety certification in robotics typically rests on forward invariance of the deployed controller, a property that demands high-fidelity models at the edge of the operating envelope, exactly where models are least reliable and robots perform best. As a result, many of the behaviors we most want robots to run, from learned policies to aggressive maneuvers at the limits of traction, cannot be certified at all. We introduce recoverability, a contraction-theoretic safety concept that resolves this tension by certifying a robot's ability to reengage a trusted recovery mode rather than the invariance of a deployed behavior. We prove that switching to the recovery mode from anywhere inside a certified tube guarantees exponential convergence, even under disturbances and model error. To decide when to switch, we derive the contraction insertion gradient, the first-order effect of a recovery insertion on the certificate, and embed it in a switching-time program in which feasibility alone guarantees safety and optimality only shapes performance. To supply the certificates, we develop sequential convex synthesis that monotonically grows the certified tube toward the exact viability kernel. We validate our approach in simulated quadrotor flight through uncertain obstacles, where it outperforms invariance-based safety filters, and in hardware deployments aboard a full-scale IndyNXT autonomous racecar, where it repeatedly recovers the car from an aggressive raceline at the limits of handling in the rain. Recoverability provides a certified exit strategy, extending formal safety guarantees to aggressive, learned, and experimental behaviors without requiring their invariance.

</details>


### [63] [SPICE: Stochastic Policy for Interaction in Cluttered Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23585)
*Nidhi Parayil,Benjamin Greenland,Thierry Peynot,Chris Lehnert*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SPICE是一种基于触觉条件扩散策略的机器人控制方法，用于在密集植物冠层中到达目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 农业任务如采摘和修剪需要机器人穿过密集枝叶，接触不可避免且视觉受阻；间歇性接触导致自由空间与接触丰富观测不平衡，难以学习接触响应行为。

Method: 提出SPICE，一种以触觉传感作为唯一环境反馈的触觉条件扩散策略，实现三种行为：穿过柔性枝叶、绕行部分阻挡茎干、操纵阻挡目标路径的枝条。

Result: 在180多次真实机器人试验中，SPICE枝条绕行成功率达95%（最强触觉基线45%），实现超越反应式控制器的枝条操纵，在视觉遮挡下仍有效（图像条件DP失败），在未见过的番茄和青柠植物上零样本迁移40次试验成功率达87.5%。

Conclusion: 触觉条件生成策略在视觉遮挡的植物环境中具有交互潜力。

Abstract: Agricultural tasks such as harvesting and pruning require robots to reach through dense foliage, where contact is often unavoidable and vision is obstructed. Intermittent contact creates an imbalance between free-space and contact-rich observations, complicating the learning of contact-responsive behaviours. We present SPICE, a tactileconditioned diffusion policy for reaching targets in dense plant canopies using tactile sensing as the sole environmental feedback. SPICE exhibits three behaviours: moving through compliant foliage, manoeuvring around partially obstructing stems, and manipulating branches that block the target path. To the best of our knowledge, SPICE is the first tactile-conditioned diffusion policy for target reaching through physically cluttered plant canopies. Across over 180 real-robot trials, SPICE was evaluated against tactile-conditioned, image-conditioned, and reactive-control baselines. Spatial tactile information and explicit contact conditioning proved important, with SPICE achieving 95% branch-manoeuvring success versus 45% for the strongest tactile baseline. SPICE also achieved branch manipulation beyond the reactive controller and remained effective under visual occlusion, where the image-conditioned DP failed. Zero-shot transfer across 40 trials on unseen tomato and lime plants achieved 87.5% success rate. These results demonstrate the potential of tactile-conditioned generative policies for interaction in visually occluded plant environments.

</details>


### [64] [LuxTac: A Flexible, Scalable, and Open-Source Optical Taxel Array with Distributed Three-Axis Force Sensing for Tactile Robot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23575)
*Md Rakibul Islam Prince,John Pelan,Zhiyuan Zhang,Adyaa Khera,Katherine Pesetski,Adeesh Desai,Akimizu Sugiyama,Minwoo Ki,Benjamin Alan Capelle,Temitope Ibrahim Amosa,Zijie Xu,Yihan Huang,Taehun Chung,Long Wang,Wei Li,Shaoting Lin,Yu She*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LuxTac是一种约3.5毫米厚、基于商用数字RGBC光-数转换器的柔性可扩展光学触觉阵列，通过可互换凝胶 cartridge 实现法向力与剪切力的单 taxel 分辨，法向线性度R²=0.999，剪切三轴力估计R²最高0.96，并提升机器人插孔任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低成本压阻式触觉阵列仅能感知压力，而可解析剪切力的相机式光学传感器体积过大、难以在夹爪上密集铺贴，因此需要一种薄型、可扩展、无相机的光学触觉阵列。

Method: 每个 taxel 由半透明白色硅胶层覆盖的数字RGBC光-数转换器和不透明黑色薄膜组成，利用背散射LED光强随局部压入深度单调变化；同一电路板可更换两种凝胶 cartridge：普通型用于法向力感知，彩色壁型通过返回光颜色编码局部剪切。使用自动CNC压头和六轴力/力矩传感器进行表征，并为每个 taxel 建立三轴力估计模型。

Result: 普通 cartridge 在104 mN至至少5.8 N范围内对力呈线性响应（R²=0.999），重复性2.0%、迟滞5.9%，归一化后 taxel 间差异6-10%；剪切 cartridge 在三种压头形状和九个留出会话中，汇总估计x/y/z方向R²=0.96/0.96/0.92，单 taxel 估计R²=0.92/0.93/0.94，剪切方向中位误差4.8度；将LuxTac压力读数加入Action Chunking Transformer策略后，插孔任务成功率较纯视觉基线显著提升。

Conclusion: LuxTac是首个在单个 taxel 层面解析剪切力的光学 taxel 阵列，兼具薄型、低成本、可扩展和无需相机/磁体/凸点覆层的优点，并能有效增强机器人操作策略。

Abstract: Low-cost tactile arrays have made touch a practical input for robot learning, but piezoresistive designs sense only pressure, while camera-based sensors that also resolve shear are too bulky to tile over a gripper. To solve this problem, we present LuxTac, a flexible, scalable optical taxel array, about 3.5 mm thick and built from off-the-shelf parts. Each taxel is a digital RGBC light-to-digital converter under a translucent white silicone layer capped by an opaque black film, so the back-scattered LED light falls monotonically with local indentation depth. The same board accepts two interchangeable gel cartridges: a plain one for normal sensing, and one with colored walls that encodes the local shear in the color of the returned light, with no camera, magnets, or bump overlay, making LuxTac, to our knowledge, the first optical taxel array to resolve shear at individual taxels. Characterized with an automated CNC indenter and a six-axis force/torque sensor, the normal cartridge responds linearly to force ($R^2$ = 0.999) from 104 mN to at least 5.8 N, with 2.0% repeatability, 5.9% hysteresis, and a taxel-to-taxel variation of 6-10% after normalization. With the shear cartridge, per-taxel models estimate the three-axis force at every taxel; across three indenter shapes and nine held-out sessions, the summed estimates reach $R^2$ = 0.96/0.96/0.92 in x/y/z, each taxel's estimate 0.92/0.93/0.94, and the median shear-direction error is 4.8 degrees. Adding LuxTac pressure readings to an Action Chunking Transformer policy substantially improves peg-in-hole success over a vision-only baseline. Design files, code, and videos are available at this https URL .

</details>


### [65] [Learning the Dynamics of Deformable Linear Objects Through Coupled Oscillators for Bimanual Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23563)
*Iván López-Broceño,Maximilian Stölzle,Daniel Feliu-Talegon,Daniela Rus,Cosimo Della Santina*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Φ-CON结合浮基耦合振子网络与学习仿射微分同胚，学习力驱动的可变形线状物体动力学，并据此设计具有闭环稳定性保证的PD控制器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学习型DLO方法使用简化变形模型，忽略动态效应与机械响应，仅适用于轻质柔性物体和慢速运动。

Method: 提出Φ-CON：由仅依赖DLO形状的潜势能浮基耦合振子网络（FBCON）与观测位置到潜坐标的学习仿射微分同胚Φ组成，具有构造性稳定形状动力学和显式拉格朗日动能/势能；基于该结构设计补偿学习势能的模型PD控制器。

Result: 在四个仿真DLO和真实双臂机器人上评估：仿真预测精度与NODE、LNN相当，参数量分别减少97.2%和90.2%；相比无模型PD基线，仿真端点调节平均误差降低97.5%-99.7%，真实实验两目标平均每目标误差降低27.9%。

Conclusion: 显式建模刚重物体的机械响应对DLO控制至关重要，Φ-CON在保持精度同时显著降低参数量并提供闭环稳定性保证。

Abstract: Many learning-based approaches for deformable linear objects (DLOs) rely on simplified deformation models that neglect dynamic effects and the mechanical response of the objects, limiting their applicability to lightweight, compliant objects and slow motions. Inspired by recent advances in learning physically structured dynamics with coupled oscillator networks (CONs), we propose $\Phi$-CON for learning force-driven DLO dynamics. The model combines a floating-base coupled oscillator network (FBCON), whose latent potential energy depends only on the DLO's shape, with a learned affine diffeomorphism $\Phi$ between observed positions along the DLO and latent coordinates. Its shape dynamics are stable by construction, and its Lagrangian structure yields explicit expressions for kinetic and potential energy. We exploit this structure to design a model-based proportional-derivative (PD) controller that compensates for the learned potential forces, thereby establishing closed-loop stability guarantees and ensuring convergence. We evaluate the approach on four simulated DLOs and a real bimanual robot setup. In simulation, $\Phi$-CON achieves prediction accuracy comparable to neural ordinary differential equation (NODE) models and Lagrangian neural networks (LNNs), with $97.2\%$ and $90.2\%$ fewer parameters, respectively. Relative to a model-free PD baseline, the proposed controller reduces mean endpoint regulation error by $97.5$-$99.7\%$ in simulation and achieves a mean per-target reduction of $27.9\%$ across both endpoints in real-world experiments, demonstrating the importance of explicitly accounting for the mechanical response of stiff and heavy objects.

</details>


### [66] [RESCUE Jumper: A Centimeter-Scale Rolling and Jumping Robot for Search and Rescue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23531)
*Kiran Marques,Shashwat Singh,Ben D'Ambrosia,Zeynep Temel,Melisa Orta Martinez*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出125克厘米级机器人RESCUE Jumper，用同一对执行器实现滚动与跳跃，通过倒转轮子方向卷绕弹性尾跳越障碍，并建立三阶段跳跃模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 厘米级机器人可进入人类无法到达的狭窄空间，适合城市搜救，但小尺寸使其部署后越障能力差，需兼顾地面滚动与跳跃。

Method: 机器人宽6.2 cm、轮径6.2 cm；正向轮动实现滚动，反向轮动卷绕弹簧钢尾储存弹性势能并释放跳跃；通过调节卷绕速度控制跳跃方向，并分三阶段建模：尾部释放确定接触点与姿态、尾部伸直至离地、弹道飞行预测高度与横向位移。

Result: 卷绕速度从0.75 rad/s增至8.73 rad/s时跳跃由后向转为前向；起跳角相对竖直轴为45°至-11°，最大高度9.2 cm、最大横向位移11.2 cm；定性测试展示其在杂乱真实环境中的运动能力。

Conclusion: 该模型与实验可为受限空间内厘米级搜救与检测机器人的跳跃机构设计提供参考。

Abstract: Centimeter-scale robots are promising for urban search and rescue because they can enter confined spaces that are inaccessible or unsafe for humans. However, their small size limits obstacle traversal after deployment. This paper presents the RESCUE Jumper, a 125 g mobile robot with a 6.2 cm width and 6.2 cm diameter wheels that combines ground rolling with jumping to traverse steps and gaps that exceeds its rolling capability. The RESCUE Jumper uses its two actuators for both rolling mode and jumping by reversing the direction of wheel motion. Forward wheel motion enables rolling, while reverse wheel motion winds a spring steel tail to store elastic potential energy and release it for jumping. The RESCUE Jumper varies tail winding speed to control jump direction, transitioning from backward to forward jumps as winding speed increases from 0.75 rad/s to 8.73 rad/s. The launch angle of the jumps ranges from 45 degree to -11 degree with respect to the vertical axis, reaching a maximum height of 9.2 cm and a maximum lateral displacement of 11.2 cm. We model the winding speed-dependent jumping behavior in three phases: tail release to determine the contact point and robot orientation, tail straightening until liftoff, and ballistic flight to predict jump height and lateral displacement. Finally, qualitative tests highlight the robot's locomotion in cluttered, real-world environments. The model and experiments presented in this work can inform future designs of space-constrained jump mechanisms in centimeter-scale robots for search and rescue and inspection in unstructured environments.

</details>


### [67] [Learning and Control in Physically Coupled Dynamical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23502)
*Bao Trung Truong,Dat Thanh Nguyen,Thinh T. Doan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出低成本耦合动力学测试平台TAG，结合倾斜迷宫球板与旋转倒立摆，用模型基与无模型强化学习分别学习两个子系统控制策略，发现耦合导致迷宫策略成功率骤降但能在线快速恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究物理耦合动力学系统中的学习与控制问题，需要一种能同时体现两个子系统相互扰动影响的紧凑低成本实验平台，以考察一个子系统动态对另一个子系统学习过程的影响。

Method: 构建TAG平台，将主动倾斜并转向的迷宫球板与顶部旋转倒立摆耦合；采用互补的强化学习范式，对迷宫控制使用基于模型的方法，对倒立摆控制使用无模型方法，分别独立训练两个子系统的控制策略。

Result: 引入倒立摆后迷宫策略成功率立即急剧下降，但策略能在少量学习回合内在线适应并恢复到耦合前性能；独立训练的两个策略均成功控制迷宫板与倒立摆，尽管二者存在物理耦合。

Conclusion: TAG平台可有效研究物理耦合动力学系统中的学习与控制，模型基与无模型强化学习互补范式能应对子系统间相互扰动，且策略具备在线适应耦合影响的能力。

Abstract: We introduce TAG (Testbed for Autonomous Games), a compact, low-cost robotic platform for studying learning and control in physically coupled dynamical systems. TAG combines an actively tilted and steered ball-in-maze board with a top-mounted rotary inverted pendulum. The pendulum's reaction torque and vibration disturb the board and marble, while the board's tilt, driven by the maze-solving policy, simultaneously disturbs the pendulum's balance. The main challenge in learning control policies for each subsystem is accounting for the impact of one subsystem's dynamics on the other's learning process. To address this challenge, we present complementary reinforcement learning paradigms, leveraging model-based and model-free approaches to learn the control policies for the maze and pendulum, respectively. Our key finding is that introducing the pendulum causes an immediate, sharp drop in the maze policy's success rate, but the policy adapts online and recovers to its pre-coupling performance within a small number of learning episodes. Experimental results show that both policies, trained independently, successfully control the maze board and the pendulum despite their physical coupling.

</details>


### [68] [BiCAT: Bimanual Coordination Action Tokenization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23499)
*Xuanwei Liu,Guipu Cao,Zhongyi Luo,Jiafei Duan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: BiCAT提出双臂协调动作分词器，通过共享动作表示与跨臂注意力建模双臂协作，提升VLA策略在双臂操作任务中的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA动作分词器将双臂拼接为单一向量，无法共享双臂共同运动模式且跨臂依赖纠缠于每个token中，迫使策略隐式学习协调关系。

Method: BiCAT在双臂间共享一个运动表示，并通过对称跨臂注意力和联合解码器使每臂的token以其伙伴臂为条件；策略并行预测每臂所有token，先生成左臂再以左臂为条件生成右臂。

Result: 在RoboTwin 2.0的十个任务和三种实体的六个真实任务上，BiCAT在对比分词器中成功率最高；仿真中平均成功率比八个基线中最强者高17.6个百分点，在双臂紧密协调任务上增益最大，每动作块仅需40.5 ms。

Conclusion: 动作分词器是建模双臂协调的自然位置，显式共享与跨臂条件化可显著提升双臂操作性能。

Abstract: Autoregressive vision-language-action (VLA) models predict discrete action tokens with the next-token objective of their vision-language backbone, so the action tokenizer defines the interface between the policy and the robot. In bimanual manipulation, the two arms share a repertoire of movements yet coordinate through complementary roles, and an action representation should capture both. However, most existing tokenizers encode both arms as a single concatenated action vector, in which motion patterns common to the two arms are not shared and cross-arm dependencies remain entangled within each token. The policy must therefore learn both aspects implicitly. We propose Bimanual Coordination Action Tokenization (BiCAT), which shares one motion representation across arms while conditioning each arm's tokens on its partner through symmetric cross-arm attention and a joint decoder. The policy then predicts all tokens of each arm in parallel, generating the left arm first and the right arm conditioned on it. Across ten RoboTwin 2.0 tasks and six real-world tasks on three embodiments, BiCAT achieves the highest success among the compared tokenizers. In simulation, it improves mean success by 17.6 percentage points over the strongest of eight baselines, with the largest gains on tasks that require close coordination between the arms, while requiring only 40.5 ms per action chunk. These results suggest that the action tokenizer is a natural place to model bimanual coordination.

</details>


### [69] [Meaning Reaches Object Choice but Not Completion: Where Is a VLA Vulnerable to Landmark Synonyms?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24076)
*Ryoichi Kurihara,Kota Sunamoto*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究了GR00T N1.7视觉-语言-动作策略对指令改写的鲁棒性，发现同义词会导致任务完成率下降，并定位了连接器前后两侧的干预效果与微调修复机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 成功率的单一指标无法区分策略是选错物体还是完不成任务，也无法定位策略中应在何处干预；因此需要研究VLA策略对指令改写的脆弱性及其内部机制。

Method: 在LIBERO-Spatial两个仅地标词不同的任务上测试GR00T N1.7；通过同义词与虚构词对比、在视觉语言模型与动作生成器之间连接器前后粘贴地标token向量、隐藏地标token、以及在多样化地标词上微调（包括仅训练连接器和复制连接器）等方式进行因果干预与闭环评估。

Result: 训练外同义词使策略选择其命名词所指的碗，但某任务闭环完成率仅67.6%（原词为100%）；连接器前粘贴地标向量几乎总能切换到另一碗，连接器后单独粘贴地标、其余指令或图像token很少改变选择，但任意两者组合即可；隐藏地标token仍降低同义词完成率；多样化地标词微调可修复同义词完成率（仅训练连接器或复制连接器即可），原指令微调无效；开环起始帧评分漏掉了连接器外权重的修复贡献。

Conclusion: 在当前证据下，对指令改写的鲁棒性应在闭环中执行动作来评判，干预需覆盖连接器两侧，并分别评估物体选择与任务完成。

Abstract: VLA policies are fragile to rewording. Success rates alone cannot say whether a policy fails to choose the object or to complete the task, nor where in the policy to intervene. We study the released GR00T N1.7 on two LIBERO-Spatial tasks differing only in the landmark word telling which of two bowls to take. Synonyms absent from the training instructions send the policy to the bowl they name, unlike made-up words, yet complete 67.6% of one task's closed-loop episodes, against the original word's 100%. Before the connector between the vision-language model and the action generator, replacing only the landmark tokens' vectors with the other landmark's (pasting) sends nearly every episode to the other bowl. After the connector, pasting at the landmark, the remaining instruction, or the image tokens alone rarely moves the choice, but any two do. Hiding the landmark tokens from the action generator after the connector still lowers completion for the synonyms that fall short. Finetuning on diversified landmark words repairs that one task's completion for synonyms absent from finetuning; finetuning on the original instructions does not. Training only the connector on the diversified words suffices, as does copying the connector of a policy fully finetuned on them into one finetuned on the original instructions. An open-loop score on starting frames misses that weights outside the connector also carry part of the repair. For now, on this evidence, robustness to rewording should be judged in closed loop, with actions executed, intervening on both sides of the connector, scoring choice and completion separately.

</details>


### [70] [Measuring the Conservatism of Latent Safety Filters for World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23685)
*Johannes Mootz,Mani Amani,Reza Akhavian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出重叠度指标衡量潜在安全过滤器的保守性，并证明其为满足安全约束下必须拒绝的安全转移最小比例；在视觉导航任务中，独立编码器设计重叠度最低、拒绝安全动作最少、到达目标最多。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜在安全过滤器虽经保形校准限制接受不安全动作的概率，但可能因潜在表示而过度保守拒绝大量安全动作，需要量化这种保守性以比较不同世界模型安全设计。

Method: 将相似潜在和动作分组为单元，定义重叠度为单元内安全转移比例；证明重叠度是每单元单决策过滤器在数据中不接受任何不安全转移时必须拒绝的安全转移最小份额；结合保形校准比较三种设计：在世界模型上训练分类器、使用世界模型本身、使用独立编码器。

Result: 在视觉导航任务中，独立编码器的重叠度最低；在相同保形水平下，其过滤器拒绝的安全动作最少，规划器到达目标数最多。

Conclusion: 重叠度可作为比较潜在安全过滤器保守性的有效指标，独立编码器设计在安全性与效率间取得最佳平衡，减少对安全动作的误拒并提升任务完成率。

Abstract: A latent safety filter decides from the latent of a world model whether a planned action is safe. Conformal calibration sets the threshold of the filter so that it accepts an unsafe action only with a small probability. However, the filter can still be conservative and refuse many safe actions, depending on the latent. To measure the conservatism that a latent forces on the filter, we group similar latents and actions into cells. A filter that gives one decision per cell can refuse the unsafe transitions of a cell only by refusing the safe ones in it as well. We call the share of safe transitions that lie in such cells the overlap. We prove that the overlap is the minimum share of safe transitions that such a filter has to refuse in order to accept no unsafe transition in the data. Both the overlap and the conformal calibration can be applied to any latent. We use this to compare three existing designs, in which the safety labels train a classifier on top of the world model, the world model itself, or a separate encoder beside it. In a visual navigation task, the separate encoder has the lowest overlap, and at the same conformal level its filter refuses the fewest safe actions and lets the planner reach the most goals of the three.

</details>


### [71] [Finetuning VLAs for Dexterous Manipulation: The Right Checkpoint Is All You Need](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23676)
*Artur Kuramshin,Charlotte Cloutier,Michael Przystupa,Kirsty Ellis,Mohammad S. Akhter,Glen Berseth*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究如何通过监督微调(SFT)与扩散引导强化学习(DSRL)结合，将视觉-语言-动作模型适配到多指灵巧手。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA操作数据集多针对平行夹爪，且多指手机器人遥操作数据难以采集，导致向灵巧手迁移困难。

Method: 基于π0.5模型与LEAP灵巧手，在两个仿真任务中仅用少量示范，评估五种微调方案（涉及冻结VLM、只训练动作专家、LoRA等），并比较SFT与后续DSRL的表现。

Result: (1) SFT成功率与DSRL后成功率相关性弱，最佳SFT检查点往往并非最易被引导；(2) 冻结VLM、仅微调动作专家在训练和分布外放置下均可获得最易引导的策略；(3) LoRA是最不易引导的方法。

Conclusion: 推荐适配方案：冻结VLM、完整训练动作专家，且不以SFT rollout成功率作为检查点选择指标。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action models have demonstrated strong manipulation capabilities. A limitation of VLA models is that most manipulation datasets focus on parallel-jaw gripper systems, making transfer to multi-finger hands challenging, particularly as teleoperation datasets are harder to collect with multi-fingered robots. In this work, we study how to best adapt VLAs to dexterous hands by pairing supervised fine-tuning (SFT) with diffusion-steering reinforcement learning (DSRL). Using $\pi_{0.5}$ and a LEAP hand, we evaluate five fine-tuning recipes across two simulation tasks using only a handful of demonstrations. Our empirical analysis yields three key insights: (1) SFT success is poorly correlated with post-DSRL success, meaning the best SFT checkpoint is rarely the most effective to steer; (2) freezing the vision-language model (VLM) and tuning only the action expert produces the most steerable policy for both training and out-of-distribution placements; and (3) low-rank adaptation (LoRA) is the least steerable approach. Consequently, we recommend an adaptation recipe that freezes the VLM, fully trains the action expert, and eschews SFT rollout success as a checkpoint selection metric.

</details>


### [72] [RECLAIM: Robot-Vision-Informed Adversarial Patch Defense via Embodied Attention Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24494)
*Jiayou Zhu,Peng Guo,Litian Zhang,Xinze Shi,Yuting Zhang,Zelong Yu,Yize Tian,Jirong Liu,Zifeng Kang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RECLAIM是一种免训练、推理时的防御方法，利用冻结VLA模型自身对局部视觉干预的注意力响应来指导净化，从而抵御对抗补丁攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言-动作策略在闭环物理交互中易受对抗补丁攻击，现有防御依赖策略微调或辅助模型，需要额外训练或推理开销。

Method: 利用冻结VLA对局部视觉编辑的注意力变化作为反馈，结合机器人几何信息精确定位补丁并选择局部处理，并在动作改变场景时重新验证净化效果。

Result: 在四种对抗攻击方法和多个操作任务套件上，RECLAIM使95%任务至少一次rollout成功，将LIBERO-Object成功率从0%提升至70.0%，平均决策时间仅548.9毫秒/控制步。

Conclusion: 策略引导的视觉净化能有效防御具身智能体对抗补丁攻击并恢复闭环任务执行。

Abstract: Vision-language-action policies control embodied agents through a perception-action loop grounded in physical interaction, where adversarial patches can corrupt action predictions and thereby alter subsequent observations. To mitigate these threats, many existing defenses rely on policy adaptation or auxiliary models, but require additional training or inference. We introduce RECLAIM, a training-free, inference-time defense that uses a frozen VLA's own attention response to local visual interventions to guide purification during closed-loop embodied manipulation. Our key insight is that attention changes induced by local edits provide feedback for choosing where and how to purify as the robot interacts with its environment. RECLAIM combines this feedback with robot geometry to refine patch localization and select local treatments, then revalidates them as the agent's embodied actions change the observed scene. We evaluate RECLAIM against patches generated by four adversarial attack methods across multiple manipulation task suites under frozen VLA control. Under adversarial patch attacks, RECLAIM succeeds in at least one rollout on 95% of the evaluated tasks and raises rollout success on LIBERO-Object from 0% without defense to 70.0%. Its mean decision time is only 548.9 ms per control step. These results highlight policy-guided visual purification as an effective approach to defending embodied agents against adversarial patches and recovering closed-loop task execution.

</details>


### [73] [Efficient World-Model Planning via Physics-Guided Search Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24171)
*Hongyu Zhang,Xiaoshuai Hao,Heye Huang,Qingwen Meng,Wenjun Shi,Jianao Xu,Xin Hu,Xuanru Jin,Jingyi Zhang,Baicang Guo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PGSD是一种单视图规划框架，利用执行感知选择、间隙感知搜索教师和未来条件修复学生，在冻结预训练规划器与奖励器的前提下实现池外轨迹修复，无需在线生成未来场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型通过预测未来场景评估候选轨迹，但准确预测不保证遵守道路边界；当所有候选轨迹均越界时，重排序无法选出可行轨迹，需要池外修复机制。

Method: PGSD包含三部分：(1)执行感知选择在模拟执行下检查自车道路合规性并选最高分可行轨迹；(2)间隙感知搜索教师生成池外修正，并选执行中最小道路边界间隙更大的示范以缓冲模仿误差；(3)未来条件修复学生从奖励器特征和执行反馈预测单次修正，用于单次 rollout 验证；预训练规划器与奖励器冻结，不在线生成未来场景。

Result: NAVSIM v1上单视图PGSD达90.97 PDMS，超过所有对比的单视图规划器，并保留搜索教师相对执行感知选择PDMS增益的73.2%；NAVSIM v2 navhard上达38.96 EPDMS，比WorldDrive提升11.24点(40.5%)；学生每次修复触发用单次验证rollout替代最多十次搜索rollout；HUGSIM Hard样例提供零样本迁移的定性证据。

Conclusion: PGSD通过结合未来表示与执行反馈实现可验证的池外修复，在单视图设置下显著提升规划性能并降低在线搜索开销，具备零样本迁移潜力。
