Abstract: World models support autonomous driving by evaluating candidate trajectories through predicted future scene evolution. However, accurate future prediction does not ensure road-compliant execution; when all candidates violate road boundaries, re-ranking cannot select a feasible trajectory. We propose Physics-Guided Search Distillation (PGSD), a single-view planning framework that combines future representations and execution feedback for verified beyond-pool repair through three components: (1) Execution-Aware Selection checks ego-vehicle road compliance under simulated execution and selects the highest-scoring feasible trajectory; (2) a Clearance-Aware Search Teacher generates beyond-pool corrections and selects demonstrations with larger minimum road-boundary clearance during execution to buffer imitation errors; and (3) a Future-Conditioned Repair Student predicts one correction from rewarder features and execution feedback for single-rollout verification. The pretrained planner and rewarder remain frozen; no future scenes are generated online. On NAVSIM v1, single-view PGSD achieves 90.97 PDMS, exceeding all compared prior single-view planners and retaining 73.2% of the search teacher's PDMS gain over execution-aware selection. On NAVSIM v2 navhard, it reaches 38.96 EPDMS, improving on WorldDrive by 11.24 points (40.5%). The student replaces up to ten search rollouts with a single verification rollout per repair trigger. HUGSIM Hard examples provide qualitative evidence of zero-shot transfer.

</details>


### [74] [Ego2X: Contact-aware Transfer from Egocentric Human Data to Any Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24138)
*Xinke Lyu,Bowen Yang,Zhicheng Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Ego2X提出以物体为中心的框架，将单目第一视角视频转换为物理可执行的机器人数据，覆盖灵巧手和并行夹爪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自我中心到机器人的流程主要依据视觉外观判断保真度，导致轨迹在物理上失效：手指悬停在物体旁、夹爪在空处闭合；作者认为物体如何运动以及驱动运动的接触才是关键。

Method: 提出CORE（以接触为中心的物体交互表示），记录物体表面锚点上的每次接触及相对力、抓取模式和交互状态；在流程中用CORE通过物体相对约束纠正手-物漂移，引导逆运动学朝向机器人必须实现的接触，并在手臂擦除和机器人合成时保留被持物体。

Result: 在HOT3D上，接触驱动纠正将接触中手部深度误差降低33.7%；在EgoDex上，接触驱动重定向实现62.9%的标注接触，超过官方HuRo流程的27.9%；在RoboTwin 2.0中，仅用Ego2X回合后训练X-VLA（无模拟器演示），在200个配对场景中将任务成功率从零样本40.5%提升至67.0%。

Conclusion: 以接触和物体运动为中心的转换能生成物理可执行的机器人数据，显著优于仅凭视觉外观的方法，并将公开转换流程和CORE标注数据集。

Abstract: Embodied models depend on real interaction data, yet collecting it on physical robots is slow and labor-intensive. Recent ego-to-robot pipelines retarget first-person view human videos to robot data via transferring hand and arm motion. They judge fidelity mainly by visual appearance. However, trajectories often fail in terms of physics: fingers hover beside objects, and grippers close on empty space. We argue that how the object moves and the contacts that drive the motion count. Thus, we present Ego2X, an object-centric framework that converts monocular egocentric video into physically executable robot data across dexterous hands and parallel grippers. Ego2X centers on CORE (Contact-centric Object-interaction REpresentation), which records each contact on the object by its surface anchor, as well as relative force, grasp mode, and interaction state. Throughout the pipeline, CORE corrects hand-object drift via object-relative constraints, steers inverse kinematics toward contacts the robot must realize, and preserves held objects during arm erasure and robot compositing. On HOT3D, contact-driven correction reduces in-contact hand depth error by 33.7%, while on EgoDex, contact-driven retargeting realizes 62.9% of annotated contacts, surpassing the official HuRo pipeline (27.9%). In RoboTwin 2.0, post-training X-VLA on Ego2X episodes alone, without simulator demonstrations, raises task success rate from 40.5% zero-shot to 67.0% across 200 paired scenes. We will publicly release our conversion pipeline and CORE-annotated dataset for dexterous hands and parallel grippers.

</details>


### [75] [SemPhys: Semantic-Physical Co-Learning for Humanoid Motion Generation via Differentiable World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24074)
*Zejian Kang,Yeqiang Wang,Zekai Chen,Boyang Shen,Mingyu Ma,Bingqian Wu,Fan Zhou,Jianjie Zhang,Chentao Shen,Wentao Yang,Rui Qian,Xiangru Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SemPhys通过模拟执行中的语义反馈训练运动生成器，使机器人实际执行动作与自然语言指令语义对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法先生成运动学参考再由跟踪控制器执行，但控制器在接触约束下维持平衡时会使执行动作偏离参考，改变其传达的语义；因此语义对齐应直接评估在机器人实际执行的完整运动上。

Method: 提出SemPhys框架：利用可微世界模型和固定跟踪控制器预测记录执行的一小段；将该预测插入原位置，对完整轨迹与指令评分并反向传播通过预测段；同时用配对锚定损失保持生成参考与初始生成器对应参考接近以稳定适应。

Result: 在BONES-SEED上，SemPhys基本保持了从参考到执行的指令检索准确率，并在所评估方法中于执行动作上取得最高检索准确率。

Conclusion: 直接在实际执行运动上评估语义对齐并利用模拟执行反馈训练，能有效缩小参考与执行间的语义差距，提升人机协作中语言指令到全身运动的语义一致性。

Abstract: In human-robot collaboration, natural language lets people direct a humanoid's whole-body movements without programming joint trajectories. A common approach generates a kinematic reference from an instruction and relies on a tracking controller to execute it. However, as the controller maintains balance under contact constraints, the executed motion can depart from the reference in ways that change the action it conveys. Our central insight is that semantic alignment must be evaluated directly on the complete motion the robot physically executes. Building on this insight, we introduce \textbf{SemPhys}, a framework that trains motion generators using semantic feedback from simulated executions. A differentiable world model and a fixed tracking controller predict a short segment of a recorded execution. SemPhys inserts this prediction at its original position, scores the complete trajectory against the instruction, and backpropagates through the predicted segment. A paired anchoring loss stabilizes adaptation by keeping each generated reference close to its counterpart from the initial generator. On BONES-SEED, SemPhys largely preserves instruction retrieval accuracy from reference to execution and achieves the highest retrieval accuracy on executed motions among the evaluated methods.

</details>


### [76] [WAM-Policy: World Action Models with On-Policy Distillation for Quadrupedal Loco-Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24011)
*Junlin Long,Xu Deng,Yiran Wang,Yue Yang,Yang Zhao,Zeyu Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: WAM-Policy通过两阶段将面向机械臂的世界动作模型（WAM）迁移为语言条件四足“三腿行走+单腿操作”策略，先做跨形态适配与在线自蒸馏加速，再用强化学习提升并多教师蒸馏合并，实现快采样且精度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无臂四足loco-manipulation策略不遵循语言指令；WAM虽能作为语言条件操作策略，但仅面向机械臂预训练且推理采样慢。

Method: 两阶段：第一阶段Cross-Morphology Policy Adaptation and Acceleration将arm-centric WAM适配到四足，并用on-policy self-distillation（OPSD）将迁移策略蒸馏为一个视频步、两个动作步的学生；第二阶段Task-Specific Improvement and Merging用强化学习微调教师，并通过multi-teacher on-policy distillation（M-OPD）将慢速专家合并为少步学生。

Result: 在QLM-Bench的10个仿真任务（1000条无遥操作合成演示）上，迁移策略达77.0%，OPSD学生保持76.7%且采样快14倍；任务特定RL与M-OPD将其提升至85.0%；OPSD学生已部署于真实Unitree Go2 Air。

Conclusion: WAM-Policy以显著更低的推理成本获得更强的四足语言条件loco-manipulation策略。

Abstract: Arm-free quadrupeds can perform loco-manipulation, walking on three legs while manipulating with a front leg, yet no existing policy for it follows language instructions. World action models (WAMs) have emerged as effective language-conditioned manipulation policies, but are pretrained for robot arms and sample slowly. WAM-Policy turns one WAM into a quadrupedal loco-manipulation policy via two stages. The first, Cross-Morphology Policy Adaptation and Acceleration, adapts the arm-centric WAM to quadrupeds and distills the transferred policy into a student with one video and two action steps by on-policy self-distillation (OPSD). This student, however, is bounded by its teacher, which only imitates demonstrations. The second stage, Task-Specific Improvement and Merging, therefore tunes the teacher by reinforcement learning (RL) and merges the slow specialists into one few-step student by multi-teacher on-policy distillation (M-OPD). On QLM-Bench, ten simulated tasks with 1,000 demonstrations synthesized without teleoperation, the transferred policy reaches 77.0%, and the OPSD student keeps 76.7% while sampling 14 times faster. Task-specific RL and M-OPD lift it to 85.0%. We also deploy the OPSD student on a real Unitree Go2 Air. WAM-Policy thus achieves a stronger quadrupedal policy at a fraction of the inference cost.

</details>


### [77] [Facet-SLAM: Real-Time Triangle Splatting SLAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23935)
*Edward Beng Wai Tan,Siew-Kei Lam*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Facet-SLAM是首个基于三角形splatting的实时RGB-D SLAM系统，通过将跟踪与建图解耦实现约100 FPS的高效几何配准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D高斯splatting SLAM渲染逼真但表面几何差且需额外网格提取，三角形splatting可直接三角化网格，但其SLAM依赖光度跟踪，速度过慢无法满足传感器帧率定位。

Method: 将跟踪与建图角色分离：跟踪器由融合深度设定每个三角形质心，并用GICP将帧配准到这些质心；建图器通过变量替换限制为非平移更新，避免三角形偏离观测表面。

Result: 在桌面GPU上约100 FPS达到有竞争力的跟踪精度，在Jetson AGX Orin上达到传感器帧率；相比光度方法快20倍以上，渲染质量相当，表面精度提升，Replica上Delaunay网格的chamfer距离从1.55降至0.86 cm。

Conclusion: Facet-SLAM证明三角形splatting结合快速几何配准可实现实时、高表面精度的RGB-D SLAM，兼顾渲染质量与速度，并将开源实现。

Abstract: Dense SLAM systems based on 3D Gaussian splatting build maps that render photorealistically, but their surface geometry is poor, and obtaining a mesh requires extraction or fusion stages. Triangle splatting avoids this with surface primitives whose vertices can be triangulated directly into a connected mesh, yet SLAM with this representation has so far relied on photometric tracking, which is too slow for localization at sensor rate. We present Facet-SLAM, the first real-time triangle splatting SLAM system for RGB-D cameras. Fast geometric registration is the natural alternative, but it is not sufficient alone, since the mapper's optimization displaces triangles from the observed surface and makes them unreliable registration targets. We therefore separate the two roles: the tracker sets each triangle's centroid from fused depth and registers frames to these centroids by GICP, while the mapper is restricted to non-translating updates via a change of variables. Our system achieves competitive tracking accuracy at around 100 FPS on a desktop GPU and runs at sensor rate on a Jetson AGX Orin. Compared with the photometric approach, it is over 20 times faster, matches rendering quality, and improves surface accuracy, reducing the chamfer distance of the Delaunay mesh on Replica from 1.55 to 0.86 cm. We will release the implementation at this https URL .

</details>


### [78] [How Uncertain Is My Action? Self-Correcting Visual Planning with Frozen World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23620)
*Siqing Li,Luming Yang,Yijia Jiao,Junze Deng,Alper Yilmaz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 冻结潜在世界模型可通过逆向运行检测动作偏移并自我校正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部署时执行动作可能偏离指令，而冻结模型无法感知这种偏移。

Method: 将世界模型反向运行，对假设的动作偏移预测应有观测并与实际观测比较，构造仅保留偏移可产生部分的偏移分数，实现局部最优检测、Cramer-Rao估计、识别主导生成器并补偿规划器。

Result: PushT、TwoRoom、OGBench-Cube上检测AUROC误差在0.05内；PushT成功率0.650升至0.862，Cube从0.378升至0.432，达真实偏移oracle水平且优于五种测试时自适应方法；真实机械臂识别无编码器记录的基座关节偏移，将到达误差从36 mm降至3-5 mm，随机封闭偏移下恢复92-94%误差削减。

Conclusion: 冻结世界模型可充当滤波器：预测未来、检验动作接口不确定性与有效性并最终校正失败。

Abstract: A frozen latent world model combined with sampling-based model predictive control is a visual planner. At deployment, the executed action can depart from the commanded one, and the frozen model has no way to notice. We show that the model can be run in reverse: for any hypothesized actuation shift, it predicts what it should have observed under that shift, and comparing this prediction with what was actually observed quantifies the shift and lets the planner correct itself. Unlike the residual energy, which mixes the shift with the model's own error, the score keeps only the part of the residual that a shift can produce. This shift score detects the shift with a locally optimal test, estimates it with Cramer-Rao error bars, names the dominant generator, and compensates the planner once an alarm fires. On PushT, TwoRoom and OGBench-Cube with a frozen latent world model, measured detection follows the prediction to within 0.05 AUROC. Self-correction raises success from 0.650 to 0.862 on PushT and from 0.378 to 0.432 on the harder Cube task, levels with an oracle that is told the true shift at the same block, better than five common test-time adaptation methods. On a real robotic arm, the score identifies a base-joint offset that no joint encoder registers and reduces the reach error from 36 mm to 3-5 mm, and under shifts drawn at random and sealed before the session, estimates recover 92-94% of the error reduction achieved with the true shift. The broader implication is that a frozen world model can act as a filter by predicting the future, testing action-interface uncertainty and validity, and finally correcting failures.

</details>


### [79] [RageVLA: Augmentation-Trained Perception and Action for State-of-the-Art Robustness on LIBERO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23517)
*Amrit Lal Singh,Smarak Kanjilal*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RageVLA通过数据增强和策略层面的解耦，在LIBERO-PRO上以0.21B参数达到0.72成功率，超越最佳已发表模型0.53。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型在LIBERO上看似饱和，但在LIBERO-PRO上因物体移动或指令变化而失效，作者认为问题在于策略将动作与训练时的位置、场景和指令措辞绑定，而非规模不足。

Method: 数据层面：对LIBERO演示按通用规则重排，移动旋转所有物体和固定物，将任务迁移到其他环境和表面，并改写所有指令。策略层面：基于冻结DINOv3特征的感知引擎输出解耦场景表示（带位置的对象列表和夹爪注视目标的紧凑编码），与TinyCLIP的图像和指令嵌入一起输入从零训练的流匹配扩散Transformer。

Result: RageVLA在LIBERO-PRO上取得0.72成功率（最佳已发表模型0.53），更大变体达0.73。LIBERO-Object测试中，打乱物体位置而图像正确时成功率从0.965降至0.215；目标物体位移28cm时成功率0.865，而π₀.₅在同一测试下仅0.105。

Conclusion: RageVLA通过鲁棒增强与接地编码的解耦设计，以较小参数量显著提升VLA策略在分布偏移下的泛化能力，并证明策略确实基于感知到的物体位置行动。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models look saturated on LIBERO, yet most of them fall apart on LIBERO-PRO as soon as an object moves or the instruction asks for something new. We argue that what is missing is not scale but decoupling. In these policies, what to do is tied to where things were during training, to the scene around them and to the wording of the instruction. We cut these ties in two places. In the data, LIBERO demonstrations are re-arranged by general rules: every object and fixture is moved and rotated, tasks are carried into other environments and onto other surfaces, and every instruction is paraphrased. In the policy, a perception engine on frozen DINOv3 features, trained on this augmented data, hands the action model the scene in decoupled form: a list of objects with their positions, and a compact code of what the gripper is looking at, next to image and instruction embeddings from TinyCLIP. A flow-matching diffusion transformer, trained from scratch, acts on this view. We call this combination of Robust Augmentation and Grounded Encoding RageVLA. With 0.21 billion parameters, RageVLA sets a new state of the art on LIBERO-PRO among VLA policies, scoring 0.72 against 0.53 for the best published model, and a larger variant reaches 0.73. Two further tests on LIBERO-Object show that the policy acts on what it perceives. When the object positions it is given are shuffled while the camera images stay correct, its success falls from 0.965 to 0.215. When the target object is displaced by 28 cm, it still succeeds in 0.865 of the episodes, where $\pi_{0.5}$, run through the same harness, succeeds in 0.105.

</details>


### [80] [Towards Pose-Robust Few-Shot Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24360)
*Wenhao Li,Zhongyi Han,Yuhao Wei,Liang Han,Yilong Yin,Liqiang Nie*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: WACO：通过将机械臂作为标定靶、静态场景作为世界锚点，从单帧RGB-D估计相机与基座的外参偏移并做规范化，使VLA策略在相机或底座移动后仍能正确操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微调后的VLA策略隐式记忆了数据采集时的相机-基座外参，三脚架被碰或底座重新对接几厘米就会导致抓取位置错误；作者指出相机移动与底座移动本质是同一现象（外参变化），且机器人自身具备纠正该变化所需的信息。

Method: 提出WACO世界锚定规范化框架：用正运动学在测得关节角下定位机械臂作为度量标定靶，静态场景作为世界锚点以区分相机移动与机器人移动；从单帧RGB-D在无真值外参和额外数据条件下估计两种偏移，将图像规范化到标定视角，将状态、动作和起始位姿规范化到标定基座坐标系，并在自身估计器伪影下微调未修改的策略；同时提出LIBERO-PoseShift基准，支持分级相机、基座与复合偏移。

Result: 在LIBERO-PoseShift、LIBERO-Plus相机轨道及UR5e真机四项任务上，WACO优于标准少样本流程和先前视角鲁棒方法：仿真中偏移场景成功率提升13.2个百分点，真机提升36.7个百分点，分别恢复90%的仿真与87%的真机相对真值oracle的差距；在无扰动训练数据、无测试时标定的方法中达到新的最优。

Conclusion: 相机与基座偏移可统一建模为外参变化，利用机器人自身的运动学与场景锚点即可在测试时无需额外标定地纠正，显著提升VLA策略对部署位姿变化的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Fine-tuning a vision-language-action (VLA) model on a few dozen demonstrations is the standard way to put a manipulation policy on a robot, yet the resulting policy silently assumes that the camera and the robot base stay exactly where they were during data collection: a bumped tripod or a base re-docked a few centimetres away makes it reach for the wrong place. We show that both failures are one phenomenon, a change of the camera-to-base extrinsic that the policy has memorised, and that the robot carries everything needed to undo it. We introduce WACO, a world-anchored canonicalization framework for VLA models: the arm, placed by forward kinematics at the measured joint angles, serves as a metric calibration target, and the static scene serves as the world anchor that tells a moved camera from a moved robot. From a single RGB-D frame, WACO estimates both shifts without ground-truth extrinsics or extra data, canonicalises the image to the calibration viewpoint and the state, action and start pose to the calibration base frame, and fine-tunes the unmodified policy under the artefacts of its own estimator. We also introduce LIBERO-PoseShift, the first benchmark for RGB VLAs with graded camera, base and compound shifts. Across LIBERO-PoseShift, the LIBERO-Plus camera track and four real-world tasks on a UR5e robot, WACO consistently outperforms the standard few-shot recipe and prior viewpoint-robust methods, raising shifted success by 13.2 points in simulation and by 36.7 points on the real robot, recovering 90% of the simulation gap to a ground-truth oracle and 87% of the real-robot gap, and establishing a new state of the art among methods that use no perturbed training data, with no test-time calibration. Project page: this https URL .

</details>


### [81] [Worth Another Look:Constrained World-Model Planning for Visual Inspection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24187)
*Ziyue Kang,Zixi Qi,Yuchen Dai,Nana Lv,Xirong Cheng,Liang Liu,Ying Li,Haoyan Wei,Haiyang Yao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 将冻结检测器上的多目标视觉巡检建模为部分可观测下的神经符号观测规划，解耦循环世界模型与符号可行性/资费规则，用滚动时域束搜索在线重规划查询、裁剪、增强与终止，在八个基准上以预算感知方式提升检测性能且无需重训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现场机器人与环境监测的视觉巡检受算力与传感预算严格限制：穷举扫描会把调用浪费在缺失类别上，短视或无搜索的策略难以应对异构资费断崖，也会丢弃需重新查询后才显现价值的视口编辑。

Method: 把巡检形式化为冻结检测器上的神经符号观测规划：紧凑循环世界模型负责学习动态，符号层精确刻画可行性与资费规则；采用带在线重规划的滚动时域束搜索联合调度类别查询、裁剪、增强与终止，并配合松弛感知的影子定价及大预算下的合法回退。

Result: 在八个陆地、光学与声呐基准上，多步前瞻在紧预算下优于静态调度与无搜索离线RL，RUOD上较静态优先级最高提升28.4个AP百分点、ARIS上提升15.1；动态定价在全部八个域提升大预算性能，规划开销约11 ms。

Conclusion: 将轻量学习动态与精确符号约束结合，可在不重训练的情况下把冻结检测器转化为自适应、预算感知的巡检智能体。

Abstract: Visual inspection in field robotics and environmental monitoring must localize multiple targets under strict compute and sensing caps. Exhaustive sweeps waste calls on absent classes, while myopic and search-free policies stumble over heterogeneous tariff cliffs or discard viewport edits whose value emerges only after re-querying. We formulate inspection over frozen detectors as neuro-symbolic observation planning under partial observability, decoupling a compact recurrent world model (WM) from exact symbolic feasibility and tariff rules. Receding-horizon beam search with online replanning jointly schedules class queries, crops, enhancements, and termination (stop), paired with slack-aware shadow pricing and legal fallback at larger budgets. Across eight terrestrial, optical, and sonar benchmarks, multi-step lookahead outperforms static schedules and search-free offline RL under tight budgets -- gaining up to 28.4 AP percentage points over static priority on RUOD and 15.1 on ARIS -- while dynamic pricing lifts large-budget performance on all eight domains at 11 ms planning cost. Ultimately, pairing lightweight learned dynamics with exact symbolic constraints turns frozen detectors into adaptive, budget-aware inspection agents without retraining.

</details>


### [82] [A Hierarchical Onboard Vision-Language-Navigation Framework for Aerial Robots in Complex Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23930)
*Yuze Wu,Mo Zhu,Xingxing Li,Yuheng Du,Zhaoqi Wang,Yuxin Fan,Wenjun Li,Kenghou Hoi,Zhichao Han,Xin Zhou,Fei Gao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VLN-AR 是一个面向无人机的分层视觉-语言-导航框架，通过解耦多模态推理与底层控制、自动化3DGS数据生成、三阶段渐进训练、轻量认知代理的长期推理及机载优化，在仿真和真实无人机导航中显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基础模型驱动的无人机导航方法受限于3D数据稀缺、轨迹可行性差、长期推理能力不足以及复杂环境下的计算效率低，难以在严格机载约束下实现语义推理与智能决策。

Method: 提出分层VLN框架VLN-AR，解耦多模态推理与低层控制，实现3D位姿指令与导航意图的结构化预测并转化为动态可行动作；构建全自动3D高斯泼溅管线生成可扩展照片级无人机导航数据；采用渐进三阶段训练策略增强接地、场景推理与复杂导航能力；引入轻量认知代理通过记忆感知任务分解改进长期推理；设计token稀疏化与模型轻量化等机载优化技术。

Result: 在资源受限无人机平台上实现7.1倍至9.4倍加速，同时保持高导航性能；仿真与真实无人机导航实验均较现有方法取得显著提升。

Conclusion: VLN-AR有效弥合了数据、推理与计算效率之间的差距，为复杂环境下无人机长期自主导航提供了可行且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAVs) are expected to achieve semantic reasoning, intelligent decision-making, and long-horizon navigation under strict onboard constraints. While recent foundation-model-driven approaches have shown promising progress toward this goal, existing methods remain limited by challenges in 3D data availability, trajectory feasibility, long-horizon reasoning, and computational efficiency in complex environments. To bridge these gaps, we propose VLN-AR, a hierarchical vision-language-navigation (VLN) framework for aerial robots. VLN-AR decouples multimodal reasoning and low-level control, enabling structured prediction of 3D pose commands and navigation intentions that are translated into dynamically feasible actions. To mitigate data scarcity and domain gaps, we introduce a fully automated 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS)-based pipeline to generate scalable photorealistic UAV navigation data. A progressive three-stage training strategy further enhances grounding, scene reasoning, and complex navigation. In addition, a lightweight cognitive agent improves long-horizon reasoning via memory-aware task decomposition. Finally, we design onboard optimization techniques, including token sparsification and model lightweighting, achieving 7.1x-9.4x speedup while maintaining high navigation performance on resource-limited UAV platforms. Experiments show great improvements over existing methods in both simulation and real-world UAV navigation. Project website: this https URL

</details>


### [83] [LAGR: Language-Aware Grasp Re-Ranking for Task Oriented Grasping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23858)
*Manav Kulshrestha,Shreyans Jain,Apoorva Vashisth,Ankush Gupta,Damon Conover,Aniket Bera*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出大规模任务导向抓取数据集和LAGR方法，联合考虑物理可行性与任务适配性，抓取成功率比现有最佳方法高9.3个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人抓取需同时满足物理可行性与下游任务适配性（如锤子把持位置因敲击或递交而不同），但现有任务导向抓取数据集规模小、合成化或标注昂贵。

Method: 构建含2.68M个5自由度带评分抓取标注、覆盖100,911个图像-任务对和17类物体的数据集；提出LAGR方法：先由视觉-语言输入生成任务无关的抓取候选及物理可行性分数，再用语言条件重排序器对所有候选联合推理打分。

Result: LAGR比次优方法高9.3个百分点，并在Franka Research 3机器人上对真实物体完成任务导向抓取验证。

Conclusion: 显式联合建模物理可行性与任务适配性并结合大规模标注数据，可有效提升任务导向抓取检测性能并迁移至真实机器人。

Abstract: Grasping is a fundamental capability for robotic manipulation, with the appropriate grasp depending on the downstream task. For example, a hammer should be held by its handle for hammering but by its head when it is handed over. Selecting such a grasp requires consideration of two criteria: physical feasibility on the object and suitability for the task. Developing such an approach requires dense grasp labels with both feasibility and task scores on real observations, but existing task-oriented grasping datasets are small, synthetic, or costly to annotate. To address this gap, we construct a dataset of 2.68 million scored 5-DoF grasp annotations over 100,911 image-task pairs from $17$ object categories. Additionally, we introduce LAGR, a task-oriented grasp detection method that explicitly addresses both these challenges. Given vision-language inputs, LAGR first generates task-independent grasp proposals along with a physical feasibility score, and then scores the generated grasps with a language-conditioned re-ranker that reasons over all proposals jointly. Experiments show that LAGR outperforms the next best method by 9.3 percentage points. We further validate LAGR on real objects by executing task-oriented grasps with a Franka Research 3 robot.

</details>


### [84] [RePlan-VLA: Progress-Aware Replanning for Long-Horizon Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23843)
*Apoorva Vashisth,Manav Kulshrestha,Damon Conover,Zachary Kingston,Aniket Bera*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出RePlan-VLA框架，将VLA模型的短时程操作能力扩展为可靠的长时程执行，无需微调策略本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型在需要多步相互依赖操作的长时程任务上性能下降，而让VLA策略同时承担长时程推理、记忆、动作生成、进度跟踪和错误恢复负担过重。

Method: RePlan-VLA是与VLA无关的框架：显式监控机器人运动以评估进度，并根据观测结果自适应修正未来计划，同时完全保留底层VLA策略，无需额外微调。

Result: 在RoboCasa365和CALVIN基准上，成功率分别比最强基线提升21.9%和10.4%；并在Franka Research 3机器人上验证了真实部署。

Conclusion: 在短时程VLA能力之上叠加执行感知的进度监控与计划修正，可有效提升长时程操作的可靠性，且具备跨VLA模型的通用性。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have emerged as a promising paradigm for general-purpose robotic manipulation, yet their performance often degrades on long-horizon tasks that require multiple interdependent manipulations. Instead of burdening the VLA policies with long-horizon reasoning, memory, action generation, progress tracking, and error recovery, we argue for leveraging their short-horizon manipulation capabilities within an execution-aware framework. To this end, we introduce \textnormal{\textbf{RePlan-VLA}}, a VLA-agnostic framework that translates a VLA's short-horizon capabilities into reliable long-horizon execution. RePlan-VLA explicitly monitors the robot's motion for progress evaluation, and adaptively revises the future plan based on observed outcomes. We preserve the underlying VLA policy entirely, requiring no additional fine-tuning. Experiments on RoboCasa365 and CALVIN benchmarks show an improvement of 21.9% and 10.4% in success rate over the next strongest baseline. We also validate our approach for real-world deployment on a Franka Research 3 robot.

</details>


### [85] [Panini- Feasible Grammar For Robot Designs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23567)
*Mayukh Debnath,Manan Roy Choudhury*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种类型化产生式语法，结合案例角色关系、参数继承和基于特异性的规则仲裁来生成操作技能与机器人形态，并引入PANINI搜索方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 符号方法可解释但忽略连续几何；学习方法处理几何但结构不透明。目标是同时获得可解释性与几何协调性。

Method: 构建类型化产生式语法，通过几何实现映射将语法组合转换为SE(3)变换（继承未激活时为幺半群同态，激活时有可测缺陷）；并引入PANINI——基于语法的形态表示上的交叉熵搜索方法。

Result: 仲裁决策可从几何包含中恢复（一致率0.905±0.062 vs 欧氏控制0.611±0.090）；PANINI在30种方法24个种子中平均排名最佳但与强通用优化器无统计显著差异；语法生成设计天然合法，协调肢体放置产生95%联合可行编辑（对比55%和0%）。

Conclusion: 类型化语法能使机器人生成更可解释且几何协调，但不意味着所有可行设计都能运动，也不意味着基于语法的搜索普遍优于通用优化器。

Abstract: Symbolic methods make robot designs interpretable but often overlook continuous geometry; learned methods handle geometry but offer less transparent structure. We introduce a typed production grammar that uses case-role relations, parameter inheritance, and specificity-based rule arbitration to generate both manipulation skills and robot morphologies. A geometric realization map translates grammar composition into transformations in SE(3), behaving as a monoid homomorphism when inheritance is inactive and exhibiting a measurable defect when inheritance is enabled. The grammar provides a compact representation, and its arbitration decisions can be recovered from geometric containment (agreement 0.905 +/- 0.062 across 10 seeds, versus 0.611 +/- 0.090 for a Euclidean control). We also introduce PANINI, a cross-entropy search method over grammar-based morphology representations. Across 30 methods and 24 seeds, PANINI achieves the best mean rank but is not statistically distinguishable from strong generic optimizers. Its main demonstrated advantage is therefore structural rather than optimization performance: grammar-generated designs are legal by construction, and coordinated limb placement produces 95% jointly feasible edits, compared with 55% for range-constrained resampling and none for flat parameterization in 6,000 draws. These results show how typed grammars can make robot generation more interpretable and geometrically coordinated, without implying that every feasible design will locomote or that grammar-based search universally outperforms generic optimizers.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [86] [WorldRuler: Interactive World Modeling under Configurable Rules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24869)
*Mingliang Zhai,Chuanhao Li,Jiaming Tan,Zhen Li,Yuwei Wu,Yunde Jia,Zian Meng,Yuanyang Yin,Kaipeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: WorldRuler提出可执行3D规则图与闭环生成框架，解决用户自定义规则下的规则-状态持久化和规则-视觉实现两大难题，并构建RuleVerse数据集验证有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式世界模型只能遵循真实世界行为，无法满足用户自定义规则的可定制玩法需求；实现自定义规则面临规则-状态持久化与规则-视觉实现两个挑战，尤其当受影响对象尚未可见时难以准确呈现规则效果。

Method: 提出WorldRuler：用可执行3D规则图组织规则定义、依赖与对象状态，闭环连接规则执行、视频生成与感知反馈；支持规则与世界状态联合保存/恢复；动态对象绑定将规则实体锚定到视觉实例并用存储状态约束首次出现；引入对象属性状态替换与反事实重建训练；基于GTA-V和Habitat构建自动化数据采集标注流程，形成RuleVerse数据集。

Result: 大量实验表明WorldRuler在保存/加载过程中保持规则-状态一致性，并能准确在指定对象与属性上实现规则结果，包括规则先于对象可见时生效的情形。

Conclusion: WorldRuler通过规则图与闭环生成机制，实现了可定制、可持久且视觉准确的规则驱动交互世界生成。

Abstract: Rules govern how player actions change interactive worlds. Different rules create distinct gameplay experiences within similar scenes and action spaces. For customizable gameplay, generative world models must follow user-defined rules beyond real-world behaviors. However, realizing user-defined rules presents two challenges: Rule-State Persistence and Rule-to-Visual Realization. The former requires preserving rule definitions, dependencies, and world states across saving and loading, so that restored worlds follow the established rules. The latter requires accurately realizing rule-induced changes on designated objects and attributes, particularly when affected objects are unseen. In this paper, we propose WorldRuler, which organizes rule definitions, dependencies, and object states in an executable 3D rule graph and connects rule execution, video generation, and perceptual feedback in a closed loop. The graph supports joint saving and restoration of rules and world states, while dynamic object binding grounds rule-specified entities in visual instances and uses stored states to constrain generation from their first appearance. We further introduce object attribute state replacement and counterfactual reconstruction training, enabling accurate attribute control and compatible appearance and motion changes while limiting interference with unrelated content. We develop an automated data collection and annotation pipeline using GTA-V and Habitat to construct RuleVerse, a dataset of rule-driven interactions with detailed state and event annotations, including cases where rules take effect before affected objects become visible. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of WorldRuler in preserving rule-state consistency across saving and loading and accurately realizing rule outcomes on designated objects and attributes.

</details>


### [87] [F$^3$: What Makes Good Experience in World-Action Models?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24708)
*Zihe Huang,Sirui Zhou,Haozhi Han,Ruge Zhang,Teng Ma,Tao Ma,Zheng Liu,Ce Zhang,Ting Cao,Yunxin Liu,Yunquan Zhang,Yifeng Chen,Kun Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出F$^3$方法，通过Form、Fit、Fusion三个决策将成功与失败rollout统一压缩为操作记录，并选择性注入策略，使世界-动作模型能从自身经验中持续提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法把检索到的rollout或演示作为上下文提供给策略，但这类经验常导致已解决任务失败；因此需研究什么样的经验对世界-动作模型（WAM）有价值。

Method: F$^3$包含三部分：Form将成功和失败的rollout压缩为简短、经验证的操作记录；Fit训练策略读取记录并预测其何时有帮助；Fusion将记录中的图像、文本和动作作为前缀与动作先验注入策略。仅用200个样本训练一次，并配合不断增长的经验库部署。

Result: 在MolmoSpaces和RoboLab上达到SOTA，且未在RoboLab上训练；相比原生Cosmos 3 Nano，成功率分别提升12.0和10.8个百分点，成功所需步数减少12.2%和11.3%，回退减少57%。

Conclusion: 经验的价值取决于存储什么、何时使用以及从何处进入策略，而非源rollout是否成功；F$^3$通过统一利用成败经验显著提升WAM性能并减少回退。

Abstract: Deployed world-action models (WAMs) can keep improving only if they make good use of their own experience. Existing methods supply retrieved rollouts or demonstrations to the policy as context, yet such experience often turns previously solved tasks into failures. What, then, makes good experience for a WAM? We show that experience is valuable only in use: its value depends not on whether the source rollout succeeded but on what is stored, when it is used, and where it enters the policy. We therefore propose F$^3$, which makes these three decisions with Form, Fit, and Fusion. Form compresses successful and failed rollouts alike into short, verified operation records; Fit teaches the policy to read a record and to predict when it helps; Fusion injects the record's images, text and actions into the policy as a prefix and an action prior. Trained once on only 200 examples and deployed with a growing experience library, the model achieves state-of-the-art results on MolmoSpaces and RoboLab without any training on RoboLab: over native Cosmos 3 Nano, it raises success by 12.0 pp and 10.8 pp, reduces steps to success by 12.2% and 11.3%, and cuts regressions by 57%.

</details>


### [88] [Risk-Triggered Search: Behavior-Preserving Safe Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24409)
*Xinyu Deng,Bohao Lan,Kunpeng Li,Mingkai Yuan,Weihang Pan,Binbin Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 Risk-Triggered Search (RTS)，在冻结的 JEPA 世界模型中用动作条件未来隐表示前缀训练风险头，并以同一前缀风险同时作为搜索触发门与硬可行性门，仅在高风险时启动局部 CEM 搜索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练视觉世界模型虽能生成多步动作计划，但仅优化终端接近目标，无法防止穿越禁区或刚体碰撞滑移；纯视觉接口缺乏解析动力学、结构化状态与显式碰撞几何，需要内部预测动作条件下的过程风险。

Method: 在冻结 JEPA 世界模型中展开动作条件未来隐前缀，用过程违规监督训练轻量风险头；以预测前缀风险统一作为触发门和可行性门，安全名义计划直接执行，告警时触发局部 CEM 搜索以在可行集内最小化名义偏离；引入保幅度量 d_AP，保留切向进展且仅在名义计划处唯一取零，避免欧氏最小修改导致零幅停滞。

Result: 在五项任务的过程安全基准与 Safe Arrival Rate (SAR) 指标下，RTS 在导航与关节控制上较 Direct 提升 12.67–41.67 个百分点，在 Cube/PushT 边界队列提升 +2.08/+4.17 pp；在 1,968 条配对轨迹上与逐步同核搜索保持 100% 动作一致性，同时减少控制器时间 17.71%–70.41%。

Conclusion: 统一门控与唯一零条件保证绕过安全状态搜索的闭环轨迹与逐步搜索严格一致，RTS 在保持名义行为与动作一致性的同时显著提升过程安全性并降低搜索开销。

Abstract: Pre-trained visual world models generate multi-step action plans from current and goal observations, yet terminal-proximity objectives fail to prevent forbidden-zone traversal or rigid-body collision sliding. Purely visual interfaces may lack analytical dynamics, structured states, or explicit collision geometry, requiring internal prediction of action-conditioned process risk. We propose Risk-Triggered Search (RTS) for frozen Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture (JEPA) world models. Rather than relying on static state features that cannot distinguish candidate plans sharing an observation, RTS unrolls action-conditioned future latent prefixes in the frozen world model and trains a lightweight risk head with process-violation supervision. To intervene selectively while preserving safe nominal behavior, RTS uses the same predicted prefix-risk criterion as both the search trigger gate and hard feasibility gate: safe nominal plans execute directly, whereas alarms trigger local Cross-Entropy Method (CEM) search to minimize nominal deviation within the feasible set. To prevent isotropic Euclidean minimal modification from shrinking obstacle-directed actions into zero-amplitude stalling, we introduce an amplitude-preserving metric $d_{\mathrm{AP}}$ that retains tangential progression while maintaining a unique zero at the nominal plan. Under unified gating and this unique-zero condition, bypassing safe-state search yields closed-loop trajectories strictly identical to every-step same-kernel search. Evaluated under a process-safety benchmark and Safe Arrival Rate (SAR) metric across five tasks, RTS improves SAR over Direct by 12.67--41.67 percentage points on navigation and articulated control and +2.08/+4.17 pp on Cube/PushT boundary cohorts, maintaining 100% action identity with every-step search while cutting controller time by 17.71%--70.41% across 1,968 paired trajectories.

</details>


### [89] [FedE2E: One-Shot Federated Learning for End-to-End Autonomous Driving with Closed-Loop Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24254)
*Tao Lian,Antonio M. López*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种用于端到端自动驾驶的一次性联邦学习方法，通过多客户端校准、基于锚点的特征对齐和逐令牌共识蒸馏，在闭环评估中显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶联邦学习方法通常需要多轮通信且缺乏闭环评估，而一次性方法主要集中于感知任务，无法满足端到端驾驶需求。

Method: 提出一次性联邦学习方法，包含多客户端校准、基于锚点的特征对齐和逐令牌共识蒸馏，从异构模型中蒸馏知识。

Result: 在Bench2Drive闭环评估中，一次性设置下驾驶得分55.85，比先前工作高4.07分；多轮设置下五轮通信后达到60.35，在相同通信轮数下所有对比方法中表现最佳。

Conclusion: 该方法在一次性与多轮联邦学习设置下均有效，显著提升了端到端自动驾驶的闭环性能。

Abstract: Federated learning enables collaborative model training without sharing datasets. However, existing federated learning methods for autonomous driving typically require multi-round communication and lack closed-loop evaluation, while one-shot methods mainly focus on perception tasks. To address these limitations, we propose \method, a one-shot federated learning method for end-to-end autonomous driving. It comprises multi-client calibration, anchor-based feature alignment, and token-wise consensus distillation to distill knowledge from heterogeneous models. Closed-loop evaluation on Bench2Drive demonstrates the effectiveness of \method in both one-shot and multi-round federated learning. In the one-shot setting, it achieves a Driving Score of 55.85, which is 4.07 points higher than prior work. In the multi-round setting, it reaches 60.35 after five communication rounds, the best performance among all compared methods under the same number of communication rounds.

</details>


### [90] [Embodied Graph: A Relational World Model for Long-Horizon Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24115)
*Zongyuan Wu,Xin Wang,Tongtong Feng,Yuan Meng,Zeyang Zhang,Ling Feng,Wenwu Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出关系世界模型（RWM），通过在图状态抽象上进行想象而非像素级观测，解决长程规划中误差累积问题，并在Minecraft和Crafter上验证有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型依赖密集像素级观测，被迫吸收冗余信息且忽略任务相关实体间关系，导致长程想象中误差累积并逐渐破坏预测，因此需要探索在稀疏状态抽象上进行想象的世界模型。

Method: 提出体现图（Embodied Graph）作为关系状态抽象，以及关系世界模型（RWM）直接在图上进行想象。RWM包含两个组件：(i) 任务感知体现图构造器，过滤冗余细节并通过在线更新维护图记忆；(ii) 体现图循环状态空间模型，学习节点级潜在动态并捕捉节点间依赖关系。

Result: 在Minecraft和Crafter上的实验证明RWM在体现环境中的有效性；在Minecraft中，RWM是唯一在给定预算内获得钻石的方法；网络交互实验进一步表明关系状态预测适用于网络智能体的决策过程。

Conclusion: 基于稀疏图状态抽象的关系世界模型能够支持结构化动态建模和长程想象，为长程决策任务提供可靠的行动后果预测，并可推广至网络智能体决策场景。

Abstract: Long-horizon tasks in embodied and web environments involve extended decision sequences and delayed feedback, making accurate planning crucial for success. World models tackle this by learning latent dynamics for imagination-based planning. However, existing approaches merely rely on dense pixel-level observations, which forces the model to absorb redundant information while overlooking relations among task-relevant entities. Consequently, small errors may accumulate over long imagined rollouts and gradually corrupt the predictions. In this paper, we explore world models that imagine over sparse state abstractions, which poses two key challenges: 1) how to remove redundant information and form a compact state abstraction; 2) how to incorporate the relational structure to learn reliable long-horizon dynamics. To address these challenges, we propose Embodied Graph, a relational state abstraction that supports structured dynamics modeling and long-horizon imagination, and the Relational World Model (RWM), which performs imagination directly over graph states. RWM consists of (i) a Task-Aware Embodied Graph Constructor that filters redundant details and maintains graph memory through online updates, and (ii) an Embodied Graph Recurrent State Space Model that learns node-wise latent dynamics while capturing inter-node dependencies. The imagined graph states provide predictions of action consequences to guide decision-making. Experiments on Minecraft and Crafter demonstrate the effectiveness of RWM in embodied environments. Notably, in Minecraft, RWM is the only method that obtains diamonds within the given budget. Experiments on web interaction further illustrate the applicability of relational state prediction to the decision making process of web agents.

</details>


### [91] [Attention Is Not Relevance: Challenging the Causality of Attention in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24051)
*Luca Lusvarghi,Javier Gozalvez*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究通过反事实分析验证，注意力机制在自动驾驶中提供的是物体相关性的关联性而非因果性指示，其准确性在复杂场景下显著下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 注意力机制广泛用于估计周围物体对自动驾驶决策的相关性，但其能否捕捉单个输入元素对模型输出的因果影响在NLP领域已有争议，本文将该讨论引入自动驾驶领域。

Method: 基于PlanT 2.0（一种基于Transformer的先进物体级规划模型），在超过80,000个合成驾驶场景中，将多种基于注意力的相关性估计方法与通过反事实分析获得的物体相关性因果参考进行对比。

Result: 注意力提供的是相关性的关联性而非因果性指示；基于注意力的方法无法一致地识别所有相关物体，且倾向于将影响很小或无影响的物体错误归类为相关；注意力在简单的单车道直路场景中能有效识别相关物体，但在多车道道路和交叉口等复杂场景中准确性下降。

Conclusion: 注意力作为物体相关性的代理存在局限性，需要能够准确捕捉单个物体对车辆驾驶的因果影响并将其与关联性关联区分开来的新方法。

Abstract: Attention has become one of the most widely adopted solutions for estimating the relevance of surrounding objects and interpreting an autonomous vehicle's driving decisions. However, its ability to capture the causal impact of individual input elements on an AI model's output has long been discussed in the literature, particularly in the Natural Language Processing (NLP) domain. This work brings this discussion into the autonomous driving domain by analyzing the accuracy of attention in estimating the impact of an input object on an autonomous vehicle's driving, i.e., its relevance. To this end, we compare different attention-based relevance estimation methods against a causal reference of object relevance obtained through counterfactual analysis. We perform our analysis on more than 80,000 synthetic driving scenes using an autonomous vehicle based on PlanT 2.0, a state-of-the-art Transformer-based model for object-level planning. Results show that attention provides a correlational rather than causal indication of object relevance. Attention-based relevance estimation methods do not consistently identify all relevant objects and tend to incorrectly classify as relevant objects with little or no impact on the vehicle's driving. In particular, results show that attention can effectively identify relevant objects in simple single-lane, straight-road scenarios, but its accuracy degrades in more complex settings, such as multi-lane roads and intersections. These findings highlight the limitations of attention as a proxy for object relevance, calling for novel methods able to accurately capture the causal impact of individual objects on the vehicle's driving and distinguish it from correlational associations.

</details>


### [92] [Vanguard: Knowing When to Trust the Draft in Speculative Vision-Language Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23917)
*Hanqi Mao,Jiajun Fan,Ruihan Guo,Shuaike Shen,Ziwen Wang,Hui Ren,Xianrui Zhong,Ge Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Vanguard提出一种无需训练的判别器，在截图辅助下对小型模型草稿步骤进行评分，决定是否交由大模型生成，从而在视觉语言智能体任务中实现更准、更快的步骤级推测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言智能体每步都调用大模型导致高延迟与高成本；现有步骤级推测依赖仅读文本的判别器，无法判断步骤是否匹配屏幕内容。

Method: Vanguard使用通用决策模型结合截图，对每个候选步骤单次前向给出正确概率；超过阈值则保留最佳草稿，否则由大模型生成，草稿与目标模型均不改动。

Result: 在Web、移动与具身智能体上，2B草稿经Vanguard判别后准确率不低于8B目标模型，在Mind2Web和EB-ALFRED上显著更高，目标模型仅写少数步骤；完成更多闭环回合，跨家族27B目标下更快且更准；是最准的判别器，阈值可迁移到未见网站。

Conclusion: Vanguard将推测从节省大模型计算转变为可超越大模型表现的方法，可与token级推测解码组合，并在数学任务上匹配训练的过程奖励模型。

Abstract: Vision-language agents that browse websites, operate phones and act in homes reason before every action, so every step waits for a large model, a latency and cost that stand in the way of deployment. Step-level speculation lets a small model draft each step and calls the large model only on rejected drafts, but its outcome rests on the judge, and a judge that reads only a step's text cannot tell whether the step fits the screen it acts on. We find that a few samples of a small model can contain a correct step about as often as the large model gets the step right, and not always on the same steps: checked against the screen, small drafts can be not only cheaper but better. We present Vanguard, a training-free judge that leaves the draft and target models unchanged: a general decision model, with the screenshot in view, gives each candidate step a probability of being right in one forward pass; the best candidate is kept if it clears a threshold, and otherwise the large model writes the step. On web, mobile and embodied agents, a 2B draft judged by Vanguard is at least as accurate as its 8B target and significantly more accurate on Mind2Web and EB-ALFRED, while the target writes only a minority of the steps; it completes far more closed-loop episodes, and with a 27B target from another family it is both faster and more accurate. Vanguard is the most accurate judge we compare on the agent tasks; it relies on the screenshot on web and mobile tasks, its calibrated threshold transfers to unseen websites, and across model families its gain over the target grows with the draft's pass@4 relative to the target's accuracy. Vanguard composes with token-level speculative decoding and matches trained process reward models on mathematics, turning speculation from a way to save the large model's work into one that can improve on it.

</details>


### [93] [RoboBuilder: Agentic Robot Co-Design with Real-world Video Feedback in the Loop](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23855)
*Ghadi Nehme,Dahyun Nam,Akshay Govind Srinivasan,Faez Ahmed*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RoboBuilder 基准测试编码智能体能否协同设计机器人硬件与程序，让设计在物理赛道通过验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有仿真不足以代表真实世界，需评估 AI 智能体设计可实际运行机器人的能力。

Method: 提出 RoboBuilder 基准：智能体用标准零件、电机和定制 3D 打印件协同设计硬件与程序；仿真通过的设计在物理赛道建造测试，并允许智能体通过请求测量和观察真实运行视频学习模拟器。

Result: 评估四个前沿编码智能体、三个难度十种障碍，共 16,427 次仿真（12,774 个不同机器人）与约 240 小时建造的 135 个物理机器人；智能体几乎解决所有简单障碍并发明基线未达的机制，但较难级别、单机器人跨越全部障碍及真实世界表现不足，仿真通过率在真实世界仅 66% 成立；展示真实失败视频后智能体重新设计，消除 72% 的一、二级失败。

Conclusion: RoboBuilder 衡量智能体向能工程化真实机器迈进的进展，闭环反馈可显著提升真实世界成功率。

Abstract: Can AI agents design robots that work in the real world? We introduce RoboBuilder, a benchmark in which coding agents co-design a robot's hardware and program from standard parts, motors and a custom 3D-printed part, and every design that passes in simulation is built and tested on a physical course. Simulation alone is not enough: under public part data, none of 15 initial passing designs crossed the real course, so an agent learns the simulator from measurements it requests and videos of real runs. Across four frontier coding agents, ten obstacles at three difficulty levels, 16,427 simulations of 12,774 distinct robots and 135 physical robots built in an estimated 240 hours, agents solve nearly all easy obstacles and invent mechanisms our search baselines do not reach, but falter at harder levels, when one robot must cross every obstacle, and in the real world, where 66% of simulated passes hold across all levels. Closing the loop helps: shown a video of its real failure, an agent redesigns the robot, sometimes correcting its simulator, and removes 72% of level-1 and level-2 failures. RoboBuilder measures progress toward agents that engineer real machines. Project Page: this https URL

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [94] [Reconstruction vs. Prediction Pre-Training of Discrete Video Representations for Continuous Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24781)
*Ron Kosova,Francesco Croce*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究在容量匹配下比较自编码与V-JEPA预测目标对视频编码器表征动态的影响，发现二者在状态保真度与长程预测能力间存在权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自监督视频编码器常以像素重建或潜特征预测为目标训练，但这些目标如何塑造用于下游动态任务的表征尚不清楚。

Method: 在连续控制环境中，使用两个相同视频编码器共享FSQ瓶颈，分别以自编码和V-JEPA目标训练；提出非对称离散化方案使预测模型也使用相同瓶颈；并通过结构探针分析表征。

Result: 自编码器保留更多物理状态信息且单步潜预测略优；预测编码器在自回归滚动中误差累积更少，且对低层视觉扰动更鲁棒、更依赖时间上下文。

Conclusion: 预测与重建目标诱导出不同的动态表征，在状态保真度与可能利于长程预测的特性之间形成权衡。

Abstract: Self-supervised video encoders are commonly trained to either reconstruct pixels or predict latent features, yet it remains unclear how these objectives shape representations for downstream dynamics. We present a capacity-matched study isolating this choice: two identical video encoders with a shared FSQ bottleneck are trained under autoencoding and V-JEPA objectives on a continuous control environment. We introduce a simple asymmetric discretization scheme to apply the same bottleneck to the predictive model. The resulting representations exhibit a clear trade-off: the autoencoder preserves substantially more physical state information and achieves slightly better one-step latent prediction, while the predictive encoder accumulates less error under autoregressive rollout. Structural probes suggest that this advantage is associated with greater robustness to low-level visual perturbations and stronger dependence on temporal context. These results indicate that predictive and reconstructive objectives induce distinct representations of dynamics, trading state fidelity for properties that may benefit long-horizon prediction.

</details>


### [95] [Latent Action Policies from Suboptimal Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24482)
*Donghyeon Ki,Xiaoyu Chen,Jun Ho Seo,Hayeong Lee,Kyungyoon Kim,Byung-Jun Lee,Li Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LAPIS 提出一种目标条件潜在策略改进方法，通过在观测到的状态-潜在动作转移上接地价值优化，并用条件流策略建模多模态结构，从而在无动作视频学习的潜在动作空间中实现可靠的离线策略改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有潜在动作模型（LAMs）仅用行为克隆训练潜在策略，缺乏显式策略改进目标；而标准离线强化学习无法直接迁移到学习到的潜在动作空间，因为潜在动作结构会导致不可物理执行的动作以及不同物理控制被合并的问题。

Method: 作者推导了策略改进能迁移到解码执行以及潜在最优与物理最优对齐的条件；基于此提出 LAPIS，将基于价值的策略改进锚定在观测到的状态-潜在动作转移上，并使用条件流策略捕捉其多模态结构。

Result: LAPIS 在大多数任务上优于基线，在视觉操作任务中提升最明显；仅使用 1% 动作标签时仍有效，而对比方法基本崩溃。

Conclusion: 通过理解潜在空间优化的有效性条件并采用接地价值改进与多模态流策略，LAPIS 能够在潜在动作空间中实现稳健的策略改进，并显著降低对动作标签的依赖。

Abstract: Latent Action Models (LAMs) learn control representations from action-free video, but existing pipelines typically train latent policies with behavioral cloning, without an explicit policy improvement objective. Although offline RL offers a natural approach to policy improvement, standard methods do not transfer directly to learned latent action spaces. We find that the structure of learned latent actions makes naive offline RL unreliable: directly maximizing value over latent actions or fitting a unimodal policy can produce latent actions that cannot be realized by physical execution, while the latent representation can merge physical controls that receive different rewards. To understand when latent-space optimization remains valid, we derive conditions under which policy improvement transfers to decoded execution and under which latent and physical optima align. Building on these insights, we propose LAPIS for goal-conditioned latent policy improvement, grounding value-based policy improvement in observed state-latent transitions and using a conditional flow policy to capture their multimodal structure. LAPIS outperforms baselines on most tasks, with the clearest gains in visual manipulation, and remains effective even with only 1% action labels, where competing methods largely collapse.

</details>


### [96] [Adaptive Action Horizon Selection via Flow-Field Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24178)
*Idan Ben-Ami,Arnon Netzer,Sarah Keren,Idit Diamant*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DUAC提出一种事后方法，从预训练VLA模型的流向量场中提取轨迹条件预测不确定性，仅需单个生成动作块和雅可比向量积即可获得动作级不确定性评分，无需重训练或多次采样，并用于自适应动作块长度选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自适应动作分块（AAC）方法通过多次采样动作块估计动作熵，带来额外推理成本，且混淆了轨迹内不确定性与不同策略间多样性；需要一种低成本、能区分局部执行不确定性的不确定性估计方法。

Method: 基于闭式流后验协方差公式，利用雅可比向量积从预训练VLA的流向量场中提取轨迹条件预测不确定性，单次生成即得动作级评分，作为事后方法无需重训练或多样本端点采样，并据此进行自适应horizon选择。

Result: 在LIBERO、LIBERO-Pro、LIBERO-Plus和RoboCasa上，基于DUAC的自适应horizon选择在评估方法中表现较好，兼顾高任务成功率与低不确定性估计延迟；DUAC与AAC强相关但捕获不同信息，对有效但多样的动作策略不敏感。

Conclusion: 流向量场可作为高效、轨迹条件的预测不确定性来源，用于机器人决策中的自适应执行。

Abstract: Reliable uncertainty estimates are important for adaptive execution in vision-language-action (VLA) models. Existing approaches such as Adaptive Action Chunking (AAC) estimate action entropy from multiple sampled action chunks, introducing additional inference cost and capturing both uncertainty within an action trajectory and diversity across alternative strategies. We introduce Divergence-based Uncertainty-aware Adaptive Action Chunking (DUAC), a post-hoc method that extracts trajectory-conditioned predictive uncertainty from the vector field of a pretrained VLA. Building on a closed-form flow-posterior covariance formulation, DUAC derives action-level uncertainty scores from a single generated chunk using Jacobian-vector products, without retraining or multiple endpoint samples. DUAC strongly correlates with AAC, while capturing distinct information: AAC measures global dispersion across sampled chunks, whereas DUAC measures local uncertainty around the trajectory selected for execution. Consequently, DUAC is less sensitive to diversity among distinct but valid action strategies. Across LIBERO, LIBERO-Pro, LIBERO-Plus, and RoboCasa, DUAC-based adaptive horizon selection performs favorably among the evaluated methods, combining strong task success with low uncertainty-estimation latency. These results establish the flow vector field as an efficient, trajectory-conditioned source of predictive uncertainty for robot decision making.

</details>


### [97] [Counterfactual World Models: JEPAs That Reason About "What If"](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24778)
*Randall Balestriero,Yann LeCun,Kerda Varaku*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 世界模型无法仅凭事实数据回答反事实问题，但利用“自然不跳跃”性质，无需反事实数据或重训练即可构建反事实世界模型，并在多个模拟器和冻结编码器上显著提升反事实预测精度与 hindsight 规划成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型在无法完全观测的环境中运行，隐藏扰动（如水流推船）会改变动作结果，需要回答在相同扰动下不同动作的反事实结果，以区分坏决策与坏运气。然而，仅用事实转移训练的世界模型无法独立回答该问题。

Method: 基于“自然不跳跃”性质，当动作与扰动作为独立力作用时，其交互在时间上是二阶的，因此反事实结果在一阶近似下等于观测结果加上世界模型在替代动作与事实动作下预测之差。由此构建无需反事实数据、额外参数或重训练的反事实世界模型。理论证明 O(h²) 误差界及匹配的极小极大下界，并刻画不连续、坐标折叠和扰动缩放命令时的失效情况。实验在 JEPA 世界模型上使用冻结的 DINOv2 和 MAE 编码器，从像素验证预测，并在四个渲染模拟器及水下航行器上评估，另用 DINOv3、SigLIP 2、CLIP 和 EVA-02 四个冻结编码器测试。

Result: 在四个渲染模拟器上，反事实 R² 从 38–46% 提升至 86–96%（同一世界模型）；第五个水下航行器上达到 90–94%（使用 16 倍事实数据）。四个额外冻结编码器在相同模拟器上给出 81–95%。在 hindsight 规划中，该方法在 35–69% 的事件中找到本可达到目标的动作，而知晓模拟器但不知结果的 oracle 规划器仅为 14–23%。

Conclusion: 反事实世界模型可通过利用物理系统的二阶交互性质，无需反事实数据或重训练即可从事实数据中有效构建，显著提升反事实预测和 hindsight 规划性能，并具有理论误差保证。

Abstract: World models operate in a world they cannot fully observe. When a hidden disturbance, such as a current pushing a boat, changes the outcome of an action, a counterfactual question follows: what would have happened under a different action, subject to the same disturbance? Its answer separates bad decisions from bad luck. We prove that world models trained on factual transitions cannot answer it on their own, whatever their data or capacity. For many physical systems, the observed outcome and a simple property of the dynamics suffice: natura non facit saltus, nature makes no leaps. When actions and disturbances act as separate forces, their interaction is second-order in time, so the counterfactual outcome is, to first order, the observed outcome plus the difference between the world model's predictions under the alternative and factual actions. This yields a counterfactual world model with no counterfactual data, extra parameters, or retraining. For transitions of duration $h$, we prove an $O(h^2)$ bound on the counterfactual error relative to the disturbance variance, establish a matching minimax lower bound from unpaired factual data, and characterize failures at discontinuities, coordinate folds, and disturbances that scale the command. We validate these predictions from pixels with JEPA world models on frozen DINOv2 and masked autoencoder (MAE) encoders. On four rendered simulators, our approach improves counterfactual $R^2$ from $38$--$46\%$ to $86$--$96\%$ with the same world models; on a fifth, an underwater vehicle, it reaches $90$--$94\%$ with sixteen times more factual data. Four further frozen encoders, DINOv3, SigLIP~2, CLIP and EVA-02, give $81$--$95\%$ on the same simulators. In hindsight planning, it finds the action that would have reached the goal in $35$--$69\%$ of events, against $14$--$23\%$ for an oracle planner that knows the simulator but not the outcome.

</details>


<div id='cs.CV'></div>

# cs.CV [[Back]](#toc)

### [98] [NereidVLA: Learning to Understand and Navigate the Underwater World](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24864)
*Chenjie Zhao,Zhiliang Zhu,Quan Chen,Yuquan Xue,Xingxue Shi,Puyue Wang,Zhiqiang Zhao,Kangye Zheng,Boyang Xu,Mingwei Lin,Dejun Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: NereidVLA将海洋视觉-语言知识迁移到语言条件三维水下导航，先以Sea2Know学习空间知识，再用专家轨迹适配，实现闭环跟踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下导航需将视觉空间线索转化为运动，但现有海洋视觉-语言数据集缺少配对的机器人轨迹，难以支撑动作学习。

Method: 先在大约两百万海洋样本上训练Sea2Know以学习目标定位、空间关系和场景结构，无需配对机器人运动监督；随后用模拟水下航行器动力学下的专家轨迹进行适配；给定RGB历史与语言指令，NereidVLA预测离散运动令牌并解码为局部三维轨迹用于闭环跟踪。

Result: 在匹配的导航训练与执行设置下，海洋空间预训练将仿真成功率从72.4%提升至84.4%；未在真实测试环境微调时，实验室水池机器人试验成功率为78.75%，对比InternVLA-N1的47.50%。

Conclusion: 海洋空间监督能够支持下游运动学习与真实水下物理导航。

Abstract: Underwater navigation requires translating visual spatial cues into motion, yet marine vision-language datasets rarely include paired robot trajectories. We present NereidVLA, a vision-language-action model that transfers marine spatial knowledge to language-conditioned 3D navigation. We first train Sea2Know on approximately two million marine examples to learn target grounding, spatial relations, and scene structure without paired robot-motion supervision. We then adapt it using expert trajectories executed under simulated underwater vehicle dynamics. Given RGB histories and a language instruction, NereidVLA predicts discrete motion tokens that decode into local 3D trajectories for closed-loop tracking. Evaluation combines an independent six-dimensional underwater navigation understanding benchmark with offline prediction and closed-loop navigation. Under matched navigation training and execution settings, marine spatial pretraining increases simulation success from 72.4% to 84.4%. Without fine-tuning on the real test environment, NereidVLA achieves 78.75% success in laboratory-pool robot trials, compared with 47.50% for InternVLA-N1. These results show that marine spatial supervision can support downstream motion learning and physical underwater navigation.

</details>


### [99] [HandWorldBench: A Unified Benchmark for Hand-Centric World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24731)
*Zhaolu Wang,Jiacheng Xu,Lihong Wang,Shuqing Wang,Jianjie Fang,Ziyou Wang,Xvyuan Liu,Rongze Tang,Yiding Ma,Xin Zhang,Jiawei Chen,Wei Wu,Chen Gao,Yong Li,Zhibo Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出HandWorldBench基准，系统评估以手为中心的世界模型在人类手、仿真灵巧手和真实灵巧手上的六维度表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型在手-物交互这一高要求场景中潜力巨大，但针对手部核心能力的系统评估尚不完善。

Method: 构建数据筛选流程，从600多小时视频中提取并筛选出20500个高质量自我中心交互片段，人工选取覆盖七类任务的500个片段，并在统一协议下对十一个模型进行六维度评估。

Result: 更强的手部姿态控制并不稳定转化为连贯的物体状态演化与持久性，也不保证稳健的跨具身泛化；部分专用手部控制模型在灵巧手任务上不如通用文本/图像到视频生成器。

Conclusion: 手中心世界模型的评估需超越姿态控制，兼顾物体状态演化和跨具身能力，HandWorldBench为此提供了统一评测基础。

Abstract: Recent advances in world models are opening new possibilities for fine-grained embodied tasks, with hand-object interaction offering a particularly demanding setting. Modeling these interactions involves capturing how hand actions change objects and how the resulting states persist over time. Yet systematic evaluation tailored to these hand-centric capabilities remains underdeveloped. To address this gap, we introduce HandWorldBench, a unified benchmark that systematically evaluates hand-centric world models across human hands, simulated dexterous hands, and real dexterous hands along six dimensions: visual quality, hand fidelity, appearance consistency, instruction following, physical consistency, and temporal coherence. To construct the benchmark, we develop a data curation pipeline that extracts hand-object interaction clips and screens them for quality, yielding 20,500 high-quality egocentric clips from over 600 hours of footage. We then manually select 500 clips covering seven task families for evaluation. Benchmarking eleven models under a unified protocol reveals that stronger hand-pose control does not consistently translate into coherent object-state evolution and persistence or robust cross-embodiment performance, with several specialized hand-control models underperforming general-purpose text/image-to-video generators on dexterous-hand tasks.

</details>


### [100] [DRIFT: Detachable Reasoning for Runtime-Flexible and Efficient End-to-End Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24719)
*Emad Aghajanzadeh,Bethany Anne Wong,Leonhard Kunze,Moritz Thoma,Maximilian Forstenhäusler,Nael Fasfous,Manoj Rohit Vemparala,Pierpaolo Mori,Christos Anagnostopoulos,Daniel Mueller-Gritschneder,Ulf Schlichtmann*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DRIFT is a lightweight end-to-end autonomous driving framework that integrates a compact language model (<150M parameters) via efficient projection of driving representations into the language embedding space, enabling strong scene description and action commentary, with a runtime-flexible planning module that can bypass the LM using distilled surrogate reasoning features without performance loss.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLMs in end-to-end autonomous driving offer scene description and reasoning but are computationally expensive, and their runtime on/off switching without harming closed-loop performance has not been demonstrated, hindering practical adoption.

Method: DRIFT projects intermediate driving representations into the language embedding space to drive a compact LM for visual question answering and action commentary, and trains a runtime-flexible planning module on surrogate reasoning features distilled from the LM so inference can condition on either the LM or the surrogate features, allowing the LM to be bypassed under deployment constraints.

Result: With only 255M parameters, DRIFT achieves up to a Driving Score of 97.03 and a Success Rate of 89.39% (mean over three runs) on Bench2Drive by interleaving its LM and surrogate reasoning at a chosen reasoning rate, on par with the strongest non-language models while far more compact than VLM-based models; it also achieves competitive results on NAVSIM and AlpaSim at significantly lower computational cost.

Conclusion: A lightweight LM with efficient representation projection and distillable surrogate reasoning can deliver strong language-enabled driving while preserving closed-loop performance and enabling flexible runtime bypass, making VLM-style capabilities practical for end-to-end autonomous driving.

Abstract: The integration of vision-language models (VLMs) into end-to-end (E2E) autonomous driving has demonstrated strong potential by providing scene description and reasoning capabilities. However, VLMs are computationally expensive, and switching language reasoning on and off at runtime without hurting closed-loop performance has not been demonstrated, which hinders practical adoption. To address these issues, we propose DRIFT, an E2E autonomous driving framework that integrates a lightweight language model (LM) for visual question answering and action commentary. By efficiently projecting intermediate driving representations into the language embedding space, we demonstrate that a compact LM (<150M parameters) can achieve strong scene description and action commentary. Furthermore, we introduce a runtime-flexible planning module that is trained on surrogate reasoning features distilled from the LM and can be conditioned on either the LM or the surrogate reasoning features at inference. This design enables bypassing the LM entirely when required by deployment constraints, without sacrificing driving performance. With only 255M parameters, DRIFT reaches up to a Driving Score (DS) of 97.03 and a Success Rate of 89.39% (mean over three runs) on Bench2Drive by interleaving its LM and surrogate reasoning at a chosen reasoning rate. This is on par with the strongest non-language models while being far more compact than existing VLM-based models. DRIFT further achieves competitive results on the real-world NAVSIM and AlpaSim benchmarks at significantly lower computational cost than existing VLM-based approaches. Project page: this https URL

</details>


### [101] [Improving Zero-shot 3D Semantic Mapping in Offroad with Geometric Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24696)
*Eerik Alamikkotervo,Risto Ojala*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于球度-高程分布几何先验的方法，融合视觉基础模型预测以提升非结构化越野环境下的语义3D建图性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前视觉基础模型在结构化场景零样本语义预测准确，但在自然环境中性能下降；而3D点云包含有助于区分语义的信息，因此需融合二者以提升越野场景语义建图。

Method: 为每个语义类别拟合球度-高程分布作为几何锚点，仅使用视觉基础模型预测置信度最高的点来拟合高质量几何先验分布，再将几何先验与视觉基础模型预测融合生成最终语义地图。

Result: 在RELLIS-3D数据集上，所提先验使SAM3基线mIoU提升6.3、准确率提升8.9；在WildScenes数据集上mIoU提升1.7、准确率提升2.1。

Conclusion: 利用高置信度预测点拟合的球度-高程几何先验能有效融合视觉基础模型，显著提升非结构化环境语义3D建图的精度与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Semantic 3D mapping is a powerful representation that enables platform agnostic mobile robot navigation in challenging offroad conditions. Current vision foundation models can accurately predict semantics zero-shot on structured scenes but performance is compromised in natural environments. On the other hand, 3D point clouds encode valuable information for discriminating semantics in scenarios where vision foundation models fail. In this work, we propose fitting a sphericity-elevation distribution for each semantic class to act as geometric anchor. Only the points with the most confident vision foundation model predictions are considered when fitting to produce high quality geometric prior distributions. The geometric prior is fused with the vision foundation model prediction to produce the final semantic mapping. The proposed prior improves SAM3 baseline by +6.3 in mIoU and +8.9 in accuracy on the RELLIS-3D dataset, and by +1.7 in mIoU and +2.1 in accuracy on the WildScenes dataset. The source code will be made public upon paper acceptance.

</details>


### [102] [ALIVE: Bring Foundation Features to Life with Events](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24579)
*Rong Zou,Haiqing Hao,Davide Scaramuzza*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ALIVE是一种基于事件的方法，利用后续事件流增量更新冻结的DINOv3编码器特征，无需重新编码图像即可保持视觉基础模型特征的新鲜度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉基础模型特征在图像观测间会因内容变化而过时，刷新需新帧和重复运行高计算成本图像编码器。

Method: 给定锚图像，ALIVE用后续事件传输和校正冻结DINOv3编码器提取的多层特征；轻量更新器仅用合成数据端到端训练，无下游任务监督，直接迁移到真实事件流。

Result: 在三个真实数据集和五个任务-数据集设置（目标检测、语义分割、深度估计）中，ALIVE在所有指标上优于所有评估的基于事件方法；DSEC上锚帧200ms时，检测mAP提升30.9，分割mIoU提升11.6，深度AbsRel提升15.9%；相比图像重编码，FLOPs减少82.4%，延迟降低2.5倍。

Conclusion: ALIVE能高效增量更新基础模型特征，显著提升下游任务性能并大幅降低计算开销。

Abstract: Visual foundation models provide powerful representations for diverse tasks, but their features become stale as visual content changes between image observations. Refreshing them requires new frames and repeated runs of a computationally expensive image encoder. We introduce ALIVE, an event-based method that incrementally updates native foundation model features between image observations. Given an anchor image, ALIVE uses subsequent events to transport and correct existing multi-layer features extracted by a frozen DINOv3 encoder. Its lightweight updater is trained end-to-end using only synthetic data, without downstream task supervision, and transfers directly to real-world event streams without any adaptation. Across three real-world datasets and five task-dataset settings spanning object detection, semantic segmentation, and depth estimation, ALIVE outperforms all evaluated event-based methods in every metric. On DSEC, when the anchor frame is 200,ms old, ALIVE improves over anchor-image predictions by 30.9,mAP in detection, 11.6,mIoU in segmentation, and 15.9% in depth AbsRel. Compared with image re-encoding, ALIVE reduces FLOPs by 82.4% and latency by a factor of 2.5.

</details>


### [103] [Comparing Each Generation with Independent Siblings Gives Video World Model Post-Training a Proper Objective for Outcome Frequencies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24512)
*Xin Li,Yiwen Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SibPO提出一种基于兄弟样本配对策略优化的物理后训练方法，通过截断逆频率加权使期望目标成为严格proper散度，在PisaBench上降低KS距离9.1-10.2%并降低机器人策略成功率估计误差13%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理后训练用单一记录未来打分使单视频更忠实，但期望分数对模型分布线性，最大化时总产生最可能结果，导致重复生成无法按数据中出现频率产生各物理结果。

Method: 利用同一历史下独立噪声生成的兄弟样本估计截断逆频率权重，加权与记录的一致性，并减去同权重乘以与兄弟样本的一致性，使期望目标在每个组大小下成为结果单元上的严格proper散度，即Sibling-paired Policy Optimization。

Result: 在PisaBench上，四个视频模型中的三个相对其奖励族外最强方法将模拟着陆时间的KS距离降低9.1-10.2%，且不降低单视频质量；估计机器人策略成功率的误差降低13%。

Conclusion: SibPO通过兄弟样本配对优化实现了对物理结果分布的正确建模，在不牺牲单视频保真度的前提下提升了多结果生成的分布匹配和下游策略评估准确性。

Abstract: Video world models are used as simulators, so when a first frame leaves several physical outcomes open, repeated generations should produce each outcome as often as it occurs in the data. Physics post-training scores every generation against the single recorded future of its history, which makes single videos more faithful, but the expected score is linear in the model distribution and is maximized by always producing the most likely outcome. The log-likelihood of the recorded outcome is maximized by the data distribution, and its gradient weights agreement with the record by the inverse of how often the model produces the generated outcome, a frequency that other generations of the same history with independent noise, its siblings, can estimate. To train with this weight from a finite group, we propose SibPO, which performs Sibling-paired Policy Optimization: siblings estimate a truncated inverse-frequency weight for agreement with the record, and a second term subtracts the same weight times the agreement with the siblings, which makes the expected objective a strictly proper divergence over outcome cells at every group size. On PisaBench, SibPO lowers the KS distance to simulated landing times by 9.1-10.2% relative to the strongest method outside its reward family on three of four video models without degrading single videos, and it lowers the error of estimated robot policy success rates by 13%.

</details>


### [104] [ShoWorld: Shape-Faithful Multi-Hypothesis Occupancy World Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24457)
*Zhiqiang Yan,Guangfa Wang,Yuan Wu,Jian Yang,Gim Hee Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ShoWorld通过分离运动与形状表示，提升占用世界模型对运动物体的预测，在Occ3D-nuScenes上达到最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有占用世界模型因运动不确定性和形状扭曲难以预测运动物体：单轨迹会平均出不合理路径，重复体素化会侵蚀或膨胀形状。

Method: 将运动与形状分开建模：运动预测器基于场景布局、邻近智能体和自车未来轨迹生成概率轨迹，各假设渲染为占用并按概率融合；形状组件从局部几何预测带占用概率和偏移的亚体素点，点随物体刚性运动，体素占用由至少一个占用点落入的概率决定。

Result: 在Occ3D-nuScenes上达到最优；使用真值占用和自车轨迹时，整体mIoU和IoU分别提升6.56和9.16点，动态类别上分别提升6.06和5.53点。

Conclusion: 分离运动与形状表示能有效缓解运动不确定性和形状扭曲，显著提升占用预测性能。

Abstract: Occupancy world models struggle to forecast moving objects due to motion uncertainty and shape distortion. A single trajectory can average plausible futures into an implausible path, and repeated re-voxelization can erode or inflate object shape even under correct motion. We introduce ShoWorld to address these challenges through separate motion and shape representations. The motion forecaster predicts probabilistic trajectories conditioned on scene layout, nearby agents and the future ego trajectory. Each hypothesis is rendered into occupancy and fused according to its probability to retain alternative futures. The shape component predicts sub-voxel points with occupancy probabilities and offsets from local geometry. These points move rigidly with the object, and voxel occupancy follows the probability that at least one occupied point falls within it. This representation reduces shape distortion under motion. ShoWorld achieves state-of-the-art results on Occ3D-nuScenes. With ground-truth occupancy and ego trajectory, it improves mIoU and IoU by 6.56 and 9.16 points overall and by 6.06 and 5.53 points on dynamic classes.

</details>


### [105] [EviScope: Evidence-Conditioned Sampling for Long-Range 3D Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24451)
*Mingyang Lyu,Yinqian Sun,Gongce Cao,Chujie Tang,Yuhao Zhang,Lu Jia,Qian Zhang,Bo Wang,Pin Lyu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EviScope是一个极简模块，利用雷达回波构建非学习鸟瞰证据场，让查询根据解码中心读取证据以调整径向采样范围并加权八帧；在TruckScenes上相对自身基线NDS变化+0.31，但mAP略有下降，而优于同参数量的几何条件器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 查询式相机-雷达检测器需要决定每个物体查询从哪些位置和帧采样，作者检验查询周围的雷达证据是否也应在150米范围内参与这些选择，因为每个距离带内物体级雷达回波计数与召回率相关。

Method: 构建当前帧雷达回波的非学习鸟瞰证据场，每个查询在其解码中心读取该场，用于缩放径向采样范围并加权八帧；模块仅增加5,450个参数（0.009%），计时无延迟增加；与同参数量的纯几何条件器在匹配种子下对比。

Result: 模块读取的证据随物体回波计数上升（Spearman 0.51），学习半径与证据强相关（四次运行ρ≈-0.91）；七组四轮配对中NDS变化+0.31±0.36、mAP变化-0.27±0.22；相比几何条件器在三个匹配种子上NDS高+1.25±0.72、mAP高+0.87±0.21，且每对mAP均更高；远距离增益未通过预指定留出测试。

Conclusion: 查询局部雷达证据能影响采样分配，但未带来稳定的端到端mAP提升；学习到的分配或推理时依赖不能替代匹配的端到端比较，需谨慎评估模块增益。

Abstract: Query-based camera-radar detectors choose where and over which frames each object query samples from the query's position or feature. We test whether radar evidence around the query should also set these choices at the 150 m range of MAN TruckScenes, where recall differs across object-level radar-return counts within every range band. EviScope is a minimal test: the current frame's returns form a non-learned bird's-eye evidence field, which each query reads at its decoded centre to scale its radial sampling extent and to weight eight frames. It adds 5,450 parameters (0.009%) to RaCFormer, with no latency increase in our timing. Our module-free recipe exceeds the published TruckScenes mAP (25.9 over seven runs against 24.1), so we measure the module only against its own baseline at matched seeds. The evidence a query reads rises with its object's return count (Spearman 0.51) but captures zero-return objects only in part, and the learned radius tracks it ($\rho\approx-0.91$ in four runs). Over seven four-epoch pairs the module changes NDS by $+0.31\pm0.36$ and mAP by $-0.27\pm0.22$ (mean $\pm$ SE). Against a geometry-only conditioner with the same parameter count, trained at three matched seeds, it is higher by $+1.25\pm0.72$ NDS and $+0.87\pm0.21$ mAP, on mAP in every pair, and the control's mean NDS is near the baseline's. An exploratory far-range gain failed an internally pre-specified held-out test, and large inference-time substitution penalties also occur without query-local evidence: neither learned allocation nor inference-time dependence substitutes for matched end-to-end comparisons.

</details>


### [106] [PhysResponse: Probing the Physics of Video Generation with Controlled Interventions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24419)
*Yuhan Li,Shuran Zhang,Haixin Tian,Baonuo Rao,Weipeng Li,Yujia Hou,Zhenyu Wang,Zijun Wang,Luoyang Lin,Lijing Luo,Jiehui Huang,Kaiyuan Yang,Jingbin Tan,Panwen Hu,Xiaodan Liang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PhysResponse基准，从场景条件模拟器反演和事件评估两个层面衡量视频生成模型对物理参数（重力、摩擦、初速度）的响应能力，并构建7.2万段受控模拟视频用于微调以改善该能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频生成模型被视为世界模拟器，不仅需要合理运动，还需正确响应指定物理参数，但现有模型、数据集和基准主要关注物理合理性，对物理参数响应性探索不足。

Method: 构建PhysResponse基准：第一层用场景条件模拟器反演恢复生成运动的有效重力、摩擦和初速度；第二层用事件评估测试临界转变附近及跨越时的结果。评估十个图生视频模型；并构建72,000段含20种多物体交互类型、参数受控的模拟视频用于微调，再加数值编码器继续训练。

Result: 最低参数误差仍比物理正确视频高一个数量级以上；模型很少同时复现转变对的两个结果；两层评估揭示互补失败，PSNR等视觉指标无法捕捉。微调后参数误差下降，且在留出交互类型、真实视频和外部物理基准上均有迁移；加数值编码器继续训练使留出类型上三项误差全部下降。

Conclusion: PhysResponse为遵循物理条件而非仅外观的世界模拟器提供了基础，表明物理参数响应性是现有模型的关键短板且可通过受控模拟数据训练改善。

Abstract: Video generation models are increasingly viewed as world simulators, which requires not only plausible motion but dynamics that respond correctly to specified physical parameters. Existing models, datasets and benchmarks, however, largely target physical plausibility, leaving this physical parameter responsiveness insufficiently explored. We introduce PhysResponse, a benchmark that measures it at two levels: scene-conditioned simulator inversion recovers the effective gravity, friction and initial speed of generated motion, and event evaluation tests outcomes near and across critical transitions. Evaluating ten image-to-video models, we find that even the lowest parameter errors exceed those of physically correct videos by over an order of magnitude, that models rarely reproduce both outcomes of a transition pair, and that the two levels expose complementary failures that visual metrics such as PSNR miss. To supply the missing training signal, we construct 72,000 simulated videos of 20 multi-object interaction types with controlled parameters; fine-tuning on them lowers parameter errors, even on held-out interaction types, and transfers to real videos and an external physics benchmark. Continued training with a numerical encoder further lowers all three errors on held-out types. PhysResponse provides a foundation for world simulators that follow physical conditions, not just appearances.

</details>


### [107] [StrideWorld: Benchmarking Scene Rigidity and Action Consistency in Interactive Video World Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24408)
*Chuang Wang,Rui Nie,Jie Song,Junyuan Gao,Jiahe Song,Haitao Zhou,Boyu Zhu,Xing Li,Jiang Wu,Conghui He,Qian Yu,Weijia Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出StrideWorld基准，用动作前固定的参考评估交互式视频世界模型的场景刚性与动作一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准的参考（重建场景或自由尺度轨迹）会吸收场景形变与节奏/长度变化，导致错误无法检测。

Method: 以起始视角的真值几何评估场景刚性；以每个动作（一条命令）在模型前几步测得的步幅作为参考来检验后续步骤；在已知相机运动的渲染视频上验证测量，并与人类判断比对。

Result: 在140个起始视角、多个开源模型生成的视频上，除一个模型外均出现场景形变，偏移达刚性渲染的7.3倍；单动作下多数模型节奏稳定，组合路线中多个模型停滞且终点偏离自身步幅预测；仅两个按估计场景深度缩放命令的模型使多数路线接近预测位置；微调两个模型可消除大部分中途停滞。

Conclusion: StrideWorld通过动作前固定参考有效暴露现有基准漏检的形变与不一致，并公开代码、数据和排行榜。

Abstract: Interactive video world models turn user commands into the exploration of a generated scene, which requires the scene to keep its 3D structure as the camera moves and each action to keep a stable effect. Existing benchmarks mostly evaluate these properties against references fitted to the video under test, either a scene reconstructed from its own frames or a camera trajectory aligned to the command path with a free scale. Such references can absorb gradual scene deformation and changes in pace and route length, so these errors can pass undetected. We therefore introduce StrideWorld, a benchmark whose references are fixed before the motion they judge. It evaluates scene rigidity against the ground-truth geometry of the start view, and action consistency against the stride of each action, the camera motion of one command. The stride is measured on the model's first steps and then used to test the steps that follow, so each model is judged only against its own steps. Its measurements are validated on rendered videos with known camera motion, and its scores agree with human judgments. Across \nvideos{} videos generated by \nmodels{} open models from $140$ start views, all models but one deform their scenes as the camera moves, with offsets up to $7.3$ times those of a rigid rendering along the same camera path. Most models keep a steady pace when a single action is held. Along composed routes, several models often stall, and most completed routes end away from the positions predicted by each model's own strides. Only the two models that scale commands by the estimated scene depth bring most routes close to these positions. Fine-tuning two models on a few rendered clips removes most of their mid-route stalls. Code, data, and a leaderboard are publicly available at this https URL .

</details>


### [108] [Pixel2Perspective: A Benchmark for Multi-Modal and Multi-View Generalization in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24327)
*Aditya Gandhamal,Aniruddh Sikdar,Arya Kishor,Suresh Sundaram*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Pixel2Perspective基准，用于多视角开放词汇语义分割评估，并设计MSRIQ框架量化RGB-IR不确定性以提升跨域鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身AI系统在未见物体环境中需提升感知，现有VLM和OVSS模型在实际部署中跨域泛化能力不足，缺乏多视角、多传感器及恶劣条件下的评估基准。

Method: 构建涵盖空中与地面视角、多传感器（含红外）、模拟到真实场景的Pixel2Perspective基准；评估CLIP等基础模型及先进OVSS模型；提出MSRIQ框架联合建模RGB与红外图像中的偶然不确定性和认知不确定性。

Result: 基准支持不同视角与传感器模态的全面评估，MSRIQ可跨多样环境条件稳健评估开放词汇分割模型，揭示模型在跨域泛化中的关键表现。

Conclusion: 工作建立了鲁棒性驱动的基准，为开放世界具身AI感知的未来研究提供关键洞察与基础。

Abstract: Embodied AI systems, such as drones and autonomous vehicles, improve navigation by enhancing perception in environments containing unseen objects. While Visual Language Models (VLMs) offer strong image understanding, their real-world application is underexplored.
