For effective deployment, open-vocabulary semantic segmentation (OVSS) models, leveraging models like CLIP to assign pixel-level labels from text, must be robust to outdoor conditions, adverse weather, and varying viewpoints. Despite their potential, these models encounter significant challenges in cross-domain generalization, hindering their practical use in embodied AI applications. In this work, we present Pixel2Perspective, a benchmark for multi-angle object localization and open-vocabulary segmentation across both aerial and ground perspectives. This benchmark supports thorough evaluation across different viewpoints and sensor modalities. We assess foundational models like CLIP and advanced OVSS models using a curated dataset that spans varied outdoor conditions, sim-to-real scenarios, and multiple sensors, including infrared, to support the development of robust and generalizable perception systems. We also present a novel framework, Multi-Source RGB-IR Uncertainty Quantification (MSRIQ), which jointly models aleatoric and epistemic uncertainties across RGB and infrared imagery. This approach supports robust evaluation of open-vocabulary segmentation models across diverse environmental conditions. Our work sets a robustness-driven benchmark, providing key insights and laying the foundation for future research in open-world embodied AI perception. The benchmark and project page are available at this https URL

</details>


### [109] [Allocentric Memory, Egocentric Acting: A Dual-Frame VLA for Urban Aerial Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24247)
*Haotian Xu,Yue Hu,Zhengqiu Zhu,Tuo Feng,Wenguan Wang,Quanjun Yin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出双帧视觉-语言-动作模型CityNav-VLA，结合动态自我中心感知与持久异中心空间记忆，实现城市语言目标无人机导航，并引入早期目标承诺（EGC）稳定远距离目标预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言目标空中导航需将动态自我中心观测与稳定位置锚定的空间证据关联，以支持长距离导航。

Method: 在固定北向上地图中增量累积视觉观测并初始化地理与地标先验；用指令、当前观测、无人机状态和运动历史条件化的动态查询检索记忆证据，预测地图坐标系目标；确定性控制器依据当前位姿将目标转换为机体坐标系指令；EGC将执行目标固定为前K次预测的坐标中位数，无额外可训练参数。

Result: 在Test Unseen上NE降低21.2%，SR和SPL分别提升15.8%和30.9%；数据集精炼后相对前SOTA，NE降低6.2%，SR和SPL分别提升5.6%和23.2%；累积消融显示导航地图特征增益最大，度量坐标进一步提升端点精度；4B骨干单张A100推理平均每次决策0.68秒，峰值内存8.6 GB。

Conclusion: CityNav-VLA通过双帧记忆与EGC有效提升城市语言目标无人机导航性能与效率。

Abstract: Language-goal aerial navigation requires a UAV to reach a destination specified in natural language using observations collected during flight. A key challenge is linking changing egocentric perception with spatial evidence anchored to stable locations for long-range navigation. We propose CityNav-VLA, a dual-frame vision-language-action model for urban navigation integrating dynamic egocentric context with persistent allocentric spatial memory. The UAV incrementally accumulates visual observations on a fixed north-up map initialized with geographic and landmark priors, preserving evidence across viewpoint changes. A dynamic query conditioned on the instruction, current observations, UAV state, and motion history retrieves relevant memory evidence to predict a map-frame target. A deterministic controller converts this target into body-frame commands using the UAV's current pose, connecting global reasoning with egocentric execution. To reduce target fluctuations over long horizons, early goal commitment (EGC) fixes the execution target to the coordinate-wise median of the first $K$ predictions without extra trainable parameters. On Test Unseen, CityNav-VLA reduces NE by 21.2% and improves SR and SPL by 15.8% and 30.9%, respectively. It also reduces NE by 6.2% and improves SR and SPL by 5.6% and 23.2%, respectively, relative to the former SOTA after dataset refinement. Cumulative ablations show that navigation-map features yield the largest gains, while metric coordinates further improve endpoint accuracy. With a 4B-scale backbone, inference averages 0.68 s per decision with 8.6 GB peak memory on a single A100, presenting remarkable model efficiency.

</details>


### [110] [Target-Consistent Action Guidance via Semi-Unbalanced Optimal Transport for Drifting World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24222)
*Md Abtahi Majeed Chowdhury,Md Rifat Ur Rahman,Md Sakib Hossain Shovon,Jaesik Choi,Minhyuk Sung*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文指出DriftWorld中借用无分类器引导的排斥当前帧机制存在目标偏置，提出Mass-Share Accentuation（MSA）通过半松弛最优传输消除该偏置，并在多个基准上提升动作排序与响应斜率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动作条件世界模型需要大量rollout用于规划和离线策略评估，多步扩散推理昂贵；漂移模型将迭代从推理移到训练实现单次生成。DriftWorld将漂移范式引入动作条件世界模型，并用排斥当前帧来增强动作跟随，但该构造继承自无分类器引导，存在目标偏置。

Method: 作者证明该排斥在预测已匹配记录未来时仍持续，存在于kernel、balanced和不平衡Wasserstein漂移场中。为此提出MSA：将当前帧视为与记录未来竞争的反事实目的地，构建半松弛最优传输问题，减去正确未来的闭式份额使引导在目标处消失，剩余部分由所有预测共享；并证明在坍缩预测下记录未来是运动方向上的唯一不动点，且引导随预测误差衰减。

Result: 在Push-T、Robomimic和BridgeV2上（训练模型表现为反应不足而非过冲），MSA在无额外推理成本下相比潜空间基线提升了动作排序、响应斜率和运动区域误差；但基准未隔离偏置消除作为原因。

Conclusion: MSA能消除DriftWorld式强调的目标偏置并改善动作条件世界模型的动作跟随质量，但需进一步实验确认性能提升确由偏置消除导致。

Abstract: Action-conditioned world models let robots imagine the outcomes of their actions for planning and offline policy evaluation, but these uses need many rollouts, which multi-step diffusion makes expensive. Drifting models move the iteration from inference to training and generate samples in one network pass. DriftWorld brought this paradigm to action-conditioned world models; to strengthen action following, it repels predictions from the current frame, a construction borrowed from classifier-free guidance. We show that this accentuation has a target bias: the push persists even when a prediction matches the recorded future, in kernel, balanced, and unbalanced Wasserstein drifting fields alike. We introduce Mass-Share Accentuation (MSA) to remove this bias. MSA treats the current frame as a counterfactual destination competing with the recorded future in a semi-relaxed optimal transport problem; subtracting the closed-form share of the correct future makes the guidance vanish at the target, and the remainder is shared by all predictions. We prove that the recorded future is the unique fixed point along the motion direction for collapsed predictions, and that the guidance fades with prediction error. On Push-T, Robomimic, and BridgeV2, where trained models under-react rather than overshoot, MSA improves action ranking, response slope, and moving-region error over latent-space baselines at no inference cost, though these benchmarks do not isolate bias removal as the cause.

</details>


### [111] [TrajWAM: Trajectory-Conditioned Future Learning for Vision-Language Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24192)
*Xuerui Fang,Gulimila Kezierbieke,Chen Wangxing,Hui Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TrajWAM是一种轨迹条件的世界动作模型，通过辅助BEV未来学习提升视觉语言驾驶规划性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界动作模型连接未来场景预测与动作规划，但如何利用每条驾驶记录中已执行轨迹与观测未来的配对关系来学习这一连接尚待研究。

Method: 提出TrajWAM：以自车轨迹查询条件化共享规划器表示，生成与DINOv3对齐的BEV未来预测；训练结合执行轨迹下的事实重建与无标签扰动轨迹下的单侧特征分离，共同训练共享规划器输出驾驶路点；推理时直接生成路点并跳过辅助预测分支。

Result: 在全部220条Bench2Drive路线上取得91.53驾驶分数和75.45%成功率，较调度匹配的纯规划微调基线提升5.31分并多成功8条路线。

Conclusion: 辅助BEV未来学习可有效增强轨迹条件化世界动作模型的规划能力，且推理时无需未来预测分支即可获得性能增益。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) connect future scene prediction with action planning for autonomous driving. Each driving record pairs an executed ego trajectory with its observed future, providing a factual outcome for learning this connection. We present TrajWAM, a trajectory-conditioned WAM that improves vision-language driving through auxiliary bird's-eye-view (BEV) future learning. An ego-trajectory query conditions DINOv3-aligned BEV predictions from shared planner representations. Training combines factual reconstruction under the executed trajectory with one-sided feature separation under an unlabeled perturbed trajectory. Together, these objectives train the shared planner to generate driving waypoints. Native inference directly generates waypoints and bypasses the auxiliary future-prediction branch. On all 220 Bench2Drive routes, TrajWAM achieves 91.53 Driving Score and 75.45% Success Rate, exceeding a schedule-matched planning-only fine-tuning baseline by 5.31 Driving Score points and eight successful routes.

</details>


### [112] [ART: Bridging the Embodiment Gap in 3D Human-to-Robot Affordance Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24182)
*Zichen Zhang,Wei Zhai,Xuji Zhang,Yang Cao,Yu Kang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出3D人-机器人可供性迁移任务与HPAD数据集，并设计ART网络将人类可供性先验转换为机器人可执行分布，在仿真与实物实验中验证有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将人类可供性先验作为直接参考，但可供性并非物体固有属性，而与执行者的具身结构耦合；人手与机器人末端执行器在结构和形态上存在差异，直接套用人类先验会导致严重的机器人执行不兼容。

Method: 构建首个大规模3D可供性基准HPAD（2,137个物体、80类、13种原子动作原语、超27,000个物体-动作对，每个物体同时标注人类先验与仿真验证的机器人标签）；提出3D可供性迁移网络ART，先通过可靠性门控流过滤人类先验中的几何假阳性，再经分层重建与图扩散细化重构为机器人可操作分布，以解决人机可供性间的拓扑错配。

Result: 在HPAD上的实验验证了所提任务的可靠性及ART的优越性；在6自由度机械臂配平行夹爪的2,400次执行基准（12个物体、4类动作）中，ART在其480次试验上取得最佳成功率86.5%。

Conclusion: 人-机器人可供性迁移范式具有实际可行性，ART能有效弥合不同具身结构导致的可供性错配，支撑机器人操作。

Abstract: 3D affordance acts as a critical link between perception and action, providing priors for robotic manipulation. While existing approaches predominantly leverage human priors as direct references, they often overlook that affordance is not an object-intrinsic property but is inherently coupled with the agent's embodiment. Due to the structural and morphological disparities between human hands and robot end-effectors, directly applying human priors causes severe incompatibility for robotic execution. Motivated by this, we introduce a novel task setting, 3D Human-to-Robot Affordance Transfer, which addresses the affordance misalignment arising from distinct embodiment structures. To support this task, we construct the Human-Robot Paired Affordance Dataset (HPAD), the first large-scale 3D affordance benchmark in which every object is annotated with both human-prior and simulation-validated robot labels. HPAD covers 2,137 objects across 80 categories and 13 atomic action primitives, yielding over 27,000 object-action pairs. On top of HPAD, we propose the 3D Affordance tRansfer neTwork (ART). ART first filters geometric false positives in the human prior via a reliability gating stream, then reconstructs the prior into a robot-actionable distribution through hierarchical reconstruction and a graph-diffusion refinement that resolves the topological mismatch between human and robot affordances. Experiments on HPAD demonstrate the reliability of the proposed task and the superiority of ART. We further validate ART on a 6-DoF arm with a parallel-jaw gripper in a 2,400-execution benchmark across 12 objects and 4 action categories; ART attains the best success rate (86.5% over its 480 trials), confirming the practicality of the transfer paradigm. Project page: this https URL

</details>


### [113] [Object Pose: The Link between Weak Perspective, Paraperspective, and Full Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24095)
*Radu Horaud,Fadi Dornaika,Bart Lamiroy,Stephane Christy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 对DeMenthon和Davis姿态估计方法进行了代数推导，提出迭代准透视姿态计算方法，分析收敛性并比较性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DeMenthon和Davis提出用弱透视模型迭代逼近透视相机姿态估计，但缺乏代数推导和更广泛类别的分析，且收敛性和标定敏感性未充分研究。

Method: 给出DeMenthon-Davis方法的代数推导，将其归入零阶（弱透视）和一阶（准透视）近似透视模型的更广方法类；详细描述针对非共面和共面物点的迭代准透视姿态计算；分析收敛性，引入旋转矩阵正交约束的简单处理方式，分析相机标定误差敏感性并确定最优实验设置，与非线性优化方法比较。

Result: 迭代准透视方法比迭代弱透视方法具有更好的收敛性质；给出了考虑旋转正交约束的简单方法；分析了标定误差敏感性并定义了最优实验设置；与非线性优化方法进行了结果比较。

Conclusion: 迭代准透视姿态计算方法在收敛性上优于迭代弱透视方法，属于更广泛的透视模型近似方法类，并可通过简单方式处理旋转正交约束，同时给出了对标定误差敏感的实验设计指导。

Abstract: Recently, DeMenthon and Davis [4,5] proposed a method for determining the pose of a 3-D object with respect to a camera from 3-D to 2-D point correspondences. The method consists of iteratively improving the pose computed with a weak perspective camera model to converge, at the limit, to a pose estimation computed with a perspective camera model. In this paper we give an algebraic derivation of DeMenthon and Davis' method and we show that it belongs to a larger class of methods where the perspective camera model is approximated either at zero order (weak perspective) or first order (paraperspective). We describe in detail an iterative paraperspective pose computation method for both non coplanar and coplanar object points. We analyse the convergence of these methods and we conclude that the iterative paraperspective method (proposed in this paper) has better convergence properties than the iterative weak perspective method. We introduce a simple way of taking into account the orthogonality constraint associated with the rotation matrix. We analyse the sensitivity to camera calibration errors and we define the optimal experimental setup with respect to imprecise camera calibration. We compare the results obtained with this method and with a non-linear optimization method.

</details>


### [114] [CausalDrive: Causal Decomposition for Data-Efficient End-to-End Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24080)
*Xiaobao Wei,Jingxian Wu,Lirong Yang,Qing Han,Yuan Gao,Ziwei Yang,Shuai Yang,Weiwei Liu,Chang Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CausalDrive将专家轨迹分解为名义意图与驾驶风格两部分，分别用确定性规划器和扩散策略建模，在NAVSIM上以更少数据达到或超过SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接模仿专家轨迹会把“去哪”的名义意图和“怎么开”的多模态驾驶风格纠缠在一起，不同组合产生相似轨迹，掩盖各自因果贡献，导致策略学习效率低、难以充分利用专家演示。

Method: 构建由导航指令、地图拓扑和自车状态生成的反事实名义轨迹以消除风格影响，为名义意图规划器提供独立于专家轨迹的监督；再以预测的名义意图和周围智能体为条件，用基于扩散的风格执行策略将驾驶风格建模为名义轨迹与专家轨迹残差的多模态分布，形成因果结构化的监督。

Result: 在NAVSIM v1上取得91.2 PDMS，在NAVSIM v2上取得89.5 EPDMS，与SOTA持平或更优；在有限专家数据下仍表现强劲，并在挑战性交互场景中优势明显。

Conclusion: 将名义意图与驾驶风格进行因果分解可减少对共享名义行为的冗余学习，把专家监督集中于有信息量的执行变化上，从而提升数据利用效率与驾驶策略性能。

Abstract: Efficient use of expert demonstrations is essential for end-to-end autonomous driving. However, directly imitating expert trajectories couples nominal intention, which specifies where the vehicle should go, with multimodal driving styles, which determine how that intention is executed. Different combinations of these factors can yield similar expert trajectories, obscuring their respective causal contributions and forcing the policy to learn both from a single entangled target. This ambiguity limits how effectively the policy can exploit expert demonstrations. We propose CausalDrive, a causal decomposition framework for data-efficient driving policy learning that separates deterministic nominal intention prediction from probabilistic driving style modeling. Specifically, we construct counterfactual nominal trajectories from navigation command, map topology, and ego state by removing style dependent effects, providing a Nominal Intention Planner with supervision independent of expert trajectories. Conditioned on the predicted nominal intention and surrounding agents, a diffusion-based Style Execution Policy models diverse driving styles as a multimodal distribution over residuals between nominal and expert trajectories. This causally structured supervision reduces redundant learning of shared nominal behavior in routine scenarios, directing expert supervision toward informative execution variations and improving data utilization. CausalDrive achieves 91.2 PDMS on NAVSIM v1 and 89.5 EPDMS on NAVSIM v2, matching or surpassing state-of-the-art performance. Further experiments demonstrate strong performance with limited expert data and clear advantages in challenging interactive scenarios.

</details>


### [115] [Freeforward4d: Annotation-free feed-forward 4d reconstruction of driving scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24007)
*Luosong Guo,Xiang Yang,Chaoyang Li,Ao Gao,Jiangming Shi,Yanyun Qu,Zhizhong Zhang,Jingyu Gong,Yuan Xie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FreeForward4D 通过将预训练视觉模型的多视角预测窗口与单目度量深度对齐并配准到统一坐标系，实现了仅用 RGB 视频的前馈 4D 重建，并在 Waymo 上超越依赖数据集标注的基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有前馈 4D 重建方法依赖数据集提供的相机、LiDAR 深度或 3D 框标注，难以适用于无标注的驾驶视频；预训练视觉模型虽可从 RGB 预测相机、深度和物体轨迹，但多视角预测窗口尺度与帧独立、单目深度仅逐图度量，几何上互不一致。

Method: 提出 FreeForward4D：将重叠预测窗口与单目度量深度标定，并跨时间配准到统一度量坐标系；在此几何上跨相机进行 3D 关联 2D 轨迹，并采用共享物体速度与局部高斯修正的分层运动建模，使训练与推理仅需 RGB 视频。

Result: 在 Waymo 上达到 27.66 dB PSNR 和 0.836 SSIM，无需尺度对齐即可恢复度量尺度相机与深度；全图 PSNR 超越依赖标注的 STORM、SLARM-F、UFO、DGGT 和 StreetForward，并在 8 秒窗口上取得最佳 RGB 保真度；仅 RGB 微调可适配 nuScenes 和 Argoverse 2。

Conclusion: 统一度量坐标系下的预训练视觉先验可替代数据集标注用于前馈 4D 重建，实现仅 RGB 的高质量驾驶场景重建，并具备跨数据集适配能力。

Abstract: Feed-forward 4D reconstruction enables efficient creation of renderable driving scenes for autonomous driving simulation. However, most existing methods rely heavily on dataset-provided cameras, LiDAR depth, or 3D box annotations, limiting their applicability to unannotated driving videos. Pretrained visual models can predict cameras, depth, and object tracks from RGB alone, but their geometry is mutually inconsistent: multi-view predictions come in short windows with independent scales and frames, while monocular depth is metric only per image. Our key insight is that, once reconciled into a single metric frame, these priors can replace dataset annotations for feed-forward 4D reconstruction. We present \textbf{FreeForward4D}, which calibrates overlapping prediction windows against monocular metric depth and registers them into one frame across time. On this geometry, 2D tracks are associated across cameras in 3D, and objects move through hierarchical motion that combines a shared object velocity with local Gaussian corrections, so that both training and inference use only RGB videos. On Waymo, FreeForward4D achieves 27.66 dB PSNR and 0.836 SSIM while recovering metric-scale cameras and depth without scale alignment. It surpasses STORM, SLARM-F, UFO, DGGT, and StreetForward, which rely on dataset annotations, in full-image PSNR and attains the best RGB fidelity on 8-second windows. RGB-only fine-tuning further adapts the framework to nuScenes and Argoverse 2. Code will be released.

</details>


### [116] [HOForce: Motion and Force Reconstruction of Hand-Object Interaction Using a Single-View RGB-D Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23871)
*Jiahui Wang,Wanqing Du,Yuanbin Zou,Zhefeng Xu,Feng Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HOForce从单视角RGB-D视频和已知物体属性中追踪手-物体交互并恢复交互力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单相机重构图-物交互具有大规模采集的实用性，但严重遮挡使力恢复和运动跟踪困难。

Method: 联合建模双手指尖力及双物体接触力；将恢复的力作为遮挡区域姿态约束以改进运动跟踪。

Result: 在DexYCB和TACO上运动恢复更准确，手-物穿透更少；触觉传感器数据集上法向力MAE较最强基线降低24.5%；恢复的力在MuJoCo中复现物体运动并可迁移到其他引擎。

Conclusion: 恢复的交互力既能提升遮挡下的运动跟踪，也能支持物理仿真中的物体运动复现与跨引擎迁移。

Abstract: Reconstructing hand-object interactions is an important task, and achieving it with a single camera makes large-scale capture practical but challenging due to heavy occlusions. We introduce HOForce to track hands and objects, and further recover interaction forces from single-view RGB-D video and known object properties. For two-hand manipulation, we jointly model fingertip forces of both hands and the contact forces between objects when two objects are presented. The reconstructed forces also improve motion tracking by giving pose constraints in the occluded regions. HOForce recovers hand and object motion more accurately on DexYCB and TACO and produces less hand-object penetration than interaction baselines on DexYCB. On our dataset of fingertip forces recorded with tactile sensors, HOForce reduces normal-force MAE by 24.5% relative to the strongest baseline. The recovered forces reproduce object motion in MuJoCo and transfer to other engines after adaptation.

</details>


### [117] [SWIFT: Slow World, Instant Freshness, and Timely Action for Asynchronous Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23813)
*Jiazhe Wang,Tianyi Yang,Ruihang Li,Fan Lu,Xianming Zeng,Jianyun Xu,Xingliang Liu,Lei Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SWIFT是一个面向自动驾驶的异步世界-动作系统，将世界模型更新移出观察-动作关键路径，在四个基准上取得最佳精度-延迟权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 驾驶场景在模型计算期间持续变化，使观测新鲜度和动作交付时间成为世界-动作规划的核心；需在保留丰富生成式世界先验的同时不阻塞实时控制。

Method: 采用双GPU异步架构：后台GPU上慢速Video DiT将环视历史流式处理为只读预测世界上下文，并以覆盖更新间隔和后台计算的预测跨度训练；前台GPU上轻量新鲜度载体每步将当前环视图像编码为门控key/value库，快速Action DiT结合实时视觉反馈与最新完成的世界上下文而无需等待。

Result: 开环NAVMIS上达94.2 PDMS、每决策198.4 ms（含轨迹评分器）；闭环Bench2Drive上80.30 Driving Score、前台延迟109.9 ms；迁移至Waymo E2E隐藏测试得8.0050 Rater Feedback Score，nuScenes上平均终点误差0.54 m、平均碰撞率0.03%。

Conclusion: SWIFT在四个基准上均优于对比的世界-动作方法，建立了有利的精度-延迟权衡。

Abstract: Driving scenes keep changing during model computation, making both observation freshness and action-delivery time central to world-action planning. To retain rich generative world priors in autonomous driving without stalling real-time control, we present SWIFT (Slow World, Instant Freshness, and Timely action), a deployment-oriented asynchronous world-action system that moves world updates off the observation-to-action critical path. On a background GPU, a slow Video DiT streams surround-view history into read-only predictive world context, trained with a predictive span that covers both the update interval and background world computation. On a foreground GPU, a lightweight freshness carrier encodes current surround-view images into a gated key/value bank at every step, letting a fast Action DiT combine live visual feedback with the latest completed world context without waiting. On open-loop NAVSIM, SWIFT reaches 94.2 PDMS at 198.4 ms per decision with a trajectory scorer; on closed-loop Bench2Drive, it achieves an 80.30 Driving Score at 109.9 ms foreground latency. SWIFT further transfers to the Waymo E2E hidden test (8.0050 Rater Feedback Score) and nuScenes (0.54 m mean endpoint error, 0.03% mean collision rate). Across all four benchmarks, SWIFT achieves the best results among compared world-action methods, establishing a favorable accuracy-latency trade-off.

</details>


### [118] [LightSEAD: Lightweight SE(3)-Aware Keypoint Detector through Selective Knowledge Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23661)
*Mayowa Adebambo,Alexander D. Rast,Andrew Bradley*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出LightSEAD轻量级SE(3)感知关键点检测器，仅6层卷积、84,289个参数，通过结构化监督实现竞争性重复性，并在边缘设备上达到高帧率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习性能提升常伴随架构复杂度和计算需求增加，本文探索“以更少资源实现更多”的替代方向，即通过结构化监督而非复杂架构来提升性能。

Method: LightSEAD使用校准的Harris伪标签初始化，并引入视角感知与外观感知监督以及选择性知识蒸馏；模型仅含6个卷积层和84,289个可学习参数；进一步针对混合精度推理优化出LightSEAD Coarse版本。

Result: 在HPatches、KITTI 06和4Seasons上取得竞争性重复性，并在MegaDepth-1500上验证了相对位姿估计的下游适用性；LightSEAD Coarse在NVIDIA RTX 4090上约909 FPS，在Jetson Orin Nano Super上139 FPS。

Conclusion: 结构化监督可在极轻量架构下实现有竞争力的关键点检测性能，并支持高效混合精度推理，适用于资源受限平台。

Abstract: The pursuit of improved performance in deep learning has often been accompanied by increasing architectural complexity and computational demands. We explore an alternative direction: achieving more with less through structured supervision. We introduce LightSEAD, a lightweight SE(3)-aware keypoint detector initialised with calibrated Harris pseudo-labels and enriched through viewpoint-aware and appearance-aware supervision and selective knowledge distillation. LightSEAD comprises only six convolutional layers and 84,289 learnable parameters, achieving competitive repeatability across HPatches, KITTI 06 and 4Seasons while demonstrating its downstream applicability to relative pose estimation on MegaDepth-1500. Further optimised for mixed-precision inference, LightSEAD Coarse achieves approximately 909 FPS on an NVIDIA RTX 4090 and 139 FPS on a Jetson Orin Nano Super.

</details>


### [119] [DOCK: Dual-Stream-Inspired Self-Supervised Object-Centric Representations Grounded in Robot Kinematics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23578)
*Pengju Liu,Caiwen Ding*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 冻结视觉编码器的特征距离几乎无法反映物体到目标的距离，作者提出DOCK学习物体表示以改善潜在空间规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜在空间规划器假设预测图像与目标图像嵌入距离能反映场景到目标的远近，但该假设缺乏验证，且冻结特征可能受背景和机器人干扰。

Method: 用模拟器执行的真实未来帧检验六个冻结DINO和V-JEPA编码器的特征距离与物体到目标距离的相关性；受腹侧（what）和背侧（where/how）视觉通路启发，从21个模拟厨房场景的9,951条持物搬运轨迹中挖掘404K位移对，训练三个组件：从patch特征池化的物体token（what）、使读出坐标差等于实测位移的空间标尺（where）、物体token上的动力学预测器（how），并将其用作规划代价。

Result: 冻结特征距离与物体到目标距离的秩相关最高仅0.11，而物体掩码质心达0.87；DOCK在21个任务中15个将物体放置到位，与读取真实物体位置的oracle相当，掩码几何为10，冻结特征距离为9；在可见未见机器人手臂时仍解决最多任务，其排序无需适应即可迁移到未见布局、物体实例和类别。

Conclusion: 冻结视觉特征距离不是可靠的规划代价，学习与物理运动一致的物体表示（DOCK）能显著提升潜在空间规划性能并具备泛化能力。

Abstract: Latent-space planners select actions by comparing the embedding of a predicted future image with that of a goal image. This assumes that distance in the embedding reflects how far the scene is from the goal. We profile this assumption with simulator-executed futures, which removes prediction error. For six frozen DINO and V-JEPA encoders, feature distance barely tracks the object's distance to its goal: the rank correlation is at most 0.11, against 0.87 for object-mask centroids. It is dominated by background and robot patches, and frozen-feature readouts capture the arm far better than the object. Inspired by the ventral (what) and dorsal (where/how) visual streams, we propose DOCK, which learns an object representation whose changes agree with measured physical motion. A grasped object moves with the gripper, so forward kinematics on the joint angles gives a proxy for the object's displacement without annotation. From 9,951 held-object transport trajectories in 21 simulated kitchen scenes, we mine 404K displacement pairs and train three components: an object token pooled from patch features (what), a spatial ruler that makes differences of readout coordinates equal to measured displacements (where), and a dynamics predictor over object tokens (how). Used as a planning cost, the learned representation places the object in 15 of 21 tasks, as many as an oracle that reads the true object position, while mask geometry reaches 10 and frozen feature distance 9. It still solves the most tasks when the unseen robot arm is visible, and its ranking transfers to held-out layouts with unseen object instances and categories without any adaptation.

</details>


### [120] [GRID-UAV: Geometry and Ray-depth Integrated Decision-making for Unmanned Aerial Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23491)
*Anas Anwarul Haq Khan,Amrin Kareem,Muhammad Umer Sheikh,Biplab Banerjee,Muhammad Haris Khan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GRID结合显式深度三维提升与隐式几何对齐，在无人机视觉-语言-动作模型中显著提升长距离飞行任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型使用2D编码器缺乏3D结构，直接输入单目深度在远距离无人机场景中不可靠且无显著增益，需研究如何有效向策略提供3D信息。

Method: 提出GRID：将单目深度提升为无人机机体系下两个相机的射线校正3D位置（显式3D提升），并在训练时对齐视觉特征与冻结的多视角几何模型（隐式几何对齐），推理无额外成本。

Result: 显式提升加入隐式对齐使到达目标率从61.5%提升至69.5%（p<0.001），隐式对齐加入显式提升使到达后完成任务率从57.7%提升至61.4%（p<0.05）；在TravelUAV 3005测试回合中每个划分均最优，相对SOTA提升9.5%（已见）、17.1%（未见物体）和25.4%（未见地图）。

Conclusion: 两种3D grounding形式失败模式互补，GRID结合二者可显著提升无人机长距离导航任务的成功率与泛化能力。

Abstract: Vision-language-action models (VLAs) encode images with 2D encoders that capture little 3D structure. A common approach is to feed the policy estimated depth, but a UAV chooses its own altitude, must judge distances tens of meters away, where monocular depth is less reliable, and must relate its forward and downward views. On long aerial routes, adding depth features directly brings no significant gain (42.5% vs. 42.6% success), so we study how 3D information should be given to the policy. Analyzing flight failures, we find that two forms of 3D grounding fail in opposite ways: explicit 3D lifting of depth collides less on the way but more often fails to finish at the target, while implicit alignment with a 3D geometry model finishes more often but collides more. We propose GRID, which combines both: (1) it lifts monocular depth into ray-corrected 3D positions in the UAV's frame for both cameras, and (2) during training only, it aligns its visual features with a frozen multi-view geometry model, at no inference cost. GRID adding explicit lifting to implicit alignment significantly increases how often the policy reaches the target ($61.5\% \rightarrow 69.5\%$, $p<0.001$), and adding implicit alignment to explicit lifting significantly increases how often it completes the task once there ($57.7\% \rightarrow 61.4\%$, $p<0.05$). On TravelUAV (3,005 test episodes), GRID achieves the best success rate on every split, with relative gains over the state of the art of 9.5% (seen), 17.1% (unseen objects) and 25.4% (unseen map).

</details>


### [121] [Autoregressive View Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23417)
*Jensen,Zhou,Samuel Rota Bulo,Denis Rozumny,Duncan Zauss,Peter Hedman,Nikhil Keetha,Philip Torr,Christian Rupprecht,Andrea Vedaldi,Peter Kontschieder*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出自回归视图模型AVM，以视图流方式生成场景而无需显式3D表示，通过统一上下文接口实现多模态条件下的相机路径生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统三维重建需构建显式3D表示，限制了生成灵活性与泛化能力，作者希望以视图流直接生成场景，统一处理图像/视频输入与多样化输出。

Method: 采用统一in-context接口，基于图像或视频条件生成沿相机路径的短视频；从输入与生成历史中检索相关视图构成有界主动上下文；以N-in-1-out形式用flow matching微调预训练双向视频扩散Transformer，再在自身rollout上调节因果模型以提升真实感、减少去噪步数并抑制长时漂移；训练规模约800万场景与视频片段。

Result: 在相机控制、文本控制、视觉质量、动态、稀疏与4D重建及长时一致性等基准上评估生成能力与场景持续性，结果显示AVM通过单一in-context接口适配多种输入输出设置。

Conclusion: AVM表明无需显式3D表示即可通过自回归视图流实现灵活、一致的场景生成，统一接口具备良好的多任务适应性与长时稳定性。

Abstract: We present the Autoregressive View Model (AVM), which generates a scene as a stream of views without constructing an explicit 3D representation. Through a unified in-context interface, AVM conditions on images or videos to generate short clips along a requested camera path. Visual context grounds reconstruction and interpolation, while a learned prior enables extrapolation into unseen regions. AVM retrieves relevant views from inputs and generated history to form a bounded active context. We fine-tune a pretrained bidirectional video diffusion transformer with flow matching in an N-in-1-out formulation. We then tune a causal model on its own rollouts to improve photorealism, reduce denoising steps, and limit long-horizon drift. We scale training to approximately 8M scenes and video clips. We evaluate generation capacity and scene persistence across benchmarks for camera control, text control, visual quality, dynamics, sparse and 4D reconstruction, and long-horizon consistency. The results demonstrate that AVM adapts to diverse input and output settings through one in-context interface.

</details>


### [122] [Does the Action Matter? Probing Action-Conditioned Semantic Forecasting as a World Model for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24840)
*Binay Kumar Singh,Niels Da Vitoria Lobo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文研究动作条件语义预测中的动作依赖性问题，通过测试时动作干预评估模型预测是否真正依赖条件动作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有驾驶世界模型评估主要关注预测保真度和规划性能，很少验证预测是否依赖条件动作，因此需要专门研究动作依赖性。

Method: 比较带特征级和输入级动作条件的预测器与无动作输入的相同模型，用标准分割指标对测试时动作干预打分，在合成环境、Cityscapes和BDD100K上验证。

Result: 执行动作在Cityscapes上最多提升中期mIoU 2.5点、BDD100K上0.6点，而错误动作分别降低最多23.2和7.0点；真实数据上特征级条件比输入级条件对动作依赖高1.6倍，合成环境中两者相同。

Conclusion: 准确率提升表明驾驶世界模型使用了动作，但不能说明其预测对动作的依赖强度。

Abstract: World models are increasingly used in autonomous driving to predict how a scene evolves under the ego vehicle's actions, supporting counterfactual reasoning and model-based planning. However, existing evaluations focus on prediction fidelity and planning performance, and rarely verify that the predictions depend on the conditioning action. In this paper, we study action dependence in action-conditioned semantic forecasting, which predicts future semantic segmentation from past segmentations and the ego action. We compare a forecaster with feature-wise and input-level action conditioning against an identical model without action input, and measure action dependence with test-time action interventions scored by standard segmentation metrics. The evaluation is validated in a synthetic environment with known action-dependent dynamics and applied to Cityscapes and BDD100K, both within each dataset and across the two vehicle platforms. Experiments show that the executed action improves mid-term mIoU by at most 2.5 points on Cityscapes and 0.6 points on BDD100K, whereas a wrong action reduces it by up to 23.2 and 7.0 points, below the model without action input. On real data, feature-wise conditioning depends 1.6 times more on the action than input-level conditioning at similar accuracy, whereas both depend on it equally in the synthetic environment, where the history does not determine the action. These results show that an accuracy gain indicates that a driving world model uses its action, but not how strongly its forecasts depend on it. Code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [123] [OCTR2A: Real-Time RGB-to-BEV Trajectory Projection and Forecasting at Urban Intersections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24352)
*Emanuele Iacobelli,Simone Teglia,Francesco Pro,Claudia Melis Tonti,Irene Amerini*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出OCTR2A架构，仅用一对对角RGB相机从感知到预测重建并预测BEV轨迹，并引入R3ST++合成基准；在真实数据上达到厘米级精度且实时运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有城市交叉口轨迹预测研究通常假设可获得可靠的鸟瞰(BEV)运动历史，这在实际中依赖LiDAR、航拍、高精地图或标定基础设施等高成本资源。作者旨在解决这一上游问题：仅利用现有城市基础设施上安装的一对对角RGB相机进行BEV轨迹重建与预测，降低对昂贵传感器的依赖。

Method: 提出模块化流水线OCTR2A，集成单视角检测与类别细化、几何跨相机匹配、基于Transformer的BEV投影以及自车中心GRU预测，采用级联训练并以软多尺度空间重叠损失为主要监督。由于缺少公开基准，构建了基于Blender的合成基准R3ST++（基于SIND轨迹生成），提供同步对角视角、跨视角对应关系和BEV真值，并可作为部署支持模拟器用于站点特定微调。

Result: 在12帧观测、12帧预测（10Hz）设置下，OCTR2A在单块消费级GPU上实时运行，投影-预测ADE/FDE为0.144/0.140至0.154/0.241米；在真实BEV历史输入下，预测模块在SIND上较最强基线ADE/FDE分别提升66.7%/41.7%；在TUMTraf-V2X上的迁移实验表明，R3ST++预训练可支持向不同传感配置的真实路侧域自适应。

Conclusion: 仅凭一对对角RGB相机即可实现实时、厘米级的BEV轨迹重建与预测，合成基准R3ST++能有效支撑预训练与真实路侧部署的域适应，为低成本感知-预测一体化提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Trajectory forecasting at urban intersections is often studied assuming that reliable bird's-eye-view (BEV) motion histories are available, which in practice requires costly resources such as LiDAR, aerial views, HD maps, or calibrated infrastructure. We address this upstream perception-to-forecasting problem by reconstructing and predicting agents' BEV trajectories only from paired opposite-corner RGB cameras mounted on existing urban infrastructure at variable heights at inference time. We introduce the Opposite-Corner Trajectory Reconstruction and Anticipation Architecture (OCTR2A), a modular pipeline integrating per-view detection with class refinement, geometric cross-camera matching, transformer-based BEV projection, and egocentric GRU forecasting, trained in cascade and mainly supervised with a soft multi-scale spatial-overlap loss. Since no public benchmark provides this sensing configuration, we introduce R3ST++, a Blender-based synthetic benchmark built from SIND trajectories that provides synchronized opposite-corner views, cross-view correspondences, and BEV ground truth, while also serving as a deployment-support simulator for site-specific fine-tuning. Experiments on these datasets show that, with 12 observed and 12 predicted frames at 10\,Hz, OCTR2A achieves projection~-~forecasting ADE/FDE of 0.144/0.140~-~0.154/0.241~meters in real time on a single consumer GPU. Evaluated on ground-truth BEV histories, the forecasting module improves over the strongest state-of-the-art methods on SIND by 66.7\%/41.7\% ADE/FDE. Transfer experiments on TUMTraf-V2X show that R3ST++ pretraining supports adaptation to real-world roadside domains with different sensing configurations.

</details>


### [124] [AeroRSI: Recursive Self-Improvement for Aerial Vision-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24077)
*Shiwei Wu,Yuwen Pan,Zhi Li,Yaowen Li,Yan Zhou,Xuemiao Xu,Yu Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AeroRSI是一个递归自我改进框架，让无人机导航策略从自身失败中迭代学习修复动作，三轮后2B参数策略成功率从46.4%升至67.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型在闭环飞行中常失败，修复失败需要策略自身到达状态下的正确动作，但人类无法大规模标注、规则规划器不可靠。

Method: 提出AeroRSI递归自我改进框架：每轮锚定当前策略的失败状态，通过从修复动作继续飞行来验证修复可靠性，并将验证后的修复蒸馏进策略，同时保持其他行为不变。

Result: 在TravelUAV上三轮迭代将监督式2B参数策略成功率从46.4%提升至67.5%，碰撞减半，较此前最优方法在相同初始化下提升10.6个百分点，且无需额外人工标注或推理开销。

Conclusion: 从失败后继续飞行来评判修复动作可提供可靠监督，使采样动作和规则提议成为有效训练信号，实现无额外标注的递归自我改进。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models enable unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) to follow natural-language instructions, yet still fail frequently in closed-loop flight. Correcting these failures requires repairs, i.e., actions that avoid them at the states the policy itself reaches, which human pilots cannot label at scale and rule-based planners cannot supply reliably. In this paper, we present AeroRSI, a recursive self-improvement framework in which an aerial navigation policy iteratively learns from its own failures. Our key insight is that a failing policy should judge its own repairs by flying on from them. Judged in this way, its sampled actions and even rule-based proposals that hurt it when imitated directly become reliable supervision. Moreover, an episode terminates at its first failure, concealing subsequent failures until the earlier ones are repaired. AeroRSI thus renews its supervision in every round, anchoring the failures of the current policy, verifying repairs by its continuation, and distilling them while keeping its behavior elsewhere. On TravelUAV, three rounds raise the success rate of a supervised 2B-parameter policy from 46.4% to 67.5% and reduce collisions by half, surpassing the previous state of the art by 10.6 points from the same initialization without extra human labels or inference cost. Code and models are available at this https URL .

</details>


### [125] [TOMAS: Topology-Aware Operation Modeling and Adaptive Staging for Egocentric Hand Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23970)
*Guanzhong Sun,Junyi Ma,Yuxuan Wu,Erhang Zhang,Chenyang Xu,Yanzi Miao,Hesheng Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出TOMAS框架，通过拓扑感知的手物推理与自适应阶段建模，提升第一人称视频中手部运动预测的准确性与多目标适应能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同一物体在不同操作意图下会产生显著不同的手部轨迹，现有方法未充分挖掘关节手运动与物体语义间的内在关系，难以捕捉操作依赖的运动模式。

Method: 构建拓扑感知的手物推理机制，将手部骨骼结构与关节动态同物体语义及空间关系关联，联合推断交互目标与潜在操作；引入自适应阶段机制，将未来手部运动组织为带可学习时间边界的有序潜在阶段，并以阶段特定几何子目标引导轨迹细化，监督信号按数据集标注自适应。

Result: 在多个公开基准上取得多维度、多目标手部运动预测的最优性能。

Conclusion: 显式建模操作语义与手部拓扑关系并结合阶段化子目标引导，可有效提升第一人称手部轨迹预测的准确性与泛化性。

Abstract: Forecasting future hand movements from egocentric videos plays an important role in understanding human intentions and hand-object interactions. However, different manipulation intentions can lead to substantially different hand trajectories even when interacting with the same object. Existing methods have not sufficiently explored the intrinsic relationships between articulated hand movements and object semantics to capture such operation-dependent motion patterns. Therefore, we propose TOMAS, a topology-aware operation modeling and adaptive staging framework for egocentric hand forecasting. Specifically, we develop a topology-aware hand-object reasoning mechanism that associates hand skeletal structures and articulation dynamics with object semantics and spatial relationships to jointly infer potential interaction targets and latent manipulation operations. The resulting object-operation representations provide interaction conditions for future hand trajectory prediction, with supervision adapted to the annotations available in each dataset. Furthermore, we introduce an adaptive staging mechanism that organizes future hand movements into ordered latent stages with learnable temporal boundaries. Stage-specific geometric subgoals are then incorporated to guide trajectory refinement according to the underlying manipulation patterns. Experimental results on multiple publicly available benchmarks demonstrate that TOMAS achieves state-of-the-art performance in multi-dimensional and multi-target hand motion forecasting.

</details>
